### [1] [Light EFT Strings and Asymptotic Accelerated Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24062)
*Veronica Collazuol,Alessandra Grieco,Irene Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 N=1 理论在微扰无穷远极限附近粒子塔、EFT 弦与 EFT 膜之间的相互作用，证明 EFT 膜的标度向量落在 EFT 弦生成的格中，并在 F 理论和 IIA 卡拉比-丘紧致化中确定其相对标度，据此排除 F 理论中两条加速膨胀轨迹，并探讨量子阻碍对无穷远极限的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索微扰无穷远极限下粒子塔、EFT 弦与 EFT 膜三类对象之间的层级与标度关系，进而评估通量紧致化中实现渐近加速膨胀（类德西特）的可能性，并填补 arXiv:2209.11821 留下的漏洞。

Method: 采用自下而上的 swampland（沼泽地）分析，结合 F 理论与 IIA 卡拉比-丘紧致化的具体构造，研究粒子塔、EFT 弦、EFT 膜的标度向量之间的格包含关系与相对标度；并利用量子引力对 N=1 无穷远极限的量子阻碍约束，分析 Kähler 势渐近形式的改变。

Result: 证明 EFT 膜的标度向量位于 EFT 弦标度向量生成的格中；在 F 理论和 IIA 卡拉比-丘紧致化中确定了二者的具体相对标度；自下而上分析未得到一般性阻碍，但排除了 F 理论中两条候选加速轨迹，因为 EFT 弦变得比哈勃尺度更轻，使四维 EFT 失效；量子阻碍结果可能通过改变 Kähler 势渐近形式提供具体解决方案。

Conclusion: EFT 膜与 EFT 弦、粒子塔之间存在严格的格包含与标度关系；F 理论中两条加速膨胀轨迹被 EFT 弦轻于哈勃尺度所排除，从而封堵了 arXiv:2209.11821 的漏洞；量子阻碍对 N=1 无穷远极限的修正为进一步研究提供了具体路径。

Abstract: We study the interplay among towers of particles, EFT strings, and EFT membranes near perturbative infinite-distance limits of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories. We show that the scaling vectors of EFT membranes lie in the lattice generated by those of the EFT strings, as previously found for particle towers, and determine their specific relative scaling in F-theory and Type IIA Calabi-Yau compactifications. We then leverage these objects to assess the viability of asymptotic accelerated expansion in flux compactifications. While our bottom-up analysis does not yield a general obstruction, we rule out two candidate accelerating trajectories in F-theory because an EFT string becomes lighter than the Hubble scale, invalidating the four-dimensional EFT. This closes the loophole found in arXiv:2209.11821. We also study how recent results on quantum obstructions to $\mathcal{N}=1$ infinite-distance limits may provide a concrete resolution by changing the asymptotic form of the Kähler potential.

</details>


### [2] [Angular Momentum Loss at Fourth Post-Minkowskian Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24072)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Carlo Heissenberg,Gustav Mogull,Rodolfo Russo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ 阶计算了两个无自旋点状物体的引力散射总角动量损失，结合振幅方法与世界线方法，区分辐射角动量与静态贡献，并由此推导出 $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ 阶保守偏转角及一阶自力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算引力散射中的角动量损失，对于理解双体系统在强场下的辐射反作用以及连接散射观测量与引力波波形具有重要意义。此前方法难以同时清晰分离辐射部分与静态（记忆）贡献，作者希望结合振幅技巧与世界线方法，在 $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ 实现系统性的推导。

Method: 作者结合散射振幅和世界线两种方法：从引力波形的两份副本出发，利用世界线自然产生的大质量线切割构造被积函数；再将两份副本粘贴（glue），采用振幅精神下的反向幺正性（reverse unitarity）计算相空间积分。由此分别得到辐射角动量和完全由初始剪切与线性记忆决定的静态贡献。

Result: 得到角动量损失的相对论表达式，以及质量偶极损失的有限（红外有限）分量；这些表达式在低速极限下与文献结果一致。进一步利用该结果推导出 $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ 阶的一阶自力保守偏转角。

Conclusion: 通过振幅与世界线方法的结合，成功在 $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ 系统分离并计算了角动量损失的辐射与静态贡献，验证了低速极限下的自洽性，并将结果推广到 $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ 保守偏转角与一阶自力，展示了该混合方法在引力散射高阶计算中的有效性。

Abstract: We calculate the total loss of angular momentum for a gravitational scattering of two non-spinning point-like objects at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$. Our methodology combines use of both amplitudes and worldline-based methods. We derive the integrand from two copies of the gravitational waveform with cuts on massive lines arising naturally from the worldline. We then glue these two copies and evaluate the resulting phase-space integrals using reverse unitarity, in the amplitudes spirit. We obtain separately the radiated angular momentum and the static contribution that, at this order, is entirely determined by the initial shear and the linear memory. Combining these two terms, we derive relativistic expressions for the angular momentum loss and for the infrared-finite component of the mass dipole loss, which in the low-velocity limit match results available in the literature. We also use this result to derive the conservative deflection angle at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ and first self-force order.

</details>


### [3] [On the Positivity of de Sitter Group Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24073)
*Donald Marolf,Xuyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在全局 de Sitter 时空（d>2 维）的微扰量子引力中，对于满足权重条件 Re Σ Δᵢ > d−1 的 N 粒子态，群平均内积的正定性成立，并以 dS₂ 作为更一般情形的玩具模型；同时发现某些较小权重态会得到负范数，并讨论了其对微扰 de Sitter 量子引力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰量子引力在全局 de Sitter 背景上存在约束，这些约束在高阶微扰中才出现，并要求线性化理论中由连续 de Sitter 等距定义的电荷全部为零。标准 Fock 空间中唯一满足这些约束的可归一化态是真空，Higuchi 提出用群平均内积来弥补缺乏允许态的问题；该内积满足重要的唯一性条件，但其正定性此前一直是未决问题。本文旨在解决这一正定性问题。

Method: 作者采用 Higuchi 提出的群平均（group-averaging）内积构造，针对标准全局 dS_d 模式下的任意自旋 N 粒子态进行分析。证明在 d>2 维、且粒子权重满足 Re Σ_{i=1}^N Δᵢ > d−1（该条件是群平均对任意有限线性组合绝对收敛的判据）时内积正定。文中还将 dS₂ 中的整数/半整数自旋（玻色/费米）粒子作为更一般情形的玩具模型处理；并进一步识别出群平均在较小权重下仍绝对收敛的若干态类，其中部分态呈现负的群平均范数。

Result: 对于 d>2 维，证明了在所有满足 Re Σ Δᵢ > d−1 的 N 粒子态上群平均内积的正定性；对 dS₂ 的玻色与费米粒子情形也给出相应结果。同时发现某些较小权重态（群平均仍绝对收敛）会得到负群平均范数。

Conclusion: 当粒子权重总和满足 Re Σ Δᵢ > d−1 时，群平均内积正定成立，为 de Sitter 微扰量子引力提供了具有正定内积的物理态空间。然而，较小权重下出现的负范数态提示人们需谨慎诠释这些态，并对微扰 de Sitter 量子引力的内积构造与物理解释提出了新的问题与含义。

Abstract: Perturbative quantum gravity about a global de Sitter (dS$_d$) background involves constraints that restrict the state of the linearized theory but which appear only at higher orders in perturbation theory. In particular, for familiar gravitational theories with $d>2$ these constraints require all charges defined by continuous de Sitter isometries to vanish in the linearized state. While standard Fock spaces have the property that the only normalizable state satisfying these constraints is the vacuum, Higuchi suggested that this lack of allowed states should be remedied by instead using the so-called group-averaging inner product. This inner product satisfies important uniqueness conditions, but its positivity has to date remained an open question. For $d > 2$, we establish this positivity here for all such states with $N$ particles (including particles of arbitrary spin) in standard global dS${}_d$ modes where the particle-weights $Δ_i$ satisfy the condition ${\rm Re} \, \sum_{i=1}^N Δ_i > d-1$, which is the criterion for group averaging to converge absolutely for arbitrary finite linear combinations of such states. We also treat the case of bosonic and fermionic (integer and half-integer spin) particles in dS$_2$ as a toy model for the more general case.
  However, we can identify certain classes of states for which group averaging continues to converge absolutely for smaller weights. Interestingly, in some cases these smaller-weight states turn out to have {\it negative} group-averaging norms. We thus also discuss the implications of this observation for perturbative de Sitter quantum gravity.

</details>


### [4] [Wrapping M5-branes on a non-spin manifold: Spin$^h$ structures and AdS$_7$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24084)
*Okan Günel,Cameron Gibson,C. N. Pope*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在七维最大规范超引力中构造了一个 AdS₂×M₅ 的超对称解，其中 M₅ 是不具备自旋结构或 spin^c 结构的五维吴流形 SU(3)/SO(3)，但具有 spin^h 结构；并将其提升为 D=11 超引力中 AdS₂×M₉ 形式的解，M₉ 为吴流形的单位切丛，进而讨论了黑洞推广、全息解释、UV/IR 流，以及 AdS₂×S⁵ 解和非紧致对偶等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在缺乏通常自旋结构（spin 或 spin^c）的流形上构造超对称解的可能性，探究吴流形这类 spin^h 流形如何凭借荷电费米子（携带半整数同位旋的 SO(3) 杨-米尔斯荷）补偿自旋联络的整体符号缺陷，从而承载超对称，并借此探索一致截断、提升、黑洞与全息对偶以及 RG 流的物理。

Method: 作者通过七维最大规范超引力的截断与拟设，构造出 AdS₂×M₅ 解，其中 M₅=SU(3)/SO(3) 为吴流形；利用 spin^h 结构（而非 spin 或 spin^c 结构）处理旋量在全息/整体上因负号导致的不良好定义问题，该负号由旋量携带的 SO(3) 杨-米尔斯荷的 SU(2) 双重覆盖半整数同位旋表示中的另一负号抵消；随后将该构型提升到 D=11 超引力得到 AdS₂×M₉，M₉ 为吴流形的单位切丛，并从十一维角度分析几何、拓扑与超对称；进一步将七维解推广为黑洞，讨论全息解释与 UV-IR 流，重新考察 AdS₂×H³×H² 解并给出流的精确解；还详细讨论 AdS₂×S⁵ 解到 D=11 的提升，并构造以 SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) 为 M₅ 的非紧致对偶解。

Result: 成功构造了基于吴流形 SU(3)/SO(3) 的超对称 AdS₂×M₅ 解及相应的 spin^h 结构机制，并将其提升为 D=11 的 AdS₂×M₉（吴流形的单位切丛）解；证明七维解可延伸为黑洞，并讨论其全息对偶与 UV/IR 流；证明 AdS₂×H³×H² 解的流方程存在精确解；给出了 AdS₂×S⁵ 到 D=11 的详细提升，比较了其内部空间 M₉（S⁵ 的单位切丛）与吴流形情形的异同；并构造了 M₅=SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) 的非紧致对偶。

Conclusion: 在缺乏 spin 和 spin^c 结构但具有 spin^h 结构的吴流形上可以构造超对称解，表明超对称在更广义的全局结构下依然成立；该构型可一致地提升到十一维并演化为黑洞，相关的全息与 RG 流具有可精确求解的结构；这些结果揭示了 spin^h 几何在超引力解的分类和全息框架中的重要角色。

Abstract: We construct a solution of seven-dimensional maximal gauged supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_5$, where $M_5$ is the five-dimensional Wu manifold $SU(3)/SO(3)$. The solution is supersymmetric, despite the fact that the Wu manifold admits neither a spin structure nor a spin$^c$ structure. Rather, it admits a spin$^h$ structure, wherein the ill-definedness of ordinary spinors due to a minus sign in their holonomy is compensated by a further minus sign arising through the spinors carrying $SO(3)$ Yang-Mills charge, in a half-integer isospin representation of the $SU(2)$ double cover of the $SO(3)$. We show how the configuration can be lifted to a solution of $D=11$ supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_9$, where $M_9$ is the unit tangent bundle of the Wu manifold. We discuss the geometry and topology of $M_9$, and study the supersymmetry from the eleven-dimensional standpoint. We extend the seven-dimensional solution to a black hole, and discuss some aspects of its holographic interpretation and the UV to IR flow. We revisit a previously-known AdS$_2\times H^3\times H^2$ solution, and its UV to IR flow, showing that the flow equations in fact admit of an exact solution. Additionally, we give a detailed discussion of the lift of the AdS$_2\times S^5$ solution to $D=11$, exhibiting similarities and differences between the properties of its internal space $M_9$ (the unit tangent bundle of $S^5$) and the Wu manifold example of our main discussion. We also construct the non-compact dual of the Wu manifold solution, where $M_5$ is taken to be $SL(3,\mathbb R)/SO(3)$.

</details>


### [5] [Topological deformations of local gauge theories: a presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24111)
*Ivan Dneprov,Alexey Donchenko,Maxim Grigoriev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出“拓扑形变”这一系统方法，在预辛 BV-AKSZ 框架内通过规范化不同次（degree）的对称性，将规范结构不同但局部自由度相同的物理系统联系起来；规范化 0 次对称性再现标量场—2-形式规范场及主手征模型—Freedman-Townsend 对偶，规范化 1 次形变则产生新的全局自由度（如幺模引力中的宇宙学常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一组局部自由度可由规范结构与场内容不同的系统描述，这些系统在规范不等价的形式解空间上只相差有限维部分，但由于规范扇区不同，其相容相互作用和对时空全局结构的敏感性可能显著不同。作者希望建立一种统一的方法来解释和构造这类等价关系（对偶）。

Method: 在预辛 BV-AKSZ 框架内引入“拓扑形变”：对一般次（degree）的对称性进行规范化（gauging），使系统耦合到一个 BF 型扇区，且不引入新的局部自由度。其核心是修改底层的预辛规范 PDE。规范化 degree-0 对称性使相差整体对称性的解被认同；规范化 degree-1 对称性（即形变）则产生新的全局自由度。

Result: 规范化 degree-0 对称性重现了四维标量场与 2-形式规范场的对偶，以及主手征模型与 Freedman-Townsend 模型的对偶；规范化 degree-1 形变产生新的全局自由度，例如幺模引力中的宇宙学常数，从而系统性地解释了这些已知对偶现象。

Conclusion: 那些局部自由度相同但规范结构不同的系统之间，可统一理解为拓扑形变的结果：规范化不同次的对称性既能实现对解的全局认同（0 次），也能生成新的全局自由度（1 次）。该框架为理解规范系统的对偶关系、相容相互作用及其对时空全局结构的依赖提供了系统工具。

Abstract: The same local degrees of freedom can be described by systems with different gauge structure and field content. Standard examples are the duality between the scalar field and the 2-form gauge field in four dimensions, and unimodular gravity, where the cosmological constant becomes a global degree of freedom. In a precise sense, the spaces of gauge-inequivalent formal solutions of such systems differ by a finite-dimensional sector. Moreover, because their gauge sectors are different, their consistent interactions and their sensitivity to the global structure of spacetime may differ substantially. We show that such systems are naturally related by what we call topological deformations: gauging a symmetry of generic degree couples the system to a BF-type sector without introducing new local degrees of freedom. The construction is carried out systematically within the presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach, where it amounts to a modification of the underlying presymplectic gauge PDE. Gauging degree-0 symmetries identifies solutions related by global symmetries, which reproduces the scalar--2-form and principal chiral model--Freedman--Townsend dualities. Gauging degree-1 symmetries, i.e. deformations, gives rise to new global degrees of freedom, such as the cosmological constant in unimodular gravity.

</details>


### [6] [Gravitational Cheshire effect from Yang-Mills instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24127)
*Borja Diez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 四维 Yang-Mills 瞬子沿紧致方向降维，可在三维空间中产生具有引力屏蔽机制的复合 stealth 物质构型，且其具体形态依赖于降维所用的引力背景（Taub-NUT 或 Eguchi-Hanson）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探寻四维（反）自对偶瞬子降维后如何在三维产生非平凡的、具有引力屏蔽效应的物质构型，并揭示四维自对偶性与三维 BPS 型关系之间的联系，以及四维中引力不可见的瞬子如何在降维后演化为 stealth 构型。

Method: 从欧氏 Yang-Mills 理论出发，沿由瞬子背景的 $U(1)$ 等距生成的紧致方向对（反）自对偶规范构型做降维。规范联络分解为三维 Yang-Mills 场与伴随 Higgs 场，度规如常给出胀子与阿贝尔规范联络。分别考虑 Taub-NUT 与 Eguchi-Hanson 两种背景，分析降维后阿贝尔与非阿贝尔两个扇区的引力反作用。

Result: 在 Taub-NUT 背景上，降维后的场组织为两对相互屏蔽的组合：阿贝尔扇区的反作用内部相消，非阿贝尔扇区亦独立相消，从而整体物质在平坦三维空间上支撑一个复合 stealth 构型。在 Eguchi-Hanson 背景上，非阿贝尔物质扇区的引力反作用相消，但阿贝尔扇区仍具引力活性，因而会形变三维背景几何，得到部分 stealth 构型。

Conclusion: 四维（反）自对偶性与三维 BPS 型关系之间存在直接联系；在四维中引力不可见的瞬子扇区经降维后，可降为完全复合 stealth 构型（Taub-NUT）或部分 stealth 物质构型（Eguchi-Hanson）。

Abstract: We show that the dimensional reduction of four-dimensional Yang-Mills instantons naturally gives rise to nontrivial matter configurations in three dimensions exhibiting an unusual mechanism of gravitational screening. Starting from Euclidean Yang-Mills theory, we reduce (anti-)self-dual gauge configurations along a compact direction generated by a $U(1)$ isometry of the instanton background. Under this reduction, the gauge connection decomposes into a three-dimensional Yang-Mills field and an adjoint Higgs field, while the metric gives rise, as usual, to a dilaton and an Abelian gauge connection. For instantons on the Taub-NUT background, the reduced fields organize themselves into two mutually screening pairs: the backreaction of the Abelian sector cancels internally, and the same occurs independently in the non-Abelian sector. As a result, the full matter content supports a composite stealth configuration on flat three-dimensional space. By contrast, the dimensional reduction of instantons on the Eguchi-Hanson background yields a non-Abelian matter sector whose gravitational backreaction cancels, while the Abelian sector remains gravitationally active and therefore deforms the three-dimensional background geometry. These examples reveal a direct connection between four-dimensional (anti-)self-duality and three-dimensional BPS-type relations, and illustrate how instantonic sectors that are gravitationally invisible in four dimensions can descend into either fully composite stealth configurations or partially stealth matter configurations after dimensional reduction.

</details>


### [7] [Landscape of Higgs vacua in 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24176)
*Amihay Hanany,Elias Van den Driessche*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究强耦合5d N=1规范理论的模空间Higgs分支，通过1/2-BPS算子及其代数关系刻画，并揭示其作为经典可积系统的结构。


<details>

Abstract: We study supercritical crossovers in four-dimensional Kerr-Newman-AdS black holes at fixed angular momentum and charge. From the exact enthalpy, we prove uniqueness of the physical critical point and positivity of the quartic Landau coefficient throughout the charge-rotation family, treating the uncharged Kerr-AdS endpoint in separate scaling variables. An exact quartic reduction determines the coexistence entropies and latent heat throughout the subcritical regime. Above criticality, the two physical spinodals continue as a complex conjugate pair. Their real temperature projection defines a Widom-type line, and varying the charge-rotation ratio assembles these lines into a crossover surface. The Landau cusp fixes the imaginary entropy and temperature to scale with the square root and three-halves powers of the pressure displacement from criticality. The projected fold and the local heat capacity ridge share a critical tangent. We derive their leading quadratic temperature separation and prove that the ridge lies at a higher temperature near criticality. Exact endpoint solutions and numerical continuation check these predictions. The cusp exponents are common to the family, while the crossover temperature depends on the thermodynamic criterion used to define it.

Abstract: We classify possible topological quantum field theories in 2+1 dimensions with up to 22 anyons in the bosonic case and up to 26 in the fermionic case, through the modular data of modular and supermodular tensor categories. We construct such data via congruence representations of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ using a numerical optimization algorithm that scales to high rank. We chart the relations between the resulting theories, including anyon condensations and Chern-Simons realizations. We find examples of modular data that do not appear to arise from any Chern-Simons theory by any sequence of condensations of anyons or gaugings of ordinary global symmetries. These examples give qualitatively new topological quantum field theories, which go beyond any notion of gauge theory. For examples in this class with 11 and 12 anyon types, we solve the pentagon and hexagon equations explicitly, showing that these modular data are realized by full unitary topological quantum field theories. We also introduce https://www.AnyonAtlas.org, an online resource for exploring the data.

Abstract: We determine the loss of mechanical angular momentum $L^μ_{\rm loss}$ of two gravitationally scattering bodies at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ using worldline quantum field theory (WQFT). We illuminate how these arise: the linear memory contributes through so-called static correlators, while the nonlinear memory vanishes at this order due to symmetry considerations. The computation of the remaining, dynamical part resembles that of the $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ impulse. We generate its integrand using a reformulation of the WQFT Feynman rules that conserves four-momentum at every vertex and plainly exhibits the classical loops. The three-loop integrals are reduced to master integrals, whose differential equations and boundary matching in the post-Newtonian limit we describe in some detail. In contrast to conservative effects at this order, only polylogarithms appear. While individual contributions to the dynamical part are divergent, these cancel in $L^μ_{\rm loss}$. The quadratic-in-radiation part of our result reproduces earlier results, while the complete result agrees with existing post-Newtonian results.

We construct the representations explicitly and quantify the convergence rate. The dynamic interference supercurrent in a system of two Andreev spin qubits exhibits multi-frequency Josephson effect in the absence of external voltage and is governed by the response of the many-body eigenfunctions ($\vert r\rangle$) to variations in the superconducting phase $φ$, determined by the $φ$-space Berry connection $C_{rr'}=\langle r|\partial_φ|r'\rangle$. The macroscopic dynamic interference supercurrent, an inherent property of the superposition state itself, enables an advanced quantum entanglement measurement of the Andreev spin qubits. These findings open new avenues for investigating superconducting nanodevice properties from a wave-function level, with substantial implications for advancing quantum science and technology in solid-state superconducting devices, such as quantum computer, communication, memory, and sensor.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Vortexable Chern bands host ideal fractional Chern insulators. For uniform pairing and a nondegenerate paired state, the exact $T=0$ stiffness determinant is the topological floor times $1+(\Torb/2π|C|)^2\le1+(\TLVP/2π|C|)^2$, where $\Torb$ and $\TLVP$ measure departures from vortexability. Enhancement costs vortexability, and the inequality is strict only if internal components share a site, as in heterostrained bilayers.

</details>

The interplay of in-plane magnetization, superconductivity, and nematicity gives rise to nontrivial Berry curvature and spin-related hybrid Berry curvature on the Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces, which are shown to lead to finite thermally driven nonequilibrium spin polarization and spin Nernst effect. We find that the responses are strongly enhanced by the emergence of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces, and are linear in temperature at low temperatures, rendering a unique spintronic character of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [57] [Effects of Nonmagnetic Impurities on Nonequilibrium Phase Transitions in Microwave-Driven Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25859)
*Taira Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究微波驱动下BCS超导体在非磁性杂质散射与能量弛豫作用下的非平衡稳态，发现非磁性杂质散射强度深刻影响非平衡相图：弱散射下单值序参量、中等散射下出现微波增强/抑制超导态之间的双稳、强散射下则为超导态与正常态之间的双稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示非磁性杂质散射如何影响微波驱动超导体非平衡稳态的性质。鉴于平衡态s波超导对非磁性杂质散射具有鲁棒性（Anderson定理），作者希望探究在非平衡条件下该鲁棒性是否仍然成立，以及杂质散射与微波驱动两大效应（Eliashberg型增强与pair-breaking抑制）之间的竞争机制。

Method: 采用Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh格林函数方法，结合自洽Born近似（self-consistent Born approximation），在微波驱动稳态下自洽求解超导序参量，并在不同温度、驱动频率及杂质散射强度下构建非平衡相图。能量弛豫效应亦被纳入模型。

Result: 非平衡相图强烈依赖杂质散射强度：(1) 无杂质或弱散射时，每个温度和驱动频率下稳态序参量单值；(2) 中等散射强度下，出现微波增强超导态与微波抑制超导态之间的双稳；(3) 散射强度进一步增大后，双稳演化为超导态与正常态之间的双稳。该演化反映微波驱动的两个效应——通过热激发准粒子再分布实现的Eliashberg型增强，与通过pair-breaking导致准粒子生成实现的抑制——之间的竞争。

Conclusion: 与平衡态s波超导对非磁性杂质散射的鲁棒性形成鲜明对比，非磁性杂质散射在决定微波驱动下非平衡超导相变的性质中起核心作用。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium steady states of a microwave-driven BCS superconductor in the presence of nonmagnetic impurity scattering and energy relaxation. Using the Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh Green's function method together with the self-consistent Born approximation, we self-consistently determine the superconducting order parameter in the microwave-driven steady state. We find that the nonequilibrium phase diagram depends strongly on the impurity-scattering strength. When impurity scattering is absent or weak, the steady-state order parameter is single-valued at each temperature and drive frequency. At intermediate scattering strengths, we find bistability between microwave-enhanced and microwave-suppressed superconducting states. As the impurity-scattering strength increases further, we instead find bistability between the superconducting and normal states. This evolution reflects competition between two effects of microwave driving: Eliashberg-type enhancement through the redistribution of thermally excited quasiparticles and suppression through pair-breaking-induced quasiparticle generation. In contrast to the robustness of equilibrium $s$-wave superconductivity against nonmagnetic impurity scattering, our results demonstrate that nonmagnetic impurity scattering plays a central role in determining the character of nonequilibrium superconducting phase transitions under microwave driving.

</details>


### [58] [Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking in the Centrosymmetric Nonsymmorphic Superconductor RuB2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26264)
*Ritu Gupta,Reena,Dibyendu Samanta,Jaskaran Singh,Thomas J. Hicken,Hubertus Luetkens,Jonas Krieger,Sudeep Kumar Ghosh,Yogesh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: RuB2在Tc=1.6 K以下表现出时间反演对称性破缺（TRSB）和完全打开的超导能隙，且为拓扑金属，其超导态很可能是内部反对称的非幺正自旋三重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称非点群AB2（A=Ru, Os）硼化物表现出反常超导性质，但其超导基态的微观本质仍未解决。本文旨在确定RuB2超导态的性质，特别是揭示其配对对称性及时间反演对称性是否破缺。

Method: 结合零场和横场μ子自旋弛豫（muSR）测量、第一性原理电子结构计算以及Ginzburg-Landau对称性分析。零场muSR探测TRSB伴随的自发磁矩，横场muSR表征超导态磁场分布并确定能隙结构；密度泛函理论计算电子能带、自旋轨道耦合及拓扑性质。

Result: 零场muSR在Tc=1.6 K以下观测到自发内部磁场，直接证明TRSB；横场muSR显示完全打开的超导能隙，能隙大小接近BCS弱耦合极限；DFT计算表明RuB2是多带Z2拓扑金属，具有显著自旋轨道耦合；Ginzburg-Landau分析提出内部反对称的非幺正自旋三重态是有前途的序参量候选，可同时解释TRSB与全能隙。

Conclusion: RuB2成为研究由晶体对称性、多带电子结构与自旋轨道耦合相互作用导致的非常规超导的理想平台，并为进一步研究相关非点群硼化物提供动力。两种muSR测量提供互补约束，虽未唯一确定序参量，但缩小了候选范围并凸显非点群对称性对超导的影响。

Abstract: Centrosymmetric nonsymmorphic AB2 (A = Ru, Os) borides exhibit anomalous superconducting properties, but the microscopic nature of their superconducting ground state remains unresolved. Here, we investigate RuB2 using zero- and transverse-field muon spin relaxation (muSR), first-principles electronic-structure calculations, and Ginzburg-Landau symmetry analysis. Zero-field muSR reveals spontaneous internal magnetic fields below Tc = 1.6 K, providing direct evidence for time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB). Transverse-field measurements establish a fully gapped superconducting state, with a gap magnitude close to the BCS weak-coupling limit. Density-functional calculations show that RuB2 is a multiband Z2 topological metal with appreciable spin-orbit coupling. Guided by the experimental observations and the symmetry of the orthorhombic crystal structure, our Ginzburg-Landau analysis suggests that an internally antisymmetric, nonunitary spin-triplet state is a promising candidate for the superconducting order parameter. This candidate is consistent with both the observed TRSB and the fully gapped superconducting state. These results establish RuB2 as a promising platform for investigating unconventional superconductivity arising from the interplay of crystal symmetry, multiband electronic structure, and spin-orbit coupling, and motivate further studies of related nonsymmorphic borides. The two muSR measurements provide complementary constraints: zero-field data probe spontaneous magnetism associated with TRSB, while transverse-field data characterize superconducting field distribution and support a full gap. The topological electronic structure and spin-orbit coupling provide context for unconventional pairing. Although these results do not uniquely determine the order parameter, they narrow candidates and highlight that nonsymmorphic symmetry may shape superconductivity in RuB2.

</details>

Abstract: A project of the novel thermal triple axis spectrometer IN1 at the PIK reactor is presented. Monte Carlo simulations were used for optimization its main parameters. The optimization of the instrument gives an estimated neutron flux as high as 109 n/cm2/s at the sample position at neutron energy about 36 meV at the designed 100 MW power. The modelling of neutron stages like monochromators, analyzers, virtual source, as well as instrument shielding was fulfilled to obtain the best performance of the spectrometer.

### [60] [Finite-size scaling of spin-wave scattering and enhancement in magnonic Fabry-Pérot nanoresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26277)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子神经网络需要紧凑、可重构且能高效散射并聚集自旋波能量的谐振节点。现有的混合磁振子法布里-珀罗谐振器此前仅针对远大于自旋波波长的条带被研究，缺乏对亚微米尺度条带行为的理解，限制了其在紧凑型可重构非线性节点中的应用。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟，研究对象为55 nm厚YIG薄膜上、宽200 nm的CoFeB条带，通过偶极耦合构成混合法布里-珀罗谐振器，逐步将条带长度从无穷缩短至640 nm，分析其在一阶共振(平行构型1.70 GHz、反平行构型1.47 GHz)下的传输间隙、面外进动幅值增强、波前分布、模式量化及非线性响应。

Result: 无限长条带作为一维腔体，在一阶共振处打开传输间隙，使YIG中面外进动幅值分别增强约5倍(平行)和12倍(反平行)，且不畸变透射波前。条带长度为数微米时，从端部发射的后向体自旋波调制沿条带的进动并使反射/透射波前碎裂。长度低于约1.2 μm时，谐振器将入射自旋波散射到薄膜整个等频轮廓，反平行构型下焦散束最明显；平行构型支持弱增强的量子化多模式，反平行构型支持单一模式，面外增强约15倍。条带宽度、厚度和形状可调谐该模式50–100 MHz，强增强效应使非线性频移与折叠现象在入射幅值仅为饱和磁化强度百分之几时就已出现。

Conclusion: 亚微米级磁振子谐振器能够作为磁振子网络中有前景的可重构非线性节点，其强能量增强特性可用于低阈值非线性调控。

Abstract: Magnonic neural networks require compact resonant nodes that scatter spin waves efficiently, concentrate spin-wave energy and can be reconfigured. Hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonators, formed by a ferromagnetic-metal stripe dipolar-coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film, provide low-loss and programmable control of spin-wave transmission, but have so far been studied only for stripes that are long compared with the spin-wave wavelength. Here we use micromagnetic simulations to determine how the response of a 200-nm-wide CoFeB stripe on a 55-nm-thick YIG film evolves as the stripe length is reduced from infinity to 640 nm. An infinitely long stripe acts as a one-dimensional cavity that opens a transmission gap at the first-order resonance (1.70 GHz and 1.47 GHz for the parallel and antiparallel magnetic configuration) and enhances the out-of-plane precession amplitude in the YIG film by factors of about 5 and 12, without distorting the transmitted wavefront. For lengths of a few micrometers, backward-volume spin waves emitted from the stripe ends modulate the precession along the stripe and fragment the reflected and transmitted wavefronts. Below about 1.2 micrometer, the resonator scatters incident spin waves over the entire isofrequency contour of the film, with caustic beams that are most pronounced in the antiparallel configuration. In this regime the parallel configuration supports weakly enhanced quantized modes, whereas the antiparallel configuration supports a single mode with an out-of-plane enhancement of about 15. The width, thickness and shape of the stripe tune this mode by 50-100 MHz, and its strong enhancement lowers the onset of nonlinear frequency shift and fold-over to incident amplitudes of a few percent of the saturation magnetization. Sub-micrometer magnonic resonators are therefore promising reconfigurable nonlinear nodes for magnonic networks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重散射理论预测，在统计各向同性多晶体中相干弹性波并非单一波，其色散方程对给定极化存在多个根，但这些额外模式从未被实验观测到。作者旨在通过精确数值模拟直接观测并验证这一多模现象，填补理论与观测之间的空白。

Result: 当波长接近晶粒相关长度进入几何区时，低频单一模式变为两个模式（纵波与横波均如此）；实测模式遵循色散方程根，方程恒存在第三个衰减超过其余模式的根；正是衰减层级而非根的数目决定可传播、可观测模式的数量。

Conclusion: 随机介质中的相干波传播在几何区本质上呈多模性，本文首次在多晶体中达到该几何区，验证了多重散射理论的预测，并澄清多模观测的物理机制在于衰减分级。

Abstract: Multiple-scattering theory predicts that the coherent elastic wave in a statistically isotropic polycrystal is not a single wave. Its dispersion equation admits multiple roots of a given polarization, but these extra modes have never been observed. Here, using grain-scale finite-element simulations of three-dimensional polycrystals, we resolve the coherent field in the frequency--wavenumber plane and directly observe the predicted multiplicity for both transverse and longitudinal excitations: a single mode at low frequencies gives way to two in the geometric regime as the wavelength approaches the grain correlation length. The measured modes follow the roots of the dispersion equation, which always carries a third root whose attenuation exceeds that of the others. It is this attenuation hierarchy, not the root count, that dictates how many modes propagate and can be observed. Coherent wave propagation in random media is thus inherently multimodal in the geometric regime, which we reach here for the first time in polycrystals.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择（CISS）使非磁性结产生自旋成为可能，但对其对称性要求的争议尚未解决，亟需厘清何种结可作极化器、何种结仅作过滤器。

Result: 自旋极化器要求手性结，而自旋过滤器可由非手性结实现；该对称性约束作用于整个结而非仅分子。非手性分子依结几何结构可作极化器或过滤器（紧束缚模型证实）。互易性禁止线性两端磁阻探测极化，需以非局域测量区分极化器与过滤器并判断结是否破缺时间反演对称性。

Conclusion: 结的整体对称性决定其自旋极化行为：极化需手性结，过滤可由非手性结实现；仅在非局域测量中方可区分极化器与过滤器并判定时间反演对称性破缺。

Abstract: Chirality-induced spin selectivity can enable spin generation by nonmagnetic junctions, but its symmetry requirements remain debated. We show that the symmetry of the junction constrains its polarizing behavior: a spin polarizer requires a chiral junction, whereas an achiral junction can act as a spin filter. This symmetry involves the junction as a whole, not only the molecule. Achiral molecules can act as polarizers or filters depending on junction geometry, as confirmed with a minimal tight-binding model. Reciprocity forbids polarization detection by linear two-terminal magnetoresistance; we propose a nonlocal measurement that distinguishes polarizers from filters and establishes whether the junction breaks time-reversal symmetry.

</details>

Abstract: The effects of charge disproportionation between V$^{4+}$ and V$^{5+}$ on the exchange interactions and related magnetic, electronic and optical properties are studied in LiV$_2$O$_5$ and NaV$_2$O$_5$. The magnetic exchange interactions between local moments on atomic sites are calculated using a linear response approach based on the QS$GW$ band structure. While the induced magnetic moments are small on the vanadyl O$_v$ and bridge O$_b$, their exchange interactions with the V are found to be larger than between V atoms. The Néel temperature $T_N$ are calculated within the mean-field (MFA) and random phase (RPA) approximations for two different crystal structures with $P2_1mn$ and $Pmmn$ spacegroups. The critical temperatures are are corrected by a quantum statistical factor of (S+1)/S to obtain reasonable agreement with experiment.

The spin wave dispersions are calculated from the classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian including magnetic moments on both V and O atoms. Besides the lowest energy weakly interacting spin waves of the two chains of spins in the unit cell, the model predicts additional high energy optical spin waves, which correspond to more complex spin excitations in which the spins on the two V across a bridge, vanadyl O and bridge O precess independently from each other instead of staying locked together according to their collinear alignment in the ground state. These show only weak dispersion and extend up to about 1 eV but we argue that these are an artefact of using the Heisenberg model beyond its validity range and would be suppressed in a more complete theory that allows for coupling of the transverse spin with longitudinal spin and charge excitations.

Abstract: he generation of even-order harmonic spectra from inversion symmetry breaking is well known, however, the tunability of the intensity and angular anisotropy on various harmonics orders remains unclear. Here, we demonstrate the second and fourth-harmonics in the ferroelectric NbOI2 exhibit distinct wavelength-dependence and anisotropies in the near-infrared regime. In particular, as the wavelength of incident light is tuned from 2150 nm to 2450 nm, the second-harmonic remains strongest for excitation along the ferroelectric polar axis, whereas the polarization of the fourth- harmonic changes preferred axis. This effect is found to be driven by the interplay of transition dipole matrix elements and interband excitations between very narrow bands near the Fermi level. We expect the tunable anisotropy of high-harmonics to enable novel optical switching devices.

Abstract: Controlling magnetic phases and properties through composition and external conditions is central to the development of magnetic materials. Atomistic foundation models offer a route to predicting such behaviour across diverse materials, but magnetic materials remain challenging because their atomic structure and local moments are strongly coupled, while temperature and disorder drive magnetic moments into non-collinear states with evolving orientations and magnitudes. Broadly pretrained atomistic models still sample only limited magnetic diversity, while finite-temperature descriptions of coupled lattice and magnetic fluctuations remain largely material-specific. Here we introduce MagPT, pretrained across 48 elements on 912,492 spin-lattice configurations spanning structural, orientational and longitudinal magnetic fluctuations over 0 $\sim$ 1,000 K and 0 $\sim$ 10 GPa. By treating species, atomic positions and vector moments together, MagPT distinguishes magnetic states that share the same atomic structure but differ in spin configuration and energy. It captures magnetic behaviour across composition, temperature and length scales, from Slater-Pauling-type composition-magnetism relations in transition-metal alloys and finite-temperature phase evolution in elemental ferromagnets and magnetic shape-memory Heusler alloys, to emergent long-wavelength non-collinear spin textures in chiral magnets and local chemistry-magnetism coupling in high-entropy alloys. MagPT moves atomistic studies of magnetic materials beyond material-specific approaches, establishing a general framework for exploring and designing magnetic matter across diverse chemistries and conditions.

</details>

Abstract: In covalent organic frameworks (COFs), the geometry of the building units predetermines the structure of the framework, yet comparable design principles relating the COF linkers to the local motions that govern guest adsorption and diffusion as well as mechanical response have remained beyond reach. Both are constructed from the same 4-connected square planar linker, $4,4',4'',4'''$-(pyrene-1,3,6,8-tetrayl)tetraaniline (PyTTA), reticulated with the 12-connected nanographene hexakis{[3,5-bis($p$-formylphenyl)-4,6-dimethoxyphenyl]}hexabenzocoronene to give COF-412, {[(HBC-LA$_{12}$)(PyTTA)$_3$]}$_{\mathit{imine}}$, and with the 6-connected triptycene 2,3,6,7,14,15-hexakis(4-formylphenyl)triptycene to give COF-564, {[(Trip-CHO)$_2$(PyTTA)$_3$]}$_{\mathit{imine}}$. The PDF-MD approach resolves the amplitude, the time scale, and the spatial extent of the motion of all COF linkers, separating the limited libration of the fused aromatic cores from the larger reorientational tilts of the pendant aryl rings they carry and from the translation of whole linkers within the net. The simulations further reveal that exchanging the nanographene- for the triptycene-based linker tunes the equilibrium tilt and the positional probability density of PyTTA, raising the tilt amplitude of its linker partner, and, notably, strengthens the coupling between the translations of neighbouring linkers. This approach provides a blueprint for understanding local dynamical properties in COFs, and shows them to be selectable through the choice of building unit and amenable to design.

Periodic trends in $ξ$ reveal a $5d$ plateau near 34$\%$, with a lanthanide-contraction-driven maximum in Hf, followed by a sharp decline across the $6p$ block. In FNDMC, $η$ escalates rapidly at the onset of group-1/2 and toward the $6p$ closed-shell limit, peaking at 5.42$\%$ in Rn. Although fixed-node errors dominate $η$ in most sixth-period elements, a locality error inversion occurs in Bi, with Pb exhibiting comparable error magnitudes. Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials are increasingly used across diverse materials, yet their reliability under directional deformation remains unclear. Density-functional theory provides references for isotropic, uniaxial and biaxial loading in MPtrj and isotropic loading in MinX-Eq. At $\pm 10\%$, every model has a larger median force error under compression, with pooled isotropic compression-to-extension ratios of 2.12 and 3.30 for MPtrj and MinX-Eq. Decomposing error growth into DFT force-scale growth and relative-error evolution shows that relative errors decrease on both branches, faster under compression in MPtrj but slower under compression in MinX-Eq. Force-scale matching moves the error ratios toward unity, although MinX-Eq retains a compression-side excess across models. Radial and SOAP analyses place compressed structures at shorter separations and farther from public reference pools, while readout-associated model features show larger compression-side displacements. These results distinguish stronger physical responses from changes in relative predictive accuracy and provide a basis for assessing universal potentials in deformation simulations.

Result: CARVS在不含环连接显式信息的情况下仍清晰给出该拓扑转变的标志；网络纠缠导致径向分布函数和结构因子出现特征性变化，包括非键合节点间短程关联的出现及低角散射前峰的消失。据此确立了无序四面体网络中拓扑转变的实验可观测结构签名，并提示具有延长连接子(linker)的框架材料可能是实验上寻找纠缠驱动拓扑转变的有前景候选体系。

Conclusion: 通过CARVS、径向分布函数和结构因子等更易实验获取的结构描述符，可有效识别纠缠驱动的拓扑转变，无需依赖实验中难以测量的环连接与环拧数等纠缠量；这为在无序四面体网络及框架材料中实验搜寻纠缠诱导的拓扑转变提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Topological transitions in disordered network materials are often characterised using descriptors of entanglement, such as ring linking and writhe, which can be difficult to access experimentally. Here we investigate whether a topological transition can instead be identified using structural descriptors more readily connected to experiment. We reanalyse configurations of a colloidal tetrahedral network model known to exhibit a pressure-driven transition between unentangled and and entangled states. We show that the cumulative all-rings vertex symbol (CARVS), a descriptor of medium-range network topology based on ring statistics, provides a clear signature of this transition despite containing no explicit information about ring linking. We further show that network entanglement produces characteristic changes in the radial distribution function and structure factor, including the emergence of short-range correlations between nonbonded nodes and the disappearance of a low-angle scattering pre-peak. These results establish experimentally accessible structural signatures of topological transitions in disordered tetrahedral networks. Comparison with canonical continuous random networks further suggests that framework materials with extended linkers may provide promising candidate systems in which to search experimentally for entanglement-driven topological transitions.

These results demonstrate that direct epitaxial growth can produce high-quality, strongly piezoelectric $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ films and provide a route toward thickness-controlled layered substrates for high-performance acoustic devices.

Temperature-dependent photoluminescence measurements confirm emission at 1550 nm (0.8 eV) within the telecommunications C-band, with characteristic QD-like temperature dependence. Abstract: We demonstrate efficient electron doping of exfoliated MoS$_2$ through the deposition of N-heterocyclic carbene (NHC) molecules onto its surface. The largest phonon-energy shifts are observed in monolayer and bilayer flakes whereas changes in bulk-like MoS$_2$ are negligible, indicating that the doping efficiency depends on the number of layers. This thickness dependence highlights the dominant role of interfacial charge transfer at the NHC/MoS$_2$ interface. Quantitative analysis of the Raman modes of as-exfoliated and NHC-covered MoS$_2$, together with comparison with literature values, allows us to estimate an increase in the two-dimensional electron density of about two-orders of magnitude in MoS$_2$ mono- and bilayers. These results demonstrate that NHC-based surface deposition is a promising approach for chemically tuning the carrier density of semiconducting transition-metal dichalcogenides and provide a potential route toward molecularly tunable two-dimensional electronic devices, in which, analogous to modulation doping, the donors reside outside the semiconductor lattice.

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce an Abaqus user-subroutine implementation of the phase-field model of fracture initiated by Kumar, Francfort, and Lopez-Pamies (\emph{J. Mech. Phys. Solids} 112, 523--551, 2018). The implementation is presented for the basic case of isotropic linear elastic brittle materials with a Drucker-Prager strength surface. The material inputs are therefore the Young's modulus $E$, the Poisson's ratio $ν$, the uniaxial tensile strength $\sts$, the uniaxial compressive strength $\scs$, and the fracture toughness $G_c$. The mathematical correspondence between the heat equation and the evolution equation for the phase field $v(\bfX,t)$ is exploited through a UMAT subroutine, wherein the driving force $c_{\texttt{e}}(\bfX,t)$ --- accounting for the material strength surface --- in the phase-field model plays the role of a heat source. To ensure that the equations for the displacement field $\bfu(\bfX,t)$ and the phase field $v(\bfX,t)$ are solved in a staggered manner --- a solution strategy that, as opposed to monolithic approaches, reliably yields the correct physical solution --- we make use of a combination of UEXTERNALDB, UAMP, MPC, and URDFIL subroutines. Within this approach, the MPC and URDFIL subroutines are additionally utilized to enforce the required bounding and irreversibility inequalities on $v(\bfX,t)$ via a primal-dual active set approach. The capabilities and robustness of the proposed implementation are demonstrated by simulating the ``Nine Circles of Elastic Brittle Fracture'' on a silicate glass --- a set of tests that span the entire range of well-settled experimental knowledge on fracture nucleation and propagation. These simulations also serve to illustrate the deployment of the code using several native Abaqus finite elements, including the simplicial elements CPS3, CAX3, and C3D4.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 FK-ReMask：1) 生成过程中维持多条去噪轨迹，并采用 Feynman-Kac 粒子选择机制复制有潜力的候选；2) 引入原子重掩码机制，在生成过程中重新开放已提交（committed）的原子位点，使备选组分得以涌现，克服仅靠克隆导致组分探索受限的问题；3) 全程推断期执行、无需梯度与重训练，可同时作用于原子坐标、晶格和原子类型通道。

Result: 在四类电子、光伏和力学目标上，FK-ReMask 提升了 SUN（稳定、独特、新颖）且性质达标材料的产率；并进一步演示了对三种光伏性质（如带隙、形成能等）的联合引导，表明多个物理目标可完全在推断期指定而无需重训练。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models provide powerful priors for generating new materials, including inorganic crystals, but generating useful materials requires satisfying specific chemical and physical properties, from mechanical stiffness to bandgaps. Existing approaches either retrain models for new target properties or steer only the atom coordinates and lattice during generation, leaving composition unchanged despite its fundamental role in determining physical properties. We present FK-ReMask, an inference-time, gradient-free method that steers both the continuous and discrete channels of a pretrained crystal diffusion model. During generation, FK-ReMask maintains multiple denoising trajectories and replicates promising candidates using Feynman-Kac particle selection. However, selection alone is insufficient: in the atom-type channel, committed elements cannot be revised, so cloning propagates existing compositions and limits the population's ability to explore new ones. We address this with an atom re-masking mechanism, which reopens committed sites during the generation process and allows alternative compositions to emerge. Across four electronic, photovoltaic, and mechanical objectives, our method improves the yield of SUN (stable, unique, novel) and property-on-target materials. We further demonstrate joint steering of three photovoltaic properties, showing that multiple physical objectives can be specified entirely at inference time without retraining.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Thermal conductivity ($κ$) measurement of a thin film is critical for developing thermal management materials in heterogeneous systems. A nanoscale point contact method based on scanning thermal microscopy (SThM) offers a unique approach to probe $κ$ with nanoscale spatial resolution in sub-100 nm thick films. Extracting $κ$ of a thin film from this method requires separating film, substrate, and interface contributions that are comparable in magnitude. Multiple unknowns make this extraction a high dimensional problem. In this work, we used dimensional analysis to reduce the number of variables to provide physical insights into the realistic bounds of $κ$ measurement. We show that the total tip-sample thermal resistance $R_{ss}$, originally a function of six variables, reduces to four independent dimensionless groups. These groups are used to construct a mathematical relation that captures the dependence of $R_{ss}$ on the governing parameters. The resulting analysis shows that the point contact tip-film-substrate problem is governed by a single dimensionless invariant, $Ω= R_{ss}κ_{\mathrm{sub}}t$, and two control parameters: the confinement ratio $t/b$ and the thermal contrast $κ_{\mathrm{sub}}/κ_{\mathrm{film}}$. From the invariant, we derive an amplification factor, set only by the contact geometry and the interfaces, that converts the uncertainty of a measurement into an uncertainty in the extracted conductivity. This factor scales with the ratio of the parasitic interface resistance to the film's sheet thermal resistance and bounds the film conductivity that a given contact can resolve. We apply the framework to a 14$\pm$1 nm Al film grown on SiO$_2$ to locate the measurement within the established bound. The framework then lets us map its thermal resistance with sub-100 nm spatial resolution and recover the local thermal conductivity.

Abstract: The thermal conductivity of natural diamond can be further improved by ~50% by eliminating isotope impurity to obtain pure 12C diamond. However, unlike isolated point defects, how color centers scatter phonons and correspondingly limit the thermal conductivity remain poorly understood. Here, we report the thermal conductivities of two isotope-purified diamond with different Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) color center concentrations, confirmed by photoluminescence (PL) and secondary-ion mass spectrometry (SIMS). Results show ~ 3200 W m-1 K-1 and ~ 2100 W m-1 K-1 for two samples of low and high NV concentrations, respectively. Modified Callaway model analyses show that first-principles-derived scattering parameters can reproduce room-temperature thermal conductivity of the high NV concentration sample under a vacancy concentration assumption (~30% of the total Nitrogen concentration), but fail to reproduce its temperature dependence. Compared to PL and SIMS results, which show ~7.1-fold NV concentration and ~92-fold difference in total Nitrogen concentration between two samples, suggesting the assumed 30% vacancy fraction may be greatly overestimated. Taken together, these results suggest that NV-color-center defects may scatter phonons more strongly than models treating Nitrogen and vacancy as independent point defects, providing new insights into color-center-induced phonon-scattering mechanisms.

Abstract: This article describes the application of HEXRD, an open-source Python library for X-ray diffraction analysis, and HEXRDGUI, its companion graphical application, to dynamic compression experiments. Built on a generic multi-detector geometric model, the toolkit supports arbitrary instrument configurations with independently positioned planar or cylindrical detector panels, arbitrary beam directions, and multiple X-ray sources. It calibrates instruments from powder rings, Laue spots or the rings of an unknown phase, fits whole patterns by Le Bail or Rietveld refinement, and imports data from the TARDIS, FIDDLE and PXRDIP platforms at the National Ignition Facility (NIF) and OMEGA EP. Although originally developed for high-energy diffraction microscopy at synchrotron sources, HEXRD has been adopted as an analysis tool for dynamic compression X-ray diffraction experiments at facilities including the NIF, the Matter in Extreme Conditions end-station at the Linac Coherent Light Source, the Dynamic Compression Sector (DCS) at the Advanced Photon Source, OMEGA EP at the Laboratory for Laser Energetics, and the High Energy Density (HED) instrument at the European XFEL. Calibrated from the same ring positions on a single planar detector, HEXRD and the independent code pyFAI predict the same scattering angle 2$θ$ to numerical precision. On a dual-source dataset from the NIF, calibrating the detectors against both X-ray sources at once fits the rings of both to an RMS of 0.18° in 2$θ$, about one image-plate pixel, whereas a fit to either source alone, with the plate tilts held at their nominal values, fits its own rings nearly as well but mispredicts the other's by about 0.6°. The software is freely available under a BSD license.

</details>

Abstract: The extrinsic bulk photovoltaic effect from asymmetric electron-defect scattering can make an important contribution to the total photocurrent and is highly diagnostic of defect type. This consists of the lower boundary energy and changes Fermi level bonding in NbB$_2$ and TaB$_2$.

*Shixian Liu,Bin Liu,Fei Yin,Gang Wang,Yiming Dong,Yangjun Qin,Alexandra V. Kosyakova,Qiang Xian,Alexander A. Barinov,Ke Xu,Gang Zhang*

Acoustic branches exhibit response times differing by a factor of five, with competing scattering channels producing a transient energy overshoot. ### [85] [Microscopic Compensation of Circular Optical Response in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25489)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文区分了磁性对称性（决定圆二色响应是否被允许）与微观补偿（决定残留信号的大小与符号），提出通过分解有限频率带间 Kubo 响应来解析弱/消失/变号磁光信号的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱的或消失的圆光信号不一定意味着螺旋度敏感的跃迁弱，它可能是更大且相互抵消的微观贡献的残余。传统宏观求和会掩盖动量空间内与不同跃迁间的抵消机制，导致对补偿磁体中磁光信号的误判。

Method: 作者在对跃迁和动量求和之前分解有限频率带间 Kubo 响应，将固定动量处不同跃迁间的抵消与跨布里渊区的动量抵消区分开。用一个精确补偿的 d 波模型确立对称性基准，并对 MnTe 进行第一性原理 Wannier 计算，分析圆二色跃迁核（circular kernel）在动量空间的分布与符号变化。

Result: 在 MnTe 中发现一个“对称性暗”的 Néel 取向，其变号圆形核的体积分严格为零（消光）；旋转 Néel 矢量可解除该消光，但允许的响应仍表现为大正、负动量扇区之间的微小失衡。该失衡随光子能量反转并翻转宏观符号。

Conclusion: 磁对称性决定圆二色响应是否被允许，而微观补偿决定其残存强度与符号。这一区分提供了诠释补偿磁体中弱、消失及变号磁光信号的通用框架。

Abstract: A weak or vanishing circular optical signal need not imply weak helicity-sensitive transitions: it can be the residual of much larger opposing microscopic contributions. We resolve the finite-frequency interband Kubo response before summing over transitions and momentum, separating cancellation among transitions at fixed momentum from cancellation across the Brillouin zone. An exactly compensated $d$-wave model establishes the symmetry benchmark. In MnTe, first-principles Wannier calculations reveal a symmetry-dark Néel orientation with an extended sign-changing circular kernel whose bulk integral vanishes. Reorienting the Néel vector removes this extinction, yet the allowed response remains a small imbalance between large positive- and negative-momentum sectors; the imbalance reverses with photon energy and flips the macroscopic sign. Thus magnetic symmetry decides whether circular response is allowed, whereas microscopic compensation determines how much survives and with what sign. This distinction provides a general framework for interpreting weak, vanishing, and sign-changing magneto-optical signals in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [86] [Autonomous adaptive spectral analysis with an LLM agent for self-driving laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25619)
*Yuki K. Wakabayashi,Yui Ogawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由大语言模型（LLM）监督的自适应光谱分析智能体，用于自动驾驶实验室中的表征数据异常处理：确定性数值脚本正常则直接采用，可恢复失败则通过有界参数调整重新分析，无法科学辩护时返回 NaN，并在 203 条 β-Ga2O3 透射光谱上验证（保留 183 条、恢复 16 条、NaN 4 条）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶实验室闭环中，表征数据常需自动分析以提取定量指标，但现有分析脚本依赖物理假设与预设判据，对判据之外的测量会失败；因此需要自动决定“能否恢复出可靠数值，抑或不报告数值”，即实现自主的异常处理。

Method: 实现一个由 LLM 监督的自适应光谱分析智能体：对每条光谱先执行预定义确定性分析脚本，并利用原始光谱与拟合诊断评估结果；有效结果原样接受，可恢复失败通过对预定义拟合参数的有界调整进行重分析，无法获得科学上可辩护的数值时返回 NaN。该方法在 203 条外延 β-Ga2O3 薄膜的紫外-可见透射光谱上评估（对应此前报道的自驱动薄膜生长实验），提取 Urbach 能量作为光学质量指标，并记录光谱特征、参数调整与决策理由作为审计记录。

Result: 预定义光学分析脚本成功返回 183 条光谱的 Urbach 能量，20 条失败；智能体保留这 183 个值，对 20 个异常案例进行判断，通过有界参数调整恢复其中 16 个，另 4 个因光谱证据不足以支持可靠的 Urbach 尾而返回 NaN。审计记录完整记录了光谱特征、参数变化及每次决策的理由。

Conclusion: 结果表明 LLM 智能体可在保持定量属性提取由确定性数值分析完成的前提下，实现自主科学异常处理，有助于提升自动驾驶实验室对超出预设判据的测量数据的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories accelerate materials exploration through autonomous closed-loop integration of synthesis, characterization, and next-experiment selection. However, characterization does not always directly provide the target property; measured spectra or images often require automated analysis to extract quantitative descriptors based on physically motivated assumptions and predefined criteria. Such procedures can fail for measurements outside these criteria, requiring a decision on whether a reliable value can be recovered or no numerical value should be reported. Here, we implemented an autonomous adaptive spectral-analysis agent using a large language model (LLM) to supervise a deterministic numerical-analysis routine. For each spectrum, the agent executes the predefined analysis script and assesses the result using the measured spectrum and fitting diagnostics. Valid results are accepted unchanged, recoverable failures are reanalyzed through bounded adjustments of predefined fitting parameters, and NaN (not a number) is returned when no scientifically defensible value can be obtained. We evaluated the workflow on optical transmittance spectra from 203 epitaxial beta-Ga2O3 films grown during a previously reported self-driving thin-film growth campaign. The predefined optical analysis script returned Urbach energy, an optical-quality metric, for 183 spectra and failed for 20. The agent retained those 183 values and evaluated 20 exception cases, recovering 16 through bounded parameter adjustments and returning NaN for 4 when the spectral evidence did not support a reliable Urbach tail. The audit records captured spectral features, parameter changes, and rationale for each decision. These results demonstrate autonomous scientific exception handling by an LLM agent while leaving quantitative property extraction to deterministic numerical analysis.

</details>

*Adam Zheng,April Zheng,Sheng Ran*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AI/ML的多模态方法，将材料晶体结构图神经网络（GNN）与材料性质/结构文本的大语言模型（LLM）进行信息融合，用于预测材料磁序（非磁性、铁磁性、反铁磁性、亚铁磁性四类），其准确率优于单独的GNN或LLM。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的材料磁性测试昂贵且耗时，而已有AI/ML模型多为单一模态。作者希望结合材料的晶体结构信息（图结构）与语义文本描述（性质与结构），利用多模态信息融合提升磁序预测的准确性。

Result: 在包含Materials Project、Magndata及铀化合物数据集的组合数据上，GNN与LLM在多模态方法（三种融合策略）下的总体准确率均优于单独的GNN方法和单独的LLM方法。

Conclusion: 结合晶体结构GNN与语义文本LLM的多模态信息融合方法能有效提升材料磁序预测性能，证明了结构信息与文本描述互补的多模态建模对材料性质预测的价值。

Abstract: AI/ML models are useful for the prediction of materials' properties. Recently, there has been great progress in developing Neural Network models to predict the properties of solids, molecules, and crystals, avoiding expensive and time-consuming laboratory testing. This study presents a novel AI/ML-based multi-modal approach to predict material magnetic ordering using material crystal structure information as input to a Graph Neural Network model (GNN) and semantic text descriptions of the material's properties and structure as input to a Large Language Model (LLM), before performing information fusion of the GNN and LLM outputs to predict material magnetic ordering in four classes (Non-magnetic, Ferromagnetic, Antiferromagnetic, Ferrimagnetic). The model is trained, validated, and tested on a dataset which is a combination of Materials Project dataset, Magndata dataset, and a set of uranium compounds with magnetic ordering determined in laboratory testing, with an 80/10/10 (training/validation/testing) data split. The proposed GNN and LLM multi-modal approach achieves better overall accuracy than both the standalone GNN and LLM approaches with three fusion strategies.

</details>


### [88] [A Stress-Induced Tetragonal Distortion in K2OsCl6 under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25913)
*Neha Bura,Peije Zhang,Ganesh Bera,Catalin Popescu,Caleb J. Bennett,Brendan J. Kennedy,Daniel Errandonea*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: K2OsCl6（立方相）在高压下依次经历立方→四方（>9.5 GPa）→单斜（≈14.2 GPa）的结构转变，其中四方相源于非静水应力引起的对称性降低而非真实热力学相，单斜相在17.9 GPa以上保持稳定至35.4 GPa，压缩过程中体积模量显著增大（26→71 GPa），且所有转变为可逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高压下K2OsCl6的结构行为及其相变序列，探讨非静水应力对对称性降低的影响，并与同类化合物（如K2IrCl6）的高压行为进行对比。

Method: 采用角散式同步辐射粉末X射线衍射，将立方（Fm-3m）K2OsCl6加压至35.4 GPa，通过分析衍射图谱与晶胞参数确定各压力区间的相组成与结构，并进行状态方程（EOS）拟合获得体积模量。

Result: K2OsCl6在9.5(3) GPa以上由立方相转变为四方（P4/mnc）相，约14.2 GPa开始转变为单斜（P21/n）相；四方与单斜相在14.2–17.2 GPa共存，17.9 GPa以上仅存单斜相并稳定至最高压力。四方结构被归因于压力传递介质引起的非静水偏应力所致的应力诱导对称性降低。体积模量从立方相的26(2) GPa升至单斜相的71(3) GPa，表明跨相变可压缩性显著降低。该相变序列与K2IrCl6类似，但K2OsCl6的相变压力更低，且所有转变为可逆。

Conclusion: K2OsCl6在压缩下呈现立方→四方→单斜的相变序列，中间四方相为应力诱导的对称性降低而非独立热力学相。单斜相在高压下稳定且可压缩性显著下降（体积模量增大），其高压行为与K2IrCl6相似但转变压力更低，且所有结构转变均可在减压时恢复。

Abstract: The structural behaviour of cubic (Fm-3m) K2OsCl6 under compression was investigated to 35.4 GPa using angle-dispersive synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction. Upon increasing pressure, K2OsCl6 undergoes a sequence of structural transformations from the ambient-pressure cubic phase to a tetragonal (P4/mnc) phase above 9.5(3) GPa, followed by a transition to a monoclinic (P21/n) phase beginning at approximately 14.2 GPa. The tetragonal and monoclinic phases coexist over the pressure range 14.2-17.2 GPa, while the monoclinic phase is observed as the sole phase at 17.9 GPa and remains stable up to the maximum pressure investigated. Analysis of the diffraction profiles and unit-cell parameters indicates that the intermediate tetragonal structure is likely associated with stress-induced symmetry lowering rather than a distinct thermodynamic phase. This distortion, which is adequately described by the tetragonal P4/mnc space group, is attributed to non-hydrostatic deviatoric stresses arising from the pressure-transmitting medium. Equation-of-state analysis reveals a pronounced reduction in compressibility across the structural transitions, with the bulk modulus increasing from 26(2) GPa for the cubic phase to 71(3) GPa for the monoclinic phase. The pressure-induced structural sequence is analogous to that reported for related compounds such as K2IrCl6, although the transitions in K2OsCl6 occur at lower pressures. All structural transformations are reversible upon decompression.

</details>


### [89] [Interfacial origin of magnetic compensation and topological-like Hall effect in crystalline lutetium iron garnet thin films.](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25965)
*Satakshi Pandey,Anne Forget,Sarah Clement,Christophe Gatel,B{é}n{é}dicte Warot- Fonrose,Gr{é}goire de Loubens,Jean-Baptiste Moussy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了在不同厚度下，生长于 SGGG(111) 衬底上的单晶 LuIG（Lu3Fe5O12）薄膜的结构、化学和磁性，发现界面处存在约 3 nm 富 Gd 层，导致约 50 K 的磁补偿温度；并利用相邻 Pt 薄膜通过反常霍尔效应（AHE）探测了与 DMI 相关的手性自旋纹理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土铁石榴石（REIG）因高居里温度、低 Gilbert 阻尼和优异绝缘性成为自旋器件关键材料，且近期被发现可承载鲁棒的拓扑手性自旋纹理，有望用作下一代自旋电子器件的信息载体。因此需要深入理解 REIG 薄膜界面处的结构与磁性行为，以服务于器件工程。

Method: 在 SGGG(111) 衬底上生长不同厚度的单晶 LuIG 薄膜，表征其结构、化学与磁性；发现 LuIG/SGGG 界面存在约 3 nm 富 Gd 层并产生约 50 K 磁补偿温度。进一步在 LuIG 层上沉积超薄 Pt 薄膜，利用反常霍尔效应（AHE）进行探测，通过厚度依赖的 AHE 测量分析非单调特征及其来源。

Result: 实验证实 LuIG/SGGG 界面存在约 3 nm 富 Gd 的 LuIG 层，带来约 50 K 的磁补偿温度。AHE 回线除磁补偿点外还呈现显著的非单调特征，通常归因于自旋霍尔类拓扑霍尔效应（SH-THE）以及由 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（DMI）产生的非共线自旋纹理。厚度依赖的 AHE 测量表明，Gd 扩散导致的成分变化破坏了反演对称性，可能对 DMI 有所贡献。

Conclusion: Gd 扩散引发的界面成分变化打破了反演对称性并可能贡献 DMI，这一发现有助于从基础层面理解和工程化磁性石榴石的不同界面，从而推动先进自旋电子器件的应用。

Abstract: Ferrimagnetic oxides such as rare-earth iron garnets (REIG) have emerged as essential components of spin-based devices owing to their high Curie temperatures, low Gilbert damping, and excellent insulating properties. Recently, REIGs have attracted immense interest for hosting robust chiral spin textures that can be potential information carriers in next-generation spintronic devices. Here we investigate the structural, chemical, and magnetic behaviour of single-crystal Lu3Fe5O12 (LuIG) thin films for different film thicknesses on a Gd3(Mg,Zr)Ga5O12(111) or SGGG(111) substrate. Our results demonstrate the presence of a 3 nm-thick Gd-rich LuIG layer at the LuIG/SGGG interface, which gives rise to a magnetic compensation temperature around 50 K. We further probe the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in LuIG layers with an adjacent platinum ultrathin film. In addition to the magnetic compensation point, the AHE loops show pronounced nonmonotonic features, often attributed to the spin Hall topological-like Hall effect (SH-THE) and non-collinear spin textures arising from the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI). LuIG thickness-dependent AHE measurements reveal that the composition variation due to Gd diffusion breaks the inversion symmetry and could contribute to DMI. These findings shed light on fundamental understanding and engineering of the different interfaces of magnetic garnets for advanced spintronic device applications.

</details>


### [90] [Magnetoelectric Bulk Photovoltaic Effect in Centrosymmetric Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26057)
*Longjun Xiang,Fuming Xu,Lei Zhang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证了一种磁电体光伏效应（MBPE），表明在中心对称固体中，通过光的电场与磁场分量的双线性耦合，也可以产生直流光电流，从而突破了体光伏效应要求破坏空间反演对称的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为体光伏电流需要破坏空间反演对称性，这限制了中心对称材料在光电应用中的可用性。作者旨在探索在中心对称固体中实现直流光电流的新机制。

Method: 作者揭示了源于光的电场与磁场分量双线性耦合的磁电体光伏效应（MBPE），其量子几何起源为塞曼量子度规（Zeeman quantum metric）与塞曼贝里曲率（Zeeman Berry curvature）的偶极矩。通过对称性分析，识别出四种不同的MBPE机制，包括时间反演偶/奇的类注入和类位移光电流，分别由线偏振或圆偏振光驱动。

Result: 对称性分析表明，在垂直入射光下，MBPE广泛适用于二维中心对称的非磁性和磁性材料。在中心对称单层Pt₂HgSe₃和交错磁性（altermagnetic）RuF₄中预言了量级约为1 mA/m的可观光电流密度。特别是，RuF₄中的类注入光电流在切换光的螺旋度或Néel矢量方向时会发生符号反转。

Conclusion: 该工作将体光伏效应扩展到中心对称固体，并为中心对称材料的光电应用以及交错磁序的电学读出开辟了新途径。

Abstract: It is commonly believed that a bulk photovoltaic current requires broken spatial inversion symmetry. However, in this work, we demonstrate that dc photocurrents can arise in centrosymmetric solids from the bilinear coupling between the electric and magnetic components of light. This magnetoelectric bulk photovoltaic effect (MBPE) has a quantum-geometric origin in the dipole moments of the Zeeman quantum metric and Zeeman Berry curvature. We identify four distinct MBPE mechanisms, including time-reversal-even and time-reversal-odd injection-like and shift-like photocurrents driven by linearly or circularly polarized light. Our symmetry analysis shows that the MBPE is allowed in broad classes of two-dimensional centrosymmetric nonmagnetic and magnetic materials under normally incident light. As representative examples, we predict sizable MBPEs in centrosymmetric monolayer Pt$_2$HgSe$_3$ and altermagnetic RuF$_4$, with current densities on the order of $1~\mathrm{mA/m}$. Notably, the injection-like photocurrent in RuF$_4$ reverses sign upon switching either the light helicity or the Néel vector. Our results extend the bulk photovoltaic effect to centrosymmetric solids and open new opportunities for electrical readout of altermagnetic order.

</details>


### [91] [Nanowire-Assisted Epitaxial Lateral Overgrowth of Planar GaN(0001) on Diamond(100)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26158)
*Brijmohan Paramanik,Mayur Shinde,Subhranshu Sekhar Sahu,Soumyadip Chatterjee,Prajwal Pandey,Anshul Jain,Himanjal Bhattacharyya,Alpesh Donga,Apurba Laha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究在金刚石(100)衬底上通过纳米线辅助的侧向外延生长(ELOG)技术制备GaN，以克服晶格与热失配导致的位错问题，并证明该方法能有效降低位错密度、恢复近体材料晶格参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN-on-diamond 异质结构在高功率、高频电子器件中前景广阔，但直接在金刚石(100)上外延生长因严重的晶格失配和热失配而产生高位错密度，制约其应用，因此需要一种能抑制位错传播并恢复近体材料晶格参数的生长策略。

Method: 采用等离子体辅助分子束外延(PAMBE)在金刚石(100)衬底上通过纳米线辅助的侧向外延生长(ELOG)制备纤锌矿GaN层。设置两个纳米线模板(NW100、NW600)和无纳米线参考样品(NW000)进行对比，并利用高分辨XRD、φ扫描、(0002)极图、倒易空间映射以及截面透射电镜(TEM)对晶体质量、位错密度、微应变和层序进行表征。

Result: NW100 样品具有最低位错密度(5.51×10⁻⁴ nm⁻²)、最低微应变(2.736×10⁻³)、最窄的(0002)反射，且c轴晶格参数(5.1821 Å)与体材料偏差仅0.06%；而NW000样品c轴收缩(5.1633 Å)、位错密度升高(6.26×10⁻⁴ nm⁻²)，与残余拉应力一致。截面TEM证实了金刚石/AlN/纳米线/ELOG/平面GaN的层序，并观察到穿透位错弯折为近横向轨迹，直接证明纳米线模板的缺陷过滤作用。

Conclusion: 纳米线辅助ELOG是抑制位错传播并恢复GaN-on-diamond异质外延近体材料晶格参数的有效策略，为器件集成提供了结构明确的平台。

Abstract: GaN-on-diamond heterostructures are promising for high-power and high-frequency electronics, however, direct epi- taxial growth on diamond (100) remains challenging due to severe lattice and thermal mismatches, which induce high threading dislocation densities. To overcome this challenge, we have used Plasma Assisted Molecular Beam Epitaxy (PAMBE) to grow wurtzite GaN layers on diamond(100) substrates using nanowire-assisted epitaxial lateral overgrowth (ELOG). Two nanowire templates (NW100, NW600) were benchmarked against a nanowire-free reference (NW000). High-resolution XRD, φ -scans, (0002) pole figures, and reciprocal space mapping show that NW100 attains the lowest dislocation density (5.51 x 10-4 nm-2) and microstrain (2.736 x 10-3), the narrowest (0002) reflection, and a c-axis lattice parameter (5.1821 Å) within 0.06% of the bulk value; NW000, in contrast, exhibits a contracted c-axis (5.1633 Å) and elevated dislocation density (6.26 x 10-4 nm-2), consistent with residual tensile stress. Cross-sectional TEM con- firms the intended diamond/AlN/nanowire/ELOG/planar-GaN layer sequence and shows threading dislocations bending into near-lateral trajectories, directly evidencing defect filtering by the nanowire template. Together, these results estab- lish nanowire-assisted ELOG as an effective strategy to suppress dislocation propagation and recover near-bulk lattice parameters in GaN-on-diamond heteroepitaxy, providing a structurally well-defined platform for device integration.

</details>


### [92] [Hidden piezo-Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26293)
*San-Dong Guo,Shi-Hao Zhang,Bingwen Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"隐藏压电霍尔效应"：在特定量子材料中，单轴应变可在各个扇区（sector）内分别诱导出非零的反常霍尔电导，但全局反常霍尔电导保持为零，即各扇区的贡献相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往对隐藏自旋极化的研究确立了量子材料中一大类"隐藏现象"。作者意在将隐藏物理推广到应变调控的输运领域，探索是否存在应变诱导的、仅在扇区层面显现而全局消失的霍尔响应。

Method: 针对 d 波隐藏交错磁体（hidden altermagnet），作者采用紧束缚模型并结合第一性原理计算，对实验上已合成的材料 RbV₂Te₂O 进行研究；通过施加单轴应变并旋转应变方向（从 x 轴到 y 轴），考察各扇区及全局的反常霍尔电导变化。

Result: 零应变时，整个系统及其扇区均无反常霍尔效应；施加单轴应变后，各扇区出现大小相等、符号相反的反常霍尔电导，而全局总和保持抵消为零。将应变方向从 x 轴旋转到 y 轴会反转各扇区 AHC 的符号，但依然不产生净霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 该工作将隐藏物理拓展至应变调控输运，并指出同一对称性机制还可能引发隐藏压电磁光、压电热霍尔、压电能斯特和压电埃廷斯豪森效应，同时将 RbV₂Te₂O 确定为实验验证的有力候选材料。

Abstract: Extensive studies of hidden spin polarization have established a broad class of hidden phenomena in quantum materials. Here, we introduce the hidden piezo-Hall effect, in which strain induces finite anomalous Hall conductivities (AHCs) in individual sectors while the global AHC remains zero. At zero strain, neither the full system nor its sectors exhibit an anomalous Hall effect (AHE). Under uniaxial strain, however, opposite sector AHCs emerge while their sum remains canceled. We establish this effect for a $d$-wave hidden altermagnet using a tight-binding model and first-principles calculations for the experimentally synthesized material $\mathrm{RbV_2Te_2O}$. Rotating the strain direction from the $x$ to the $y$ axis reverses the signs of the sector AHCs without generating a net Hall response. The same symmetry mechanism may also give rise to hidden piezomagneto-optical, piezo-thermal Hall, piezo-Nernst, and piezo-Ettingshausen effects. Our work extends hidden physics to strain-controlled transport and identifies $\mathrm{RbV_2Te_2O}$ as a promising candidate for experimental verification.

</details>


### [93] [Machine Learning-Assisted Discovery of Carbon-Related Defect Formation Mechanisms in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26301)
*Grace Huang,Christopher Fivecoat,Xun Xu,Mengen Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过微调机器学习原子间势（MLIP）研究金刚石中碳相关缺陷（自间隙子与空位）的迁移及缺陷复合体（双间隙、双空位）的形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石是固态自旋量子比特等功能缺陷的重要宿主材料。在辐照及随后的热退火过程中会产生空位和间隙子等碳相关缺陷，它们会迁移并形成新的缺陷复合体。作者旨在研究这一动力学驱动过程，并揭示中性电荷态下双间隙子和双空位的形成机制。

Method: 利用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算生成的小数据集，对通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）进行微调，从而研究缺陷迁移与复合过程。

Result: 得到单间隙子迁移势垒为1.84 eV；两个初始分离的单间隙子只需克服更低的势垒即可形成双间隙构型。在训练数据之外，MLIP还识别出另一种双间隙构型的形成路径以及双空位的逐步解离机制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了迁移辅助的缺陷复合体形成机制，并证明针对性的MLIP训练能够有效识别反应路径。

Abstract: Diamond is an important host material for functional defects such as solid-state spin qubits. During the defect creation by irradiation and subsequent thermal annealing, carbon-related defects, including vacancies and interstitials, are generated and can migrate to form new defect complexes. To investigate this kinetically driven process and reveal the formation mechanisms of the di-interstitials and di-vacancies in the neutral charge state, we fine-tuned a universal machine learning interatomic potential using a small dataset generated from density functional theory calculations. We obtained a barrier of 1.84 eV for mono-interstitial migration and found that two initially separated mono-interstitials only need to overcome a lower barrier to form di-interstitial configurations. Beyond the training data, the MLIP also identified the formation pathway for an additional di-interstitial configuration and a stepwise divacancy dissociation mechanism. These results reveal migration-assisted defect complex formation mechanisms and demonstrate how targeted MLIP training can identify reaction pathways.

</details>

*Markus Ströbele,Mykhailo Brytskyi,Carl P. Romao,Scott Kroeker,Hans-Jürgen Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前金属氮碳化合物中普遍存在 (NCN)2– 离子，但含 (CN3)5–、(C2N4)6– 或 (C3N6)6– 等离子的化合物制备仍很困难。作者旨在合成并表征含 (CN3)5– 离子的新化合物，拓展对氰胺基/氮碳负离子类化合物的认识。

Method: 以 LaF3、Na2(CN2) 与 Li3N 为原料，在 550 °C 开放体系下通过传统固相复分解反应合成；利用热分析研究其生成与分解条件；采用单晶 X 射线衍射精修晶体结构；报道了结构中两种晶体学不同 (CN3)5– 离子的中红外频率；并用 23Na、7Li、13C NMR 进一步确证化合物身份。

Result: 成功合成新化合物 La14.33(CN3)8N，其晶体结构与 Ce15(BN3)8N 同型；结构中存在两种晶体学上不同的 (CN3)5– 离子，红外与 NMR 数据均支持该化合物的组成与结构。

Conclusion: 证明了含 (CN3)5– 离子的稀土氮碳化合物可以通过固相复分解反应合成，La14.33(CN3)8N 的成功制备为其同类物种的探索和表征提供了范例，并揭示了其与 Ce15(BN3)8N 的结构关联。

Abstract: Metal nitridocarbonates containing the (NCN)2- ion are established for a multitude of metal atoms throughout the periodic table. The preparation of nitridocarbonate compounds containing a (CN3)5-, (C2N4)6-, or (C3N6)6- ion remains challenging, although corresponding species have been established. The new compound La14.33(CN3)8N was synthesized in a conventional solid-state metathesis reaction from a mixture of LaF3, Na2(CN2) and Li3N in an open system at 550 °C. The reaction conditions, including the formation and de-composition of La14.33(CN3)8N are analyzed on basis of thermoanalytic studies. The crystal structure of La14.33(CN3)8N was refined by single-crystal X-ray diffraction, and the mid-IR frequencies resulting from two crystallographically distinct (CN3)5- ions in the structure are reported. 23Na, 7Li and 13C NMR studies provide additional support for the identity of the compound. The crystal structure of La14.33(CN3)8N is isotypic to that of Ce15(BN3)8N.

</details>
