TL;DR: 本文利用先前建立的电荷态衰减形式理论，研究含替代氮(N_C)杂质和醚封端表面的金刚石样品中，单负电荷替代硼杂质(B⁻_C)对相对粒子布居分配的影响。作者证明给定电子给体与受体的电荷跃迁能级、相对布居和温度即可完全确定两者间的电荷转移时间尺度，并通过该框架推断出额外的B_C缺陷在调控与醚封端表面杂化的N_C的相对布居，其引起的局域费米能级移动与B_C一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含氮杂质和醚封端表面的金刚石样品中，实验测得的NV⁻中心与N_C之间的电荷转移时间尺度、温度及NV⁻相对布居无法仅由这两个缺陷自身解释，需要确定是否存在额外的缺陷在调节N_C的布居，并识别该缺陷的身份。

Method: 作者基于其早先建立的电荷态衰减形式理论，提出一个通用关系：给定任意两个缺陷（一个施体和一个受体），只要已知其电荷跃迁能级、相对布居和温度，即可完全确定两者间的电荷转移时间尺度。应用该公式，结合文献中的实验数据（NV⁻中心与醚封端表面杂化N_C之间的电荷转移时间尺度、温度及NV⁻相对布居），反推N_C杂化态相对布居的波动，并通过局域费米能级移动来推断额外缺陷的存在与种类。

Result: 研究发现需要引入一个额外的缺陷来调制与醚封端表面杂化的N_C的相对布居；该额外缺陷引起的局域费米能级移动与替代硼杂质(B_C)一致，故推断其为B_C。该结果使局域电离缺陷相对浓度的确定成为可能。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套基于电荷跃迁能级、相对布居与温度完全确定施体-受体对之间电荷转移时间尺度的框架，并据此论证在含氮和醚封端表面的金刚石中，替代硼杂质(B_C)在调控N_C杂化态的相对布居，从而能够确定局域电离缺陷的相对浓度。

Abstract: Leveraging our earlier formalism for charge-state decay [see R. Kuate Defo et al., 108 235208 (2023)], we investigate relative population partitioning in the presence of singly negatively charged substitutional boron impurities (B$^-_{\rm C}$) in diamond samples, where these diamond samples also contain substitutional nitrogen (N$_{\rm C }$) impurities and have ether-terminated surfaces. Effectively, given any two defects, one donor and one acceptor, such that their charge-transition levels, their relative populations, and the temperature are known, the timescale for charge transfer between the two defects is completely determined. We use this insight and experimental measurements for the timescale for charge transfer between NV$^-$ centers and N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond, as well as the temperature and the relative populations for the NV$^-$ centers, [see Z. Yuan et al., Phys. Rev. Res. 2, 033263 (2020)] to argue that an additional defect must be modulating the relative populations of N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond. The shifts in the local Fermi level for N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond are consistent with the additional defect being a substitutional boron impurity (B$_{\rm C}$). Ultimately, our results allow for the determination of local relative concentrations of ionized defects.

</details>


### [96] [Atomic-level study of dose-dependent lattice thermal conductivity degradation in irradiated tungsten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26502)
*Mary K Chessey,José Julio Gutiérrez Moreno,Sajjan Singh Mehta,Paolo Settembri,Arda Erbasan,Dominik Freinberger,Marina García Romero,Mervi J Mantsinen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合非平衡分子动力学(NEMD)与创建弛豫算法(CRA)模拟中子辐照钨的缺陷结构，并用机器学习(ML)建立缺陷特征与晶格热导率(κ)退化之间的定量关系，发现空位密度是控制热输运的主导因素，且在0.05 at.%临界空位阈值处出现台阶式退化转变，声子散射完全饱和，相对热导率最大降幅约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨(W)因高导热、高熔点、低溅射产额和低氚吸收成为未来聚变堆等离子体面材料(PFC)的首选，但中子辐照引入的复杂纳米结构缺陷会严重劣化其关键性能。现有研究缺乏对辐照剂量、缺陷类型/分布与热导率退化之间定量关系的系统认识，亟需一种能关联结构与热输运的预测框架。

Method: 采用非平衡分子动力学(NEMD)模拟，结合创建弛豫算法(CRA)，在最高1.8 cdpa的不同辐照剂量下生成包含空位和自间隙原子(SIA)团簇的多样化缺陷结构；通过将空间分辨的温度梯度与局部缺陷浓度相关联，并用机器学习(ML)模型建立缺陷特征与热导率退化的定量关系，最终构建多缺陷预测映射。

Result: 机器学习分析确定空位密度是控制热输运的主导结构因素；热导率随缺陷密度增加呈显著的非线性抑制；预测映射显示在0.05 at.%临界空位阈值处发生台阶式退化转变，超过该阈值后声子散射完全饱和，相对晶格热导率最大退化被限制在约30%。

Conclusion: 该整合模拟与机器学习的框架为量化辐照聚变材料中结构-热导率关系提供了强有力的预测工具，揭示了空位密度作为主导因子及其临界阈值效应，可指导未来PFC材料的辐照性能评估与设计。

Abstract: Plasma-facing components (PFCs) in future fusion reactors must withstand extreme environments, making tungsten (W) a prime candidate material due to its high thermal conductivity, high melting temperature, low sputtering yield, low tritium absorption. However, neutron irradiation introduces complex nanostructural defects that severely degrade these critical properties. In this work, we use Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics (NEMD) simulations coupled with the Creation Relaxation Algorithm (CRA) to investigate the lattice thermal conductivity (kappa) of W across a wide range of irradiation doses. Systems treated with different doses up to 1.8 canonical displacements per atom (cdpa) were simulated to generate and study diverse defect structures, including vacancies and self-interstitial atom (SIA) clusters. By correlating spatially-resolved temperature gradients with local defect concentrations, we show that even small changes in defect type and distribution significantly affect thermal transport. We employ Machine Learning (ML) models to develop a quantitative relationship between the defect features and the resulting thermal conductivity degradation. The ML analysis identifies vacancy density as the dominant structural factor controlling thermal transport. We found a significant, nonlinear suppression of kappa with increasing defect density. Predictive multi-defect mapping built upon our ML model resolved a step-like degradation transition at a critical vacancy threshold of 0.05 at.%, beyond which phonon scattering completely saturated and capped the maximum relative lattice thermal conductivity degradation at approximately 30 at.%. This integrated simulation and ML approach provides a powerful, predictive framework for quantifying the complex relationship between structure and thermal conductivity in irradiated fusion materials.

</details>


### [97] [Stabilization of Ferroelectric Domains via Interfacial Strain in HoFeO3- BaTiO3 Nanofiber Heterostructures for Enhanced Hybrid Energy Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26611)
*Smita Chaturvedi,Swathi Krishna,Shivshankar Jokare,Supriya Sahoo,Vikash Kushwaha,Priyank Shyam,Mandar Shirolkar,R. Boomishankar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种 HoFeO3-BaTiO3（HFO-BTO）氧化物纳米纤维异质结构，通过交替溶胶-凝胶静电纺丝制备，界面处晶格畸变稳定了铁电畴，结合压电与摩擦电协同输出，使封装于 PDMS 的器件功率密度达到 24.31 μW/cm³，显著优于单一组分器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压电纳米发电机（PENGs）在自供能机械能收集方面很有前景，但其输出常受去极化场、电荷泄漏以及应力向活性偶极子传递效率低等问题的制约，尤其在聚合物主导的复合材料中更为突出。因此需要探索能改善界面应力传递和极化稳定性的无铅异质结构材料体系。

Method: 采用交替溶胶-凝胶静电纺丝法制备 HFO-BTO 纳米纤维异质结构，使正交相 HoFeO3（Pbnm）与四方相 BaTiO3（P4mm）紧密接触共存；利用 Rietveld 精修 XRD、高分辨透射电镜和拉曼光谱表征界面晶格畸变；通过极化-电场回线测量极化与开关电流行为；将纳米纤维封装于 PDMS 中，在循环冲击激励下测试开路电压和功率密度，并与单独制备的 BaTiO3、HoFeO3 器件对比。

Result: 界面处存在晶格畸变；异质结构在零场下的剩余极化与 BaTiO3 相当（16.39 vs 15.80 μC/cm²），而其开关电流峰值移动到更高电场（约 7 vs 约 3.5 kV/cm），表明应变稳定了铁电畴。器件输出为压电与摩擦电的混合响应，开路电压峰值约 +6 V 和 -8 V，在 4.7 MΩ 负载下功率密度为 24.31 μW/cm³，分别是同样工艺 BaTiO3 器件（10.00 μW/cm³）的 2.4 倍和 HoFeO3 器件（2.78 μW/cm³）的 8.7 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明界面驱动的晶格畸变与机电性能提升直接相关，全氧化物纳米纤维异质结构是实现无铅能量收集的一种有前景的途径。

Abstract: Piezoelectric nanogenerators (PENGs) are an attractive platform for self-powered mechanical-energy harvesting, but their output is often limited by depolarization fields, charge leakage, and inefficient stress transfer to the active dipoles, particularly in polymer-dominated composites. Here we report HoFeO3-BaTiO3 (HFO-BTO) nanofiber heterostructures prepared by alternating sol-gel electrospinning, in which orthorhombic HoFeO3 (Pbnm) and tetragonal BaTiO3 (P4mm) coexist in intimate contact. Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, high-resolution transmission electron microscopy, and Raman spectroscopy indicate interfacial lattice distortion. Polarization-electric field loops show that the heterostructure retains a polarization comparable to that of BaTiO3 (16.39 vs 15.80 microcoulombs per square centimeter at zero field) while its switching-current maximum moves to a higher field (about 7 vs about 3.5 kilovolts per centimeter), consistent with strain-stabilized ferroelectric domains. When the nanofibers are encapsulated in polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) and excited under cyclic impact, the device produces a hybrid output: the piezoelectric response of the strained BaTiO3 is supplemented by a triboelectric contribution from contact electrification. The HFO-BTO device delivers open-circuit voltage peaks of about +6 volts and -8 volts and a power density of 24.31 microwatts per cubic centimeter at a 4.7 megaohm load. This is 2.4 times the value for an identically fabricated BaTiO3 device (10.00 microwatts per cubic centimeter) and 8.7 times that of the HoFeO3 device (2.78 microwatts per cubic centimeter). These results link interface-driven lattice distortion to improved electromechanical output and identify all-oxide nanofiber heterostructures as a promising route to lead-free energy harvesting.

</details>


### [98] [Vacancy and Helium-Cluster Thermomigration in Tungsten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26667)
*Sanad Alturk,Enrique Martinez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过原子尺度跳跃统计框架结合非平衡分子动力学模拟，量化了体心立方钨中单空位与He1–He4团簇在热梯度下的热迁移行为，发现所有缺陷均向热区净迁移，且热迁移无法仅由团簇尺寸、扩散率或迁移能推断，并给出了可用于更大尺度模型的热输运系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨等离子体面材料中存在强热梯度，会偏置辐照缺陷的迁移，从而改变其再分布、相互作用与滞留行为。现有氢的热迁移跳跃统计框架需要扩展到空位与氦团簇，以提供聚变相关热梯度下缺陷特异性热输运参数。

Method: 扩展此前的原子跳跃统计框架：通过多个热梯度下的非平衡分子动力学模拟确定方向跳跃比，再利用几何推导的投影跳跃长度将拟合的方向偏置系数转换为热输运系数 Q*=αkT^2；同时用1000–1600 K的恒温模拟获得有效迁移能，并用长分子动力学轨迹验证He3浓度分布。

Result: 所有缺陷的α均为负值，对应净向热区迁移。He4响应最大（α=-2.2457×10^-2 K^-1），空位紧随其后，He3响应最弱。恒温模拟显示He3迁移率最低、有效迁移能最大（0.271 eV），而He4迁移率高于He3且有效迁移能最小（0.087 eV）。跳跃统计预测的He3浓度分布与长轨迹结果吻合，独立验证了方法。

Conclusion: 热迁移不能仅由团簇尺寸、扩散系数或迁移能推断，本文提供了缺陷特异性的热输运参数，可用于聚变相关热梯度下钨的高尺度模型建模。

Abstract: Strong thermal gradients in tungsten plasma-facing components can bias the migration of irradiation-induced defects and thereby alter their redistribution, interaction, and retention. Here, we extend an atomistic jump-statistics framework previously developed for hydrogen to quantify the thermomigration of a single vacancy and interstitial He$_1$--He$_4$ clusters in body-centered-cubic tungsten. Non-equilibrium molecular-dynamics simulations performed over several thermal gradients are used to determine the directional jump ratio, while geometrically derived projected jump lengths convert the fitted directional-bias coefficient into the heat-of-transport coefficient $Q^*=αkT^2$, where $k$ is the Boltzmann constant, $T$ the temperature, and $α$ a constant. All investigated defects exhibit negative values of $α$, corresponding to net migration toward the hot region. He$_4$ has the largest response, $α=-2.2457\times10^{-2}$ K$^{-1}$, followed closely by the vacancy, whereas He$_3$ exhibits the weakest response despite its intermediate cluster size. Independent constant-temperature simulations over 1000--1600 K show that He$_3$ is also the least mobile cluster and has the largest effective migration energy, $E_m^{\mathrm{eff}}=0.271$ eV. In contrast, He$_4$ is more mobile than He$_3$ and has the smallest effective migration energy, 0.087 eV. Agreement between the He$_3$ concentration profile predicted from the jump statistics and that obtained from a long molecular-dynamics trajectory provides an independent validation of the method. These results show that thermomigration cannot be inferred from cluster size, diffusivity, or migration energy alone and provide defect-specific heat-of-transport parameters for higher-length-scale models of tungsten under fusion-relevant thermal gradients.

</details>

### [99] [Two quantum spin liquid states coexisting in the kagome antiferromagnet herbertsmithite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24065)
*Arthur C. Campello,Shengtao Jiang,Wei He,Jiajia Wen,Daniel M. Pajerowski,Steven A. Kivelson,Hong-Chen Jiang,Young S. Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过对赫伯特斯密特石单晶的高分辨非弹性中子散射与大规模DMRG/dynamical DMRG模拟的对比，作者验证了自旋哈密顿量，并发现其基态可理解为两个近似简并的、有能隙的量子自旋液体态的共存混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赫伯特斯密特石(ZnCu₃(OH)₆Cl₂)是被研究最多的量子自旋液体候选材料之一，但其基态的确切性质至今仍未解决。作者旨在通过实验与大规模数值模拟的定量对照，确定自旋哈密顿量并揭示基态的基本性质。

Method: 对赫伯特斯密特石单晶(ZnₓCu₄₋ₓ(OD)₆Cl₂, x≈0.85)进行高分辨非弹性中子散射测量，并将实验测得的海森堡模型能量密度与大规模密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)及动力学DMRG(DDMRG)模拟结果进行基准对比。

Result: 实测自旋关联确定的能量密度与模拟结果高度吻合，验证了哈密顿量。DMRG揭示出两个近乎简并的量子自旋液体态：两者均保持平移对称性，但一个保留晶格点群对称性，另一个则形成破坏点群对称性的各向异性自旋关联。这两个态具有不同的自旋结构因子，中子谱与二者的混合态达成高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究建立了中子散射与数值模拟之间的定量对应关系，表明赫伯特斯密特石处于两个近邻有能隙量子自旋液体态共存的体系中。

Abstract: Herbertsmithite (ZnCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Cl$_2$) is one of the most extensively studied quantum spin liquid (QSL) candidates, yet the precise nature of its ground state remains unresolved. Here, we compare the results of high-resolution inelastic neutron scattering on single crystals of herbertsmithite (Zn$_\mathrm{x}$Cu$_\mathrm{4-x}$(OD)$_6$Cl$_2$, $x\simeq 0.85$) with large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and dynamical DMRG simulations to benchmark the spin Hamiltonian and uncover fundamental properties of the ground state. The measured spin correlations determine an energy density for the Heisenberg model that closely matches simulations, an essential verification of the Hamiltonian. In parallel, DMRG simulations reveal two nearly degenerate QSL states. Both preserve translational symmetry, but one retains the lattice point-group symmetries whereas the other develops anisotropic spin correlations that break point-group symmetry. These two QSLs have distinct spin structure factors, and, remarkably, close agreement with the neutron spectra is achieved with an admixture of the two states. Our results establish a quantitative correspondence between neutron scattering and simulations, and suggest that herbertsmithite realizes a regime in which two proximate gapped QSL states coexist.

</details>


### [100] [Monolayer van der Waals kagome lattice for high-temperature flat-band physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24071)
*Hyungkun Bong,Choongjae Won,Seongyong Lee,Soomin Oh,Phuong X. Nguyen,Raghav Chaturvedi,Sehyun Oh,Jiyong Han,Paul Malinowski,Chad Mowers,Jimin Kim,Sae Hee Ryu,Hyunjong Lee,Jinhyeok Choi,Sangsu Yer,Chris Jozwiak,Aaron Bostwick,Eli Rotenberg,Craig M. Polley,Matthew D. Watson,Timur K. Kim,Heejun Yang,Jieun Lee,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Jae-Hoon Park,Gil Young Cho,Kyle M. Shen,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次在二维单层极限下实现了理想的kagome晶格，利用新型范德华家族A2T3X4，通过nano-ARPES观测到费米能级附近的孤立kagome平带，并可经门电压调控到费米能级，构建了比莫尔人工平带系统电子密度高220倍以上的内禀二维平带平台，为高温分数量子反常霍尔态等平带物理研究开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome晶格材料蕴含拓扑、关联与阻挫交织的丰富量子现象，但此前研究多局限于三维块体晶体；实现真正的二维kagome系统是长期目标，以解锁三维系统中无法企及的新物理与工程途径。同时，现有莫尔人工平带系统存在平带电子密度低的瓶颈，亟需更高密度的内禀二维平带平台以提升平带关联现象的特征温度。

Method: 本文采用新型范德华kagome家族A2T3X4（A=K,Rb,Cs；T=Ni,Pd,Pt；X=S,Se），构建首个理想的二维单层kagome晶格。利用纳米聚焦角分辨光电子能谱（nano-ARPES）直接表征A2T3X4从体材料到单层极限的电子结构；通过二维A2T3X4的门电压工程将kagome平带调控至费米能级，实现部分填充的平带物理；并结合多体计算支持高温平带现象的理论预期。

Result: 在单层极限下直接观测到位于费米能级下方、孤立的kagome衍生平带；经门电压调控可将该平带调至费米能级，直接触及部分填充平带物理，建立了一个性质全新的二维平带平台。相比现有莫尔人工平带系统，该内禀平带的电子密度提高了220倍以上，进入全新的密度与能区范围，为将分数量子反常霍尔态等平带现象提升到远高于当前的温度提供了明确途径。

Conclusion: 首个理想二维单层kagome晶格的实现，结合nano-ARPES表征与门电压调控，建立了一个电子密度比莫尔系统高220倍以上的内禀二维平带平台，开辟了全新的密度与能域，使关联平带现象（如分数量子反常霍尔态）有望被推至更高温度，正如理论长期设想并由多体计算所支持。

Abstract: Kagome lattice materials host a remarkably rich landscape of novel quantum phenomena, all arising from the intrinsic interplay of topology, correlation, and frustration. While previous studies have been mostly limited to bulk three-dimensional (3D) kagome crystals, the realization of a true two-dimensional (2D) kagome system has remained a long-standing goal, to unlock fundamentally new physics and engineering pathways inaccessible in 3D systems. In this work, we establish the first ideal 2D monolayer kagome lattices by leveraging a novel and versatile van der Waals (vdW) kagome family, A2T3X4 (A = K, Rb, Cs; T = Ni, Pd, Pt; X = S, Se). Our nano-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (nano-ARPES) directly characterizes the electronic structure of A2T3X4 down to the monolayer limit, notably revealing an isolated kagome-derived flat band just below the Fermi level. Via gate-engineering of 2D A2T3X4, the kagome flat band can be further tuned to the Fermi level, allowing direct access to the partially filled flat band physics and establishing a qualitatively new 2D flat band platform. Compared to existing moire-based artificial flat band systems, this vdW kagome-based intrinsic flat band system realizes more than 220 times higher flat band electron density, opening an entirely new density and energy regime for exploring 2D flat band physics. Harnessing this high-electron-density flat band in 2D vdW kagome system provides a clear pathway to scale emergent flat band phenomena - such as fractional quantum anomalous Hall states - to far higher temperatures than currently achievable, as long-envisioned in theory and further supported by our many-body calculations.

</details>


### [101] [Kohler's Rule Holds in the Strange Metallic Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24208)
*Rebekah M. Jin,Emily Z. Zhang,Jixun K. Ding,Brian Moritz,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用行列式量子蒙特卡洛研究空穴掺杂的Hubbard-Hofstadter模型磁阻，发现尽管存在超越Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限的线性电阻率，Kohler定律在很宽相互作用强度和掺杂范围内意外成立，其出现与局域单重态关联的建立有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强关联奇异金属中观测到违反Kohler定律的反常磁输运现象，但违反的确切成因尚不清楚。作者希望厘清强关联与奇异金属性本身是否必然导致反常磁阻。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）计算空穴掺杂Hubbard-Hofstadter模型的磁阻，系统考察不同在位相互作用强度和空穴掺杂浓度下的磁输运行为，并结合双占据率和比热识别特征温度T*。

Result: 尽管体系呈现超越Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限的T线性电阻率且名义上缺乏明确定义的准粒子，Kohler定律在很宽的在位相互作用与空穴掺杂范围内出人意料地成立。Kohler标度出现在由双占据率和比热确定的交叉温度T*≈t以下，与格点间局域单重态关联的出现吻合。

Conclusion: 强关联与奇异金属性本身并不必然产生反常磁阻；该区域的Kohler标度可能反映了一种更一般的多体量子相干形式。

Abstract: Unconventional magnetotransport violating Kohler's rule has been reported in many strongly correlated strange metals, but the exact origin of these violations is yet unknown. To investigate this question, we use determinant quantum Monte Carlo to compute the magnetoresistance of the hole-doped Hubbard-Hofstadter model. Despite $T$-linear resistivity exceeding the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit and the nominal absence of well-defined quasiparticles, we find Kohler's rule unexpectedly holds across a wide range of on-site interaction strengths and hole dopings. In particular, Kohler emerges below a crossover temperature $T^\star\approx t$ identified from the double occupancy and specific heat, coinciding with the onset of local singlet correlations between lattice sites. We thus propose that Kohler scaling in this regime reflects a more general form of many-body quantum coherence. Our results show that strong correlations and strange metallicity do not themselves generically produce anomalous magnetoresistance.

</details>


### [102] [Theory of High-Temperature Fractional Chern Phases in Monolayer van der Waals Kagome Materials A$_2$T$_3$X$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24633)
*Soomin Oh,Jiyong Han,Jinhyeok Choi,Donghae Seo,Mingu Kang,Gil Young Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从理论上指出，新合成的范德华 kagome 家族 A₂T₃X₄ 单层可作为在零磁场中实现高温分数量子霍尔（分数陈绝缘体）的平台，其本征拓扑平带与强库仑相互作用相结合，并可通过栅压调控载流子密度与层间组装；精确对角化显示 Cs₂Pd₃S₄ 中存在约 26 meV 的分数陈绝缘体多体能隙，双层堆叠还可实现分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分数陈绝缘体（FCI）仅能在摩尔超晶格材料中实现，超晶格的长周期削弱了电子相互作用，使这些相被限制在低温下。早期理论设想中，原子尺度晶格上的拓扑平带有望在更高温度维持拓扑序，但长期缺乏合适的材料。本文旨在寻找一个结合原子尺度拓扑平带、强相互作用和可调的实验平台。

Method: 作者对近年合成的范德华 kagome 材料家族 A₂T₃X₄ 单层进行研究：基于密度泛函理论（DFT）导出有效模型，并采用精确对角化（允许最多一个带间电子-空穴激发的带混合）计算其多体性质；同时通过栅极调控载流子密度和层间（双层）组装——包括反平行磁化排布——来探索分数陈绝缘体、反常复合费米液体、电荷有序相等多种相，并评估双层分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体。

Result: 研究表明 A₂T₃X₄ 单层在宽填充范围内呈现铁磁性，支撑多样的拓扑或关联电子相；特别是在 Cs₂Pd₃S₄ 中，分数陈绝缘体在允许最多一个带间电子-空穴激发的最大可计算系统中表现出约 26 meV 的多体能隙。双层结构在反平行磁化下，于现实层间距即可形成分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一个实验上可获取的材料家族，化学组分、载流子密度与堆叠方式为在更高能量尺度下实现分数化及调控拓扑序提供了互补路径，为在零磁场、较高温度下研究分数陈绝缘体及分数量子自旋霍尔效应开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators bring the fractional quantum Hall effect, with its anyonic excitations, to zero magnetic field. So far they have been realized in moiré materials, where the long superlattice period weakens electronic interactions and confines these phases to cryogenic temperatures. Early proposals instead envisioned topological flat bands on atomic-scale lattices, where fractionalization could persist to far higher temperatures, but a suitable material has been lacking. Here we theoretically identify monolayers of the recently synthesized van der Waals kagome family $\mathrm{A}_2\mathrm{T}_3\mathrm{X}_4$ as such a platform, combining intrinsic topological flat bands and strong Coulomb interactions with gate-tunable carrier density and layer assembly. Exact diagonalization of models derived from density functional theory reveals ferromagnetism over broad filling ranges, supporting diverse fractional Chern insulators, anomalous composite Fermi liquids, and charge-ordered phases. In particular, in $\mathrm{Cs}_2\mathrm{Pd}_3\mathrm{S}_4$, the fractional Chern insulator exhibits a many-body gap of approximately 26 meV in the largest system accessible with band mixing allowing at most one interband electron-hole excitation. Layer assembly further provides a natural route to fractional quantum spin Hall insulators: bilayers with antiparallel magnetizations support this phase at realistic separations. These results identify an experimentally accessible material family in which chemical composition, carrier density, and stacking offer complementary routes to fractionalization at enhanced energy scales and control over topological order.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨弱各向异性的偶极-交换反铁磁体 LiGdF₄ 的磁有序行为与场致相变，揭示交换相互作用与偶极相互作用竞争这一特殊磁挫败对有序温度和相图的影响。

Method: 对 LiGdF₄ 单晶样品进行低温磁共振测量；在 T_N=0.19(1) K 以下观测共振谱随磁有序相形成的演化；沿晶体四重轴施加外磁场，探测场致相变序列；在低场磁相及饱和场以上区域记录高分辨磁共振谱。

Result: 在 T_N=0.19(1) K 以下观测到由有序相形成引起的共振谱转变；沿四重轴施场时检测到一系列场致相变；在其中一个低场磁相及饱和场以上均观测到良好分辨的磁共振谱。

Conclusion: 交换与偶极相互作用的竞争构成一种特殊类型的磁挫败，可解释该体系的有序延迟现象和复杂的磁相图。

Abstract: We report the results of low-temperature magnetic resonance measurements performed on a single-crystal sample of a weakly anisotropic dipolar-exchange antiferromagnet LiGdF$_4$. The transformation of a resonance spectrum due to the formation of the ordered phase is observed at temperatures below $T_N=0.19(1)$ K. A sequence of field-induced phase transitions is detected for the external magnetic field applied along the fourfold axis of the crystal. A well-resolved magnetic resonance spectrum is observed in one of the low-field magnetic phases as well as above the saturation field. Competing exchange and dipolar interactions, as a special type of magnetic frustration, are suggested to account for the delayed ordering and complex magnetic phase diagram of the system.

</details>


### [104] [Interaction-driven competing instabilities in two-dimensional checkerboard quadratic band crossing semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25046)
*Jia-Hao Wen,Wen-Hao Bian,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动量壳重整化群，系统研究了自旋1/2棋盘晶格二次能带交叉点（QBCP）半金属在全部16种边际短程费米子-费米子相互作用下的低能物理，发现系统由一系列固定点支配，主导不稳定相包括向列自旋向列（NSN）、量子反常霍尔（QAH）、手性超导（SC₁）和量子自旋霍尔（QSH）序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维QBCP半金属是研究相互作用驱动量子临界的理想平台，但此前研究大多只考虑简化的相互作用模型，完整费米子-费米子相互作用的作用仍未被探索。

Method: 采用动量壳重整化群（RG）方法，引入自旋1/2棋盘晶格QBCP半金属的全部16种边际短程费米子-费米子相互作用，推导耦合流方程；通过引入源项并计算磁化率来识别主导与次主导不稳定相。

Result: 低能物理由一系列固定点支配：视初始条件不同，系统可呈现单一稳定固定点、若干离散固定点，或由少数变量参数化的三组连续固定点。主导相为NSN、QAH、SC₁和QSH序。非对角相互作用产生此前简化模型中缺失的序；连续固定点群使主导不稳定相可被系统调控；此外还出现依赖轨迹的不稳定性，即完整RG流而非仅固定点决定主导态。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维QBCP半金属及相关电子体系的低能量子临界行为和相不稳定性提供了全面的理论理解，揭示了完整相互作用带来的新物理特征。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quadratic band crossing point (QBCP) semimetals serve as a fertile platform for interaction-driven quantum criticality, nevertheless, the role of the complete fermion-fermion interactions remains largely unexplored. To address this, we systematically investigate the low-energy physics of a spin-1/2 checkerboard-lattice QBCP semimetal by considering all 16 marginally short-range fermion-fermion interactions. Using the momentum-shell renormalization group, we derive the coupled flow equations, which reveal that the low-energy physics is governed by a series of fixed points. Depending on the initial conditions, the system may exhibit a single stable fixed point, several discrete fixed points, or three continuous groups of fixed points parameterized by a few variables. By introducing source terms and evaluating susceptibilities, we identify the dominant and subdominant instabilities. The leading phases include nematic spin-nematic (NSN), quantum anomalous Hall (QAH), chiral superconducting ($\mathrm{SC}_1$), and quantum spin Hall (QSH) orders. Moreover, several features distinguish our findings from previous simplified models. Off-diagonal interactions generate terms that promote orders absent in such models, the continuous groups allow systematic tuning of the leading instability, and trajectory-dependent instabilities emerge where the full RG flow, rather than the fixed point alone, determines the dominant state. These results offer a comprehensive theoretical understanding of low-energy quantum critical behaviors and phase instabilities in two-dimensional QBCP semimetals and related electron systems.

</details>


### [105] [Categorical classification of non-Fermi liquids in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25713)
*Shubham Kukreja,Dawson M. Willerton,Sung-Sik Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过配对通道的四点函数，对2+1维非费米液体进行了非微扰分类，在固定无量纲耦合、大费米动量极限下将其划分为七个超普适类，每一类在温度与裸四费米子耦合空间中具有独特的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非费米液体的定义性特征（谱函数在费米面上缺乏准粒子峰）及其二点、三点顶角函数在许多非费米液体中定性相同，导致难以对其进行正面的、超越负面定义（negation）的分类。作者旨在寻找能够积极区分不同类型非费米液体的信息。

Method: 作者指出配对通道的四点函数包含丰富信息，可正面区分不同非费米液体；采用广义不动点概念来刻画依赖尺度的普适配对相互作用，并利用运动学约束（kinematic constraints）进行非微扰分析。在固定无量纲耦合、大费米动量极限下，对金属与长波长临界涨落耦合的系统进行分类。

Result: 2+1维非费米液体被分类为七个超普适类（superuniversality classes）；每一类在温度与裸四费米子耦合的参数空间中展现出独特的相图。该结果将早期的微扰分类推广到强相互作用的非费米液体。

Conclusion: 通过利用配对通道四点函数与广义不动点方法，可以实现对强相互作用非费米液体的非微扰分类，为该领域的正面分类提供了新框架，并将先前微扰结果推广至强耦合情形。

Abstract: A defining feature of non-Fermi liquids is a lack of the quasiparticle peak on the Fermi surface in the spectral function, which is usually realized by coupling a finite density of fermions with critical collective fluctuations. However, these features of the two- and three-point vertex functions are qualitatively the same across many non-Fermi liquids, which has made it difficult to classify them beyond the definition by negation. We point out that the four-point function in the pairing channel contains rich information that positively differentiates distinct types of non-Fermi liquids. Using the generalized notion of fixed points that captures scale-dependent universal pairing interaction, we provide a non-perturbative classification of 2+1-dimensional non-Fermi liquids. In the large Fermi momentum limit with fixed dimensionless couplings, metals coupled with long-wavelength critical fluctuations are classified into seven superuniversality classes. Each of them exhibits a unique phase diagram in the space of temperature and bare four-fermion coupling. The present work generalizes the earlier perturbative classification to strongly interacting non-Fermi liquids by leveraging kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [106] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions and deconfined criticality in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25081)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出SrCu₂(BO₃)₂中此前被忽略的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya(DM)相互作用不会破坏其去禁闭临界性：规范单重态自旋子质量被时间反演与自旋轨道锁定滑移对称性禁止到所有阶，DM仅在极低温下扭曲自旋子并重构节点，材料仍是去禁闭临界性的理想平台，且可通过NMR加以检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrCu₂(BO₃)₂被视作去禁闭临界性的首要候选材料，但现有对其临界区的理论都忽略了Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya(DM)相互作用，而DM相互作用会使kagome狄拉克自旋液体产生能隙。因此需要厘清DM相互作用是否以及如何影响该材料的去禁闭临界性。

Method: 作者将DM相互作用纳入SU(2)规范理论框架，通过对称性分析证明：时间反演对称性与自旋轨道锁定的滑移(glide)对称性共同禁止任何规范单重态自旋子质量出现到所有微扰阶。进而区分面内与面外DM项的影响——面内DM仅使自旋子发生扭曲(twist)并在0.03–0.17K以下重构狄拉克节点，面外DM则是不相干的(irrelevant)，且SO(5)各向异性仅在二阶才出现。

Result: 结果显示，规范单重态自旋子质量被对称性严格禁止到所有阶；面内DM只导致节点在极低温(0.03–0.17K以下)被重构，面外DM不相关，SO(5)各向异性仅为二阶效应。因此DM相互作用不会破坏该材料的去禁闭临界性。NMR可以检验这一结论：施加[100]方向磁场会诱导出Néel磁矩，而[001]方向磁场则不会。

Conclusion: SrCu₂(BO₃)₂在计入DM相互作用后仍是研究去禁闭临界性的有效平台，此前理论对DM项的忽略并不致命。该预言可通过NMR实验进行验证，依据是[100]磁场诱导Néel磁矩而[001]磁场不诱导这一各向异性响应。

Abstract: SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ is the leading candidate for deconfined criticality, yet theories of its critical regime omit the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions, which gap the kagome Dirac spin liquid. Adding them to the SU(2) gauge theory, we show that time reversal and a spin-orbit-locked glide forbid any gauge-singlet spinon mass to all orders. In-plane DM only twists the spinons, reconstructing the nodes below $0.03$-$0.17$ K, while out-of-plane DM is irrelevant and SO(5) anisotropy arises only at second order. The material thus remains a platform for deconfined criticality, which NMR can test, since a $[100]$ field induces a Néel moment and a $[001]$ field does not.

</details>


### [107] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions in the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25082)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统分析SrCu₂(BO₃)₂中Shastry-Sutherland狄拉克自旋液体SU(2)规范理论的费米子双线性算符，在1/N领头阶和单圈水平处理Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya(DM)相互作用，研究了自旋子能否被打开能隙、能流与电流算符的标度行为、DM诱导的Yukawa耦合、临界压力下狄拉克锥重构为d波二次带接触及其低能流，以及外磁场方向选择规则，并通过¹¹B NMR可检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临界压力下，SrCu₂(BO₃)₂的实验样品在零场下于约1 K以上有序化，与狄拉克自旋液体图像存在张力；需要厘清DM相互作用、无序和外磁场对自旋液体临界点(尤其SO(5)点)的稳定性与对称性选择规则的影响，从而判定该材料是否可实现无质量狄拉克自旋液体相并预测可观测的实验信号。

Method: 在自旋-轨道锁定空间群下对Shastry-Sutherland狄拉克自旋液体SU(2)规范理论的费米子双线性进行对称性分类；将DM相互作用在1/N领头阶与单圈处理；由对称性证明局部规范单态费米子质量在各阶均被禁戒；分析面内/面外耦合产生的自旋流(维度严格为2)与flavored spin-2流的相关性；计算DM诱导的Yukawa耦合占比；在临界压力二维样品中研究狄拉克锥重构为手征保护的d波二次带接触，并对其唯一规范单态能隙道(两个价键分量与标量手征)做单圈流，由带接触屏蔽规范场，考察流向价键序或无能隙手征Z₂自旋液体的条件；并分析无序随机场耦合与外磁场[100]/[001]方向的选择规则。

Result: 线性阶DM既不能打开自旋子能隙，也不能在T*以上破坏SO(5)点；对称性在任意阶禁戒所有局部规范单态费米子质量。面内耦合仅诱导维度严格为2的守恒自旋流，给出T*=4λ≈0.03–0.17 K；面外耦合只诱导flavored spin-2流，在幺正临界点无关。DM诱导的Yukawa耦合不到限制赝临界窗口耦合的2%。零场下实测样品在1 K以上(≥T*)有序。低于T*，临界压力二维样品中狄拉克锥重构为手征保护的d波二次带接触，唯一规范单态能隙道为两个价键分量和标量手征；带接触屏蔽规范场后单圈流终止于T**~(0.03–0.1)λ，对于材料实际交换和多数随机耦合流向含价键序的射线，否则流向有能隙手征Z₂自旋液体。两种plaquette图案间的DM偏置至多约10⁻⁴量级，方向在O(1/N)未固定；非磁淬灭无序将相关随机场耦合到plaquette二重态而不耦合到手征。沿[100]的场诱导正比于场的(π,π) Néel矩，沿[001]的场则不诱导；该选择规则在二聚体相亦成立，可通过加压¹¹B NMR检验。

Conclusion: 该理论框架表明，在SrCu₂(BO₃)₂的临界压力附近，无质量狄拉克自旋液体虽然受对称性保护免于费米子质量，但在T*~0.03–0.17 K以下会经单圈流向价键序或手征Z₂自旋液体等破缺/能隙态；DM相互作用的偏置极其微弱，外磁场与无序以高度不对称的方式耦合不同序参量，提供了可经¹¹B NMR和磁场选择规则检验的明确预言。

Abstract: We classify the fermion bilinears of the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids under the spin-orbit-locked space group and treat the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions of SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ at leading order in $1/N$ and at one loop. At linear order DM can neither gap the spinons nor destabilize the SO(5) point above a scale $T^*$. Symmetry forbids every local gauge-singlet fermion mass at any order. Among the singlet bilinears, the in-plane couplings induce only a conserved spin current of dimension exactly 2, which sets $T^*=4λ\simeq0.03$-$0.17$ K, and the out-of-plane coupling only a flavored spin-2 current, irrelevant at a unitary critical point. DM-induced Yukawa couplings start below $2\%$ of the one that bounds the pseudocritical window. In zero field, measured samples order above $1$ K, at or above $T^*$. Below $T^*$, in a two-dimensional sample at the critical pressure, the Dirac cones reconstruct into a chirally protected $d$-wave quadratic band touching whose only gauge-singlet gap channels are the two valence-bond components and the scalar chirality. A one-loop flow, with the gauge field screened by the band touching, ends at $T^{**}\sim(0.03$-$0.1)λ$ on a ray containing valence-bond order for the material's exchange and for most random couplings, and otherwise on a gapped chiral $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquid. The DM bias between the two plaquette patterns is at most of order $10^{-4}$, with a direction not fixed at $O(1/N)$, whereas non-magnetic quenched disorder couples a relevant random field to the plaquette doublet and none to the chirality. A field along $[100]$ induces a $(π,π)$ Néel moment linear in the field; a field along $[001]$ does not. This selection rule also holds in the dimer phase and is testable by $^{11}$B NMR under pressure.

</details>


### [108] [Large exchange bias from coupled antiferromagnetic order and glassy magnetic freezing in $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25102)
*Suman Mondal,Himadri Roy Dakua,Raz Luger,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 单晶 Co₀.₃₆NbS₂ 在 T_N≈26 K 处出现反铁磁有序，并在更低温度表现出类似玻璃态的冻结行为；场冷后 M(H) 回线呈现显著交换偏置，峰值约 4.4 kOe，且在阻塞温度 T_B≈12 K 附近消失，说明偏置源于冻结的非补偿磁矩与反铁磁基体的交换耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非化学计量比的磁性 NbS₂ 插层化合物中交换偏置效应的来源，判断反铁磁-玻璃态磁矩耦合是否能成为一种更普适的产生交换偏置的途径。

Method: 对单晶 Co₀.₃₆NbS₂ 进行磁化测量，观察其反铁磁转变（T_N≈26 K）与低温历史相关的玻璃态冻结响应；通过场冷（最高 70 kOe）后测量 M(H) 回线得到交换偏置场 H_EB，并系统研究训练效应（training effect）、场扫描方式、冷却场协议依赖，以及偏置随温度在 T_B≈12 K 附近的消失行为。

Result: 在 1.8 K、70 kOe 场冷扫描协议下，交换偏置场达到 |H_EB|≈4.4 kOe；偏置仅在 T_B≈12 K（远低于 T_N）以下出现，并表现出训练效应及对场扫描和冷却场协议的系统依赖，支持冻结的非补偿磁矩与反铁磁基体之间交换耦合的图像。

Conclusion: 冻结的未补偿（玻璃态）磁矩与反铁磁基体之间的交换耦合是产生交换偏置的机制，表明反铁磁-玻璃态磁矩耦合可能为这类非化学计量比磁性 NbS₂ 插层化合物提供一条更广泛的交换偏置实现途径。

Abstract: Magnetization measurements on single-crystalline $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$ reveal antiferromagnetic order near $T_{N} \simeq 26 K$ and a lower-temperature history-dependent magnetic response consistent with glassy freezing of a subset of magnetic moments. After field cooling, $M(H)$ loops exhibit a sizable exchange-bias shift, reaching $|H_{EB}| \simeq 4.4 kOe$ at $1.8 K$ for a $70 kOe$ field-cooling sweep protocol. The training effect, field-sweep and cooling-field protocol dependence, and disappearance of the bias near a blocking temperature $T_{B} \simeq 12 K$, well below $T_{N}$, support an interpretation in which frozen uncompensated moments are exchange-coupled to an antiferromagnetic matrix. These results suggest that antiferromagnetic-glassy-moment coupling may provide a broader route to exchange bias in off-stoichiometric magnetic $NbS_{2}$ intercalates.

</details>


### [109] [Frustration-Assisted Electrical Switching in $Co_xTaS_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25112)
*Matan Sterenberg,Bar Segura,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在近化学计量比的范德华反铁磁体 CoₓTaS₂ 中展示了由磁阻挫与玻璃态慢动力学驱动的电流切换，无需刚性序参量假设，激活电流密度比低插层相低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上反铁磁体电切换被描述为电流诱导力矩作用于刚性序参量；但在阻挫磁体中，竞争态会形成依赖历史的能量景观，其中缓慢集体动力学主动参与电重构。作者旨在探究这种阻挫辅助切换机制的实验证据。

Method: 以近化学计量比 CoₓTaS₂（位于霍尔活性与霍尔非活性多Q磁区间的组分敏感边界）为对象，制备微结构化器件并施加电流脉冲写入非易失各向异性电阻态；结合磁化老化和场后弛豫测量，研究温度依赖的两种动力学区间及弛豫强度与逆切换幅度的关系。

Result: 晶体无净自发霍尔响应但保留费米能级附近的范霍夫奇点，并在 T_N≈31.5 K 以下反铁磁有序。电流脉冲可写入非易失各向异性电阻态，激活电流密度比霍尔活性低插层相低近一个数量级；切换响应分为两个温度依赖的动力学区间而非单一电流阈值。磁化老化与后场弛豫揭示了缓慢的、历史依赖的磁动力学，且弛豫强度与逆切换幅度紧密相关。

Conclusion: 磁阻挫与玻璃态动力学是 CoₓTaS₂ 电重构的核心要素，建立了一条通过动力学软反铁磁景观实现功能性切换的途径。

Abstract: Electrical switching in antiferromagnets is commonly described as the action of current-induced torques on a rigid order parameter. In frustrated magnets, however, competing states can create a history-dependent energy landscape in which slow collective dynamics actively participate in electrical reconfiguration. Here, we demonstrate frustration-assisted switching in near-stoichiometric $Co_xTaS_2$, a van der Waals antiferromagnet located at the composition-sensitive boundary between Hall-active and Hall-inactive multi-Q-related magnetic regimes. Although the crystals exhibit no resolved spontaneous Hall response, they retain van Hove singularities near the Fermi level and order antiferromagnetically below $T_N \simeq 31.5 K$. Current pulses write nonvolatile anisotropic resistance states in microstructured devices, with an activation current density nearly an order of magnitude below that reported for the Hall-active lower-intercalation phase. The switching response separates into two temperature-dependent dynamical regimes rather than following a single current threshold. Independent magnetization-aging and post-field-relaxation measurements reveal slow, history-dependent magnetic dynamics, and the relaxation strength closely follows the inverse switching amplitude. These results identify magnetic frustration and glassy dynamics as central ingredients in the electrical reconfiguration of $Co_xTaS_2$, establishing a route to functional switching through a dynamically soft antiferromagnetic landscape.

</details>


### [110] [Ferroelastic domain wall driven glassy dynamics and photoresponse in a SrTiO$_3$ (111) two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25943)
*Jyotirmay Maity,Manav Beniwal,Pranab Hazra,Sumit Tomar,Srimanta Middey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于 SrTiO$_3$(STO)(111) 取向异质结构中 AlO$_x$/STO 界面的二维电子气，发现其低温输运表现出类似玻璃态的电子动力学（热滞、慢弛豫、冷却速率依赖和记忆效应），并证明其起源于 STO 的铁弹性畴壁网络，且电子-畴壁耦合可通过载流子密度调控；同时揭示极性畴壁主导持久光电导行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STO(111) 取向异质结构是研究二维电子气的重要平台，但 STO 极性铁弹性畴壁网络对电荷输运的影响此前基本未被探索。作者旨在厘清铁弹性畴壁（一种拓扑缺陷）是否以及如何影响 2DEG 的电子输运与光电响应，并探讨其可调控性。

Method: 在 AlO$_x$/STO(111) 界面制备具有不同载流子密度的两个 2DEG 样品，系统测量其低温输运性质，观察热滞、慢电阻弛豫、冷却速率依赖性和记忆效应等玻璃态特征；分析弛豫对温度的依赖关系并与体相 STO 铁弹性畴壁态的演化对比；同时测量不同温度下的持久光电导，考察极性畴壁的作用。

Result: 低密度 2DEG 表现出玻璃态电子动力学：弛豫随温度呈强烈非单调变化，约 80 K 以下增强、25 K 附近达峰、12 K 以下消失，与体相 STO 铁弹性畴壁态演化吻合而非电子能量尺度，确认畴壁网络是其起源。高密度（更金属性）界面则无此特征，表明电子-畴壁耦合可由载流子密度调控。另外，持久光电导在约 80 K 以下急剧上升，与畴壁极性起始温度一致，表明极性畴壁主导光电响应。

Conclusion: 极性铁弹性畴壁是 STO(111) 基 2DEG 中一种可通过载流子密度调控的电子自由度，为经由拓扑缺陷操控 2DEG 性质开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The (111) orientation of SrTiO$_3$ (STO)-based heterostructures has emerged as a versatile platform for two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs), yet the influence of STO's polar ferroelastic domain-wall (DW) network on charge transport remains largely unexplored. Here we study AlO$_x$/STO (111) interfaces hosting 2DEGs of different carrier densities. In the lower-density 2DEG, low-temperature transport exhibits thermal hysteresis, slow resistance relaxation, cooling-rate dependence, and memory effects, signaling glasslike electron dynamics. The relaxation is strongly non-monotonic in temperature, increasing below $\sim$80 K, peaking near 25 K, and vanishing below $\sim$12 K, tracking the established evolution of the ferroelastic DW state of bulk STO rather than any electronic energy scale, which identifies the DW network as its origin. These signatures are absent in a second, more metallic interface with much higher carrier density, demonstrating that the electron-DW coupling can be tuned. We further show that polar DWs govern the photoresponse: the persistent photoconductivity rises sharply below $\sim$80 K, coinciding with the onset of DW polarity. Together, these results establish polar ferroelastic DWs as a carrier-density-tunable electronic degree of freedom in STO (111)-based 2DEGs, opening routes to manipulate 2DEG properties through topological defects.

</details>


### [111] [Methods of density functional theory in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26012)
*Thomas E. Baker,Kasey Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一维Kohn-Sham体系的数值计算策略与精确性质（含含时性质）的确定方法，并提供了一个公开可用的一维密度泛函理论计算程序库，同时给出了一种适用于精确Kohn-Sham势含时演化的基于梯度的技术的替代性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论仅由存在性证明形式化地论证，因此开发能够研究该理论的计算方法，是理解完整理论的有用途径。作者旨在通过高效的一维数值计算策略，帮助深入理解Kohn-Sham体系及其精确性质（尤其是含时性质）。

Method: 论文讨论了在一维中高效评估Kohn-Sham体系计算的数值策略，综述了用于实现一维密度泛函的表达式，并提出一种基于梯度的技术的替代性证明，该方法可应用于精确Kohn-Sham势的含时演化；此外还开发并公开了一个一维密度泛函理论计算程序库。

Result: 论文给出了一维Kohn-Sham体系的高效数值计算策略、用于确定精确性质（包括含时）的方法，以及一个公开可用的一维DFT计算程序库；同时展示了基于梯度技术的替代性证明在精确Kohn-Sham势含时演化中的应用。

Conclusion: 通过数值计算方法研究一维Kohn-Sham体系，是理解密度泛函理论完整理论的有效路线；文中提供的数值策略、程序库以及基于梯度的替代证明，为高效评估计算和确定精确（含时）性质提供了实用工具与理论支撑。

Abstract: Density functional theory is formally justified by an existence proof. As such, the need for computational methods to study the theory is a useful route to understanding the full theory. In this paper, we discuss the numerical strategies to efficiently evaluate computations for Kohn-Sham systems in one dimension and also methods for determining exact properties, including time-dependence. An accompanying library is also available for computing one-dimensional density functional theory and publicly available. The expressions used to implement the density functional in one-dimension are surveyed. An alternative justification for a gradient-based technique is also shown that can be applied to time-dependent evolution of the exact Kohn-Sham potential.

</details>


### [112] [Evidence of valence bond solid and quantum paramagnetism in the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model from pseudofermion functional renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26135)
*Gün Günal,Erhai Zhao,Ahmet Keleş*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用伪费米子泛函重整化群（pf-fRG）研究正方晶格上 J1-J2-J3 海森堡模型的零温相图，在量子顺磁（QP）区域内进一步区分出价键固体（VBS）序与量子自旋液体（QSL）两个子区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期张量网络研究报道在量子顺磁（QP）区域存在量子自旋液体（QSL），作者因此希望用改进的pf-fRG方法，在更大的参数空间内精确确定该扩展QP区域的相边界，并进一步分辨QP区域内是否存在键序（bond order），从而区分VBS与QSL。

Method: 采用伪费米子泛函重整化群（pf-fRG）方法，通过提升频率分辨率与扩大晶格截断，对 J1-J2-J3 海森堡模型进行零温数值计算；同时测量二聚化对外加 J1 微小变形（破坏点群对称性的键各向异性）的响应，即二聚化磁化率，并跟踪其在重整化群流中的演化行为。

Result: 研究得出了扩展QP区域更精确的相边界。进一步的二聚化磁化率流动显示QP窗口分裂为两个区域：一个区域内二聚化磁化率持续增长至最低能标，表明存在价键固体（VBS）序；另一区域内该磁化率在有限能标达到峰值后被抑制，表明为量子自旋液体（QSL）。

Conclusion: QP区域并非单一相，而是由VBS序区域与QSL区域共同组成；pf-fRG的二聚化磁化率分析为区分二者提供了有效判据，支持了张量网络关于QSL存在的结论。

Abstract: Motivated by recent tensor network studies reporting a quantum spin liquid (QSL) within the quantum paramagnetic (QP) regime, we investigate numerically the zero-temperature phase diagram of the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model on the square lattice using the pseudofermion functional renormalization group (pf-fRG). Thanks to improved frequency resolution and larger lattice truncations, we are able to explore a significantly larger parameter space than previous pf-fRG work to map out the phase boundary of an extended QP region. We further resolve possible bond order within the QP regime by measuring the dimer response to small deformations in $J_1$, i.e. bond anisotropy that breaks the point-group symmetry of the Hamiltonian. The functional renormalization group flow of the dimer susceptibility suggests that the QP window splits into two: a region where it grows continuously down to the lowest energy scale to signal valence bond solid (VBS) order, and another region where it reaches a peak at a finite scale and is subsequently suppressed to indicate a QSL.

</details>


### [113] [High-order resonances and emergent coherent dynamics in interacting Stark chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26328)
*Jiahe Chang,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示相互作用斯塔克链中高阶相互作用-倾斜共振的系统层次结构，这些共振表现为具有阶数依赖时间尺度的长寿命相干振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性势梯度会强烈抑制量子动力学，但相互作用可激活共振多体过程，然而其高阶结构此前大多未被探索，因此需要系统研究高阶多体共振。

Method: 通过隔离单个共振通道，建立微观有效描述，将动力学时间尺度与底层多体交换过程联系起来，并分析其对初态结构的依赖。

Result: 发现高阶相互作用-倾斜共振的系统层次，共振阶数对应所涉及的最少基本交换步数；共振表现为具有阶数依赖时间尺度的长寿命相干振荡，且时间尺度显著依赖于初态结构。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了高阶多体共振的关键信息，并表明相称的相互作用-倾斜比会生成一系列参数化缓慢的相干多体动力学。

Abstract: A linear potential gradient can strongly suppress quantum dynamics, while interactions can activate resonant many-body processes whose higher-order structure remains largely unexplored. Here we uncover a systematic hierarchy of high-order interaction--tilt resonances in an interacting Stark chain, where the resonance order counts the minimal number of elementary exchange steps involved. These resonances manifest as long-lived coherent oscillations with order-dependent timescales. By isolating individual resonant channels and developing a microscopic effective description, we connect these dynamical timescales to the underlying many-body exchange processes and reveal a pronounced dependence on the initial-state structure. Our results provide crucial insights into high-order many-body resonances and show how commensurate interaction-to-tilt ratios generate a hierarchy of parametrically slow coherent many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [Variational Block Correlated Coupled Cluster Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26646)
*Hunter Brodie,Alexander Shilcusky,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于量子蒙特卡洛模拟的变分耦合簇方法，利用杨表表示波函数系数及其导数大幅降低变分优化的计算复杂度，并通过基于虚温度的重要性采样加速收敛，在一维和二维海森堡模型上取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期量子神经态成功的启发，作者希望将耦合簇方法改造成一种变分实现，使其既能用于量子蒙特卡洛框架，又能缓解传统变分耦合簇计算在波函数系数求导优化上的高计算复杂度问题。

Method: 提出一种适用于量子蒙特卡洛的变分耦合簇实现；引入杨表（Young tableaux）表示来编码波函数展开系数及其导数，显著降低变分优化的计算开销。该算法是通用的，文中以块关联耦合簇（BCCC）含双激发和三激发态的 ansatz 为例，在一维和二维海森堡模型上进行验证，并采用基于虚温度（fictitious temperature）的重要性采样来改进收敛性。

Result: 所提方法在海森堡模型（一维与二维）上取得了有竞争力的精度，同时为变分耦合簇计算提供了可扩展的框架；重要性采样策略显著改善了变分优化的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该方法将杨表表示与量子蒙特卡洛变分优化相结合，有效降低了计算复杂度并提升了收敛性，实现的变分耦合簇框架具备可扩展性，能够以有竞争力的精度处理一维和二维海森堡模型。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent success of quantum neural states, we present a variational implementation of the coupled cluster method suitable for quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We introduce a Young tableaux representation of the wave-function coefficients and their derivatives that substantially reduces the computational complexity of the variational optimization. While the algorithm is generic, we illustrate its implementation with an application to the Heisenberg model in one and two dimensions, using the block correlated coupled cluster ansatz (BCCC) with doubles and triples. Convergence is greatly improved by means of importance sampling based on a fictitious temperature. The resulting approach achieves competitive accuracy while providing a scalable framework for variational coupled-cluster calculations.

</details>


### [115] [Kondo Lattice model for field induced triplet pairing in CeRh$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26858)
*Indra Gankhuyag,Piers Coleman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过构建CeRh₂As₂的近藤晶格模型，揭示了由铈位点局部反演对称性破缺驱动的d-f轨道杂化如何产生反对称自旋轨道耦合（ASOC），进而导致近简并的偶/奇宇称超导态及磁场诱导的宇称转变，并将结论推广到CeSiI等其他重费米子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋三重态超导体因其拓扑性质和对高磁场的鲁棒性而备受关注。CeRh₂As₂同时具有偶宇称和候选奇宇称超导相，且存在磁场诱导的两相转变，该转变由交错反对称自旋轨道耦合（ASOC）实现。然而，CeRh₂As₂超导的重费米子起源以及ASOC的微观来源此前尚未得到解释，这是本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 作者为CeRh₂As₂建立了一个近藤晶格（Kondo lattice）模型，并论证铈位点处局部破缺的反演对称性驱动铈d轨道与f轨道之间的局域杂化，从而产生交错反对称自旋轨道耦合（ASOC）。基于该模型，作者分析了由此得到的超导配对态及其在磁场下的行为。

Result: 该近藤晶格模型产生了近简并的偶宇称和奇宇称超导态，并在磁场下展现出从偶宇称态到奇宇称赝自旋三重态（pseudospin triplet）的宇称转变，成功复现了CeRh₂As₂中磁场诱导的宇称切换现象。

Conclusion: 局部反演对称性破缺驱动的d-f杂化是CeRh₂As₂中ASOC和磁场诱导宇称转变的微观根源。该机制具有更广泛的适用性，可推广至CeSiI等其他重费米子系统。

Abstract: Spin triplet superconductors are of interest because of their topological properties, and their robustness to high magnetic fields. CeRh$_2$As$_2$ is a unique heavy fermion superconductor with both even-parity and candidate odd-parity superconducting phases with a special feature enabling a field induced phase transition between the two. The field induced transition which switches the parity of the Cooper pairs is enabled by a staggered antisymmetric spin orbit coupling (ASOC) interaction. However, the heavy fermion origins of the superconductivity in CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and the origin of the antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling are currently unexplored. Here, we develop a Kondo lattice model for CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and demonstrate how local hybridization between Cerium $d$- and $f$-orbitals, driven by the locally broken inversion symmetry, at the Cerium sites generates the ASOC. The resulting Kondo lattice model leads to nearly degenerate even and odd parity superconducting states that display a field induced parity transition between an even-parity state and an odd-parity pseudospin triplet state. We discuss the wider implications of our results extended to other heavy fermion systems like CeSiI.

</details>


### [116] [On Higher-Form Fracton Hydrodynamics with Momentum Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26862)
*Ben Bobell,Rahul Nandkishore,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用 Martin-Siggia-Rose 有效场论框架，建立了平移不变系统中奇异守恒律流体动力学（无需自发对称破缺）的表示论一致性条件与广义高斯定律约束，并证明除高阶形式对称外，无其他理论与动量守恒相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量守恒在流体中普遍存在，但与奇异守恒律理论（如分形子流体动力学）可能不相容，除非对称性自发破缺。作者希望在不引入自发对称破缺的前提下，建立平移不变系统奇异守恒律流体动力学的普适理论框架，并厘清何种守恒律能与动量守恒共存。

Method: 采用 Martin-Siggia-Rose（MSR）有效场论形式，在流体动力学形式的温和假设下，提出两个表示论一致性条件和一个广义高斯定律约束；对高阶形式对称验证这些条件并推导其理想流体动力学；利用群表示论方法对 SO(3) 及足够大奇数维 SO(d) 进行分类排除。

Result: 高阶形式对称满足一致性条件；三维中一形式情形给出理想非相对论磁流体动力学；进一步证明除高阶形式对称外，SO(3) 以及足够大奇数维 SO(d) 中不存在其他与动量守恒相容的理论。

Conclusion: 在无自发对称破缺的平移不变系统中，能够与动量守恒相容的奇异守恒律流体动力学本质上由高阶形式对称刻画，其它可能性被表示论约束排除，从而为奇异流体动力学的分类提供了严格判据。

Abstract: Momentum conservation is common in fluids, yet it may be incompatible with the hydrodynamics of theories with exotic conservation laws (such as fracton hydrodynamics) unless symmetries are spontaneously broken. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose (MSR) effective field theory formalism, we develop a representation-theoretic framework for the hydrodynamics of translationally invariant systems with exotic conservation laws without spontaneous symmetry breaking. Under mild assumptions on the form of hydrodynamics, we show that such theories must satisfy two representation theoretic consistency conditions and a generalized Gauss's law constraint. Higher-form symmetries satisfy these conditions and we derive their ideal hydrodynamics; in three dimensions, the one-form case produces ideal non-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics. Finally, we prove that no other theories are compatible with momentum conservation in SO(3) nor SO($d$), for sufficiently large odd $d$.

</details>
