### [117] [Encoding Hyperuniformity in Geometric Networks: Structural Signatures and Diffusion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24317)
*Yongyi Wang,Eli Newby,Yang Jiao,Salvatore Torquato,Réka Albert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 无序超均匀性是一种新发现的奇异状态，表现为长波长密度涨落被异常抑制。本文研究其在网络系统中的推广，发现几何网络比 Delaunay 三角剖分保留了更多底层点构型的密度信息，超均匀性在网络结构指标和扩散动力学中均有鲜明体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀性是近期发现的奇异物质状态，但如何将其推广到网络系统仍是一个重要的开放问题。作者希望揭示超均匀性在网络结构与动力学中的表现形式，并解决网络超均匀性中的一个核心难题——区分超均匀与近超均匀构型。

Method: 作者在多种系统中构建几何网络与 Delaunay 三角剖分，比较二者对底层点构型长波长密度信息的保留程度；利用贴近度中心性、顶点强度、特征向量中心性等网络结构观测量刻画顶点间的涨落；并引入基于扩散的弛豫时间中心性，将结构特征与扩散动力学相联系。

Result: 几何网络比 Delaunay 三角剖分保留更多长波长密度信息；在几何网络中，超均匀性表现为贴近度中心性、顶点强度、特征向量中心性等观测量涨落被抑制。这些指标不仅能区分超均匀与非超均匀网络，还能分离近超均匀构型与具有强局部无序的超均匀系统。基于扩散的弛豫时间中心性显示，超均匀几何网络的弛豫行为比非超均匀几何网络更均匀。

Conclusion: 长波长密度涨落的抑制会同时体现于网络结构和扩散动力学之中：几何网络是承载超均匀性信息的优良载体，结构中心性指标与扩散弛豫时间中心性共同揭示了无序超均匀网络区别于非超均匀网络的内在特征。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniformity is a recently discovered exotic state exhibiting anomalously suppressed density fluctuations at long wavelengths, and its generalization to network systems remains an important open question. Here, we show that, across a wide range of systems, geometric networks retain substantially more long-wavelength density information from the underlying point configurations than Delaunay triangulations. Within geometric networks, hyperuniformity manifests as suppressed vertex-to-vertex fluctuations in a range of network structural observables, including closeness centrality, vertex strength, and eigenvector centrality. These measures not only distinguish hyperuniform networks from non-hyperuniform ones, but also separate near-hyperuniform configurations from hyperuniform systems with strong local disorder, solving a central challenge in network hyperuniformity. We connect these structural signatures to network dynamics by introducing a relaxation-time centrality based on diffusion and demonstrate that hyperuniform geometric networks exhibit more homogeneous relaxation behavior than non-hyperuniform geometric networks. These results reveal how the suppression of large-scale density fluctuations manifests in both network structure and diffusion dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Maximum-Temperature Theorem for a Nanothermometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24340)
*Taras Plakhotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文证明了在稳态热传导下，置于等温参考面内任意位置的有限体积温度传感器，其准稳态温升存在一个与传感器形状无关的尖锐上界，该上界仅依赖于区域总热功率、热导率下界和传感器热力学体积，并据此否定了开尔文量级读数的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想点热源的稳态温度预测在传统热传导理论中发散，但实际温度计测量的是有限体积而非点值。作者旨在为传感器的准稳态温升建立一个严格、与形状无关的上界，以厘清实验中观测到的异常温升（开尔文量级）是否可能由热源定位或源-传感器距离等几何因素解释。

Method: 作者基于稳态热传导方程推导出一个严格的数学上界。该上界只需三个参数：区域内产生的总热功率 $\dot{Q}$、区域热导率的下界 $\kappa_\mathrm{m}$，以及传感器的热力学体积 $V_\mathrm{s}$。该界与热源分布、域形状以及源-传感器距离均无关，因而是一个普适的、与形状无关的尖锐上界。

Result: 对于典型参数 $\dot{Q}=50~\mathrm{pW}$、$\kappa_\mathrm{m}=0.1~\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$、$V_\mathrm{s}=10^{-22}~\mathrm{m^3}$（约 $60~\mathrm{nm}$ 直径球体体积），定理给出的最大温升约为参考水平以上 $2~\mathrm{mK}$。该结果直接适用于纳米金刚石、上转换纳米粒子、荧光聚合物颗粒和核壳量子点等体系。

Conclusion: 该定理表明，在稳态热传导条件下，无论是热源定位问题还是不确定的源-传感器距离，都无法解释开尔文量级（Kelvin-scale）的温度读数。有限的传感器体积从根本上限制了可观测的温升幅度，为相关纳米温度测量实验的异常结果提供了一种理论约束与判据。

Abstract: The steady-state temperature predicted for an ideal point heat source diverges in conventional heat-conduction theory, but no physical thermometer measures a point value. We derive a sharp, shape-independent upper bound on the quasi-steady temperature rise of a sensor with thermometric volume $V_\mathrm{s}$, placed anywhere inside a region enclosed by an isothermal reference surface. The bound requires only the total heat power generated in the region $\dot{Q}$, a lower bound on its thermal conductivity $κ_\mathrm{m}$, and the value of $V_\mathrm{s}$. It is otherwise independent of the heat-source distribution, domain shape, and source--sensor distance. For $\dot{Q}=50~\mathrm{pW}$, $κ_\mathrm{m} = 0.1~\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$, and $V_\mathrm{s}=10^{-22}\mathrm{\,m^3}$, approximately the volume of a $60~\mathrm{nm}$-diameter sphere, the theorem permits a maximum temperature rise of approximately $2~\mathrm{mK}$ above the reference level. The result applies directly to nanodiamonds, upconversion nanoparticles, fluorescent polymer particles, and core-shell quantum dots. It shows that neither source localization nor uncertain source-sensor separation can account for kelvin-scale readings under steady-state heat conduction.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond low-dissipation regime: general condition for Curzon-Ahlborn efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24434)
*Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在低耗散卡诺热机模型基础上引入逆操作时间的非线性响应，推导出在温度差二次阶恢复Curzon-Ahlborn效率的一般条件，并证明广义对称耗散条件与遵循牛顿冷却定律的CA模型均为其特例，同时给出了通用传热律下端可逆模型呈现CA效率的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CA效率在温度差一阶下具有普适性，但在二阶普遍失效；低耗散模型中的对称耗散条件是充分而非必要，且原CA端可逆模型并不要求该对称性，因此需要更一般的条件以解释二阶CA效率的成立机制。

Method: 将低耗散模型推广至包含逆操作时间的非线性响应，推导最大功率效率在温度差二次阶的展开式，并从中提取恢复CA效率的一般条件；将该条件与广义对称耗散条件、牛顿冷却定律下的CA模型及通用传热律的端可逆模型进行对照。

Result: 得到在二次阶恢复CA效率的一般条件；广义对称耗散条件与遵循牛顿冷却定律的CA模型均是该条件的特例；进一步明确了具有通用传热律的端可逆模型呈现CA效率所满足的条件。

Conclusion: 通过在低耗散模型中纳入非线性响应，本文统一了此前关于CA效率二阶普适性成立的不同条件，揭示对称耗散并非必要，为理解最大功率效率与CA效率之间的关系提供了更普遍的框架。

Abstract: In recent years, physicists have extensively investigated the universality of Curzon-Ahlborn (CA) efficiency as the efficiency at maximum power of heat engines, which, like Carnot efficiency, is determined only by heat-bath temperatures. While the universality of the CA efficiency to the first order in the temperature difference is established within linear response theory in the inverse operation time, it is generally lost at quadratic order. In the low-dissipation Carnot heat engine model known as the standard framework, the symmetric dissipation condition is necessary. Yet, it is not a necessary condition as the original CA model that adopts endoreversibility does not require such symmetry. In this study, we extend the low-dissipation model to include nonlinear response in the inverse operation time and derive a general condition under which the CA efficiency is recovered in the quadratic order. The generalized symmetric dissipation condition and the CA model with Newton's law of cooling are shown to be special cases of this condition. By applying the obtained condition, we also clarify the condition under which an endoreversible model with a general heat-transfer law exhibits the CA efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Boundary-Sensitive Quantum Order on Non-Amenable Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26859)
*Xingzhi Wang,Zohar Nussinov,Gerardo Ortiz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在非可配格（non-amenable lattices）上，边界占系统比例很大的量子系统中，作者发现了全新的体相有序形式。通过在配位数为3的无限Cayley树上研究p<5的时钟模型，并结合边界保留的iDMRG与对称性固定对偶，发现有序相与无序相之间存在一个中间相，其体相有序由任意弱的边界场诱导，而非传统的自发对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相变理论以自发对称性破缺（Landau范式）为核心，但该范式在非欧几里得几何（如非可配格）上可能失效。作者旨在探索非可配格上边界占系统广泛比例时，是否存在超越自发对称性破缺的新型体相有序机制。

Method: 作者将边界保留的无限密度矩阵重整化群（iDMRG）方法与精确的对称性固定对偶变换相结合，在配位数为3的无限Cayley树上研究了p<5的状态时钟模型（Z_p时钟模型），系统分析其相图与相变行为。

Result: 在有序相与无序相之间发现了一个涌现的中间相：该相的体相有序由任意弱的边界场诱导，而非源于传统的自发对称性破缺。中间相还实现了一种体-边界对应关系，即未极化的边界会选择特定的有序体相态。

Conclusion: 边界敏感序是一种区别于Landau自发对称性破缺范式的独特量子有序机制，非可配格的几何性质（边界占广泛比例）使得边界场能以任意弱的强度决定体相有序，从而确定了新的量子有序类型。

Abstract: We show that quantum systems on non-amenable lattices, whose boundaries constitute an extensive fraction of the system, host novel forms of bulk ordering with no Euclidean counterpart. Combining boundary-retaining iDMRG with an exact symmetry-fixing duality, we investigate $p<5$ state clock models on the coordination-3 infinite Cayley tree. We find that between the ordered and disordered phases lies an emergent intermediate phase with bulk order induced by an arbitrarily weak boundary field rather than by conventional spontaneous symmetry-breaking. This establishes boundary-sensitive order as a distinct quantum ordering mechanism defying Landau's spontaneous symmetry-breaking paradigm. The intermediate phase realizes a bulk-boundary correspondence in which the unpolarized boundary selects a specific ordered bulk state.

</details>


### [121] [Energy-optimal scheduling for clocked reversible computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24435)
*Michael C. Thumann,David Soloveichik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究计算耗散的半可逆能量—记忆—时间权衡：在有限记忆与有限时间约束下，逻辑可逆计算（以清洗代替擦除）与局部不可逆擦除之间需要折中。作者提出一个固定操作点下带有时钟热耦合的计算机模型，以可逆检查点移动与不可逆擦除的有效代价之比 λ 选择能量最优的半可逆调度，并针对依赖图为线性的顺序计算证明了最优调度是“严格可逆块 + 一次局部擦除”的重复模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算耗散既可来自信息擦除，也可来自有限时间操作；无时空约束时两者原则上均可避免。但在有限记忆和有限时间内，用可逆清洗替代擦除需要额外耗散操作，因此存在能量—记忆—时间的半可逆权衡，本文旨在精确刻画并求解这一权衡下的最优调度。

Method: 在固定操作点上建立外部时钟热耦合计算机的模型，为可逆检查点移动与局部不可逆擦除分别赋予有效代价 ε_R 与 ε_I，用其比值 λ=ε_I/ε_R 在检查点记忆约束下通过权衡可逆移动与擦除来选择能量最优半可逆调度；对依赖图为一条线的顺序计算，推导出精确的分隔器递推（separator recurrence），并证明渐近最优调度重复“严格可逆块后接一次局部擦除”的结构；进一步将调度定理耦合到随机热力学本构模型，分析温度、基本操作时间、记忆量与器件相关操作成本如何决定最优调度的形态。

Result: 推导出顺序计算（线形依赖图）的精确分隔器递推，证明渐近最优调度采用“严格可逆块 + 一次局部擦除”重复的结构；并通过随机热力学本构模型确定温度、基本操作持续时间、记忆量及器件相关操作成本对最优调度选择（可逆清洗、选择性擦除或每次处置都擦除）的影响条件。

Conclusion: 在有限记忆与有限时间下，可逆清洗与不可逆擦除之间存在半可逆的能量—记忆—时间权衡；该权衡可由可逆与不可逆操作代价比 λ 在固定操作点定量刻画。对线性顺序计算，能量最优解具有“可逆块 + 单次擦除”的重复结构，且温度、计时参数与器件成本共同决定最优策略。

Abstract: Computational dissipation may arise from both information erasure and finite-time operation, although both contributions can in principle be avoided in the absence of space and time constraints. Logically reversible computation can replace erasure with ancilla cleanup, and slow adiabatic protocols can eliminate any excess finite-time dissipation. Under limited memory and in finite time, however, choosing reversible cleanup over erasure requires additional dissipative operations, creating a semi-reversible energy--memory--time tradeoff. We formulate this tradeoff at a fixed operating point of an externally clocked thermally coupled computer, where reversible checkpoint moves and local irreversible erasures have specified effective prices $ε_R$ and $ε_I$. Their ratio $λ=ε_I/ε_R$ selects an energy-optimal semi-reversible schedule under a checkpoint-memory bound by trading reversible moves against erasures. For a sequential computation whose dependency graph is a line, we derive an exact separator recurrence and prove that an asymptotically optimal schedule repeats a strictly reversible block followed by one local erasure. Coupling this scheduling theorem to a stochastic-thermodynamic constitutive model shows how temperature, primitive duration, memory, and device-dependent operation costs determine whether the optimal schedule uses reversible cleanup, elective erasures, or erasure at every disposal.

</details>


### [122] [Autonomous Thermal Switching Outperforms the Frozen Optimum with Vanishing Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24589)
*Siyi Deng,Longxing Xu,Zhuolin Ye,Xian He,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究自主热开关能否在保持系统-辅助关联任意小的同时，超越最优冻结屏障分配的性能，并确认在固定降温预算、可见平衡及保持联合细致平衡条件下存在这一弛豫优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨自主（动态）热开关相对最优静态（冻结）屏障分配是否存在弛豫优势，同时希望这种优势不依赖显著的系统-辅助关联。

Method: 在有限可逆网络上分析最优快速开关，构造将预算集中于单条边上的混合配置；通过三态模型给出严格增益及其有限速率阈值；进一步考察集体与连续模型以提出胶体实验检验。

Result: 证明在固定降温预算、可见平衡及保持联合细致平衡下存在弛豫优势；最优快速开关将预算集中在单条边上；三态模型给出严格增益与有限速率阈值，且更快开关在弛豫全程保持增益的同时使关联趋于零。

Conclusion: 自主热开关确实能超越最优冻结屏障分配，且弛豫优势可在系统-辅助关联任意小的条件下保持，集体与连续模型结果提示可由胶体系统进行实验验证。

Abstract: Can autonomous thermal switching outperform the best frozen barrier allocation while keeping system--auxiliary correlations arbitrarily small? We establish this relaxation advantage at fixed lowering budget and visible equilibrium, preserving joint detailed balance. On finite reversible networks, optimal fast switching mixes profiles concentrating the budget on single edges. A three-state model gives a strict gain and its finite-rate threshold. Faster switching preserves the gain while correlations vanish throughout relaxation. Collective and continuous models suggest a colloidal test.

</details>


### [123] [Spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24669)
*Kinya Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究无序双位点量子击穿模型中粒子-空穴对称性的自发破缺，发现存在有限温度相变，且随每次转换产生的费米子数增加，相变由连续变为一级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序量子击穿模型中粒子-空穴对称性在有限温度下的自发破缺行为，以及对称性破缺相变的类型（连续或一级）如何随系统参数变化。

Method: 研究无序双位点量子击穿模型；在每个位点包含大量费米子模的极限下模型可解。当两位点模数之比很小时，解析推导自由能；并结合数值方法研究该极限之外的相变行为。

Result: 解析求得自由能，发现有限温度下的对称性破缺相变；随着每次转换产生的费米子数增加，相变由连续变为一级。数值结果显示两类相变在小模数比极限之外依然存在，连续相变的序参量指数接近 1/2。

Conclusion: 无序双位点量子击穿模型存在稳健的粒子-空穴对称性自发破缺相变；相变级次取决于每次转换产生的费米子数，连续相变属于平均场类型（指数约为 1/2）。

Abstract: We study spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model. The model is solvable in the limit of a large number of fermionic modes on each site. For a small ratio of the numbers of modes on the two sites, we derive the free energy analytically and find a finite-temperature symmetry-breaking transition. This transition changes from continuous to first order as the number of fermions produced in each conversion increases. Numerical results show that both types of transition persist beyond this regime, with an order-parameter exponent close to $1/2$ for continuous transitions.

</details>


### [124] [Frustration and topology in the phase behavior of solute-frustrated $q$-clock models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24923)
*Hari Rajesh,Richard C. Remsing*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了溶质挫败的 q-clock（SFqC）模型，用以探究挫败与拓扑如何共同影响相行为，发现系统具有丰富的低温相图及拓扑驱动的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挫败能增强涨落并产生新颖的相行为，当与拓扑缺陷结合时，可通过拓扑相变和挫败的拓扑相变进一步增加相图的复杂性。作者希望理解挫败与拓扑之间的相互作用如何影响相行为。

Method: 作者在 q-clock 模型（每个自旋可取 q 个取向，能展现自发对称破缺和拓扑相变）中加入溶质位点，构建 SFqC 模型；溶质与自旋的相互作用通过固定正拓扑缺陷（涡旋）浓度的下限来挫败系统。对 q=4 和 q=6 的情形进行蒙特卡洛模拟，并建立理论描述以研究相行为。

Result: SFqC 模型展现出依赖于参数选择的丰富相图，具有多种有趣的低温相，包括强挫败畴的存在以及长程序的缺失；即使在低温下也存在拓扑效应，例如拓扑缺陷可以驱动结晶。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了挫败与拓扑如何共同调控相行为和涨落，预期这些结果可指导利用挫败与拓扑来调控相行为和涨落的新型材料开发。

Abstract: Frustration can enhance fluctuations and create new and interesting phase behavior. When combined with the ability to host topological defects, frustration can increase the complexity of a system's phase diagram through topological and frustrated topological transitions. To understand the interplay of frustration and topology on phase behavior, we introduce and study solute-frustrated $q$-clock (SF$q$C) models, in which solute sites are added to the $q$-clock model, a lattice model where each spin can take one of $q$ orientations. The $q$-clock model can exhibit spontaneous symmetry breaking and topological transitions, and the interaction between the spins and a solute frustrates the system, in essence by creating a fixed lower bound on the concentration of positive topological defects or vortices. We perform Monte Carlo simulations and develop theoretical descriptions of SF$q$C models with $q=4$ and $q=6$ to understand their phase behavior. We find that these systems exhibit rich phase diagrams that depend on the choice of parameters. In particular, SF$q$C models have a wide range of interesting low-temperature phases, including the presence of strongly frustrated domains and the absence of long-range order. We also find topological effects even at low temperatures; for instance, we find that topological defects can drive crystallization. We anticipate these results to guide the development of materials that take advantage of frustration and topology to tune phase behavior and fluctuations.

</details>


### [125] [Scaling Dimensions and Two-Point Amplitudes from Operator Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24943)
*You-Chiuan Chen,Ribhu K. Kaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文指出临界晶格统计力学模型中两点函数的长程行为取决于量子场论的普适信息，但由于晶格算符会耦合到无穷多连续标度算符且系数未知，这些信息很难提取。作者对一种基于有限个晶格算符交叉关联构造的算符协方差矩阵本征分解的方法进行了详细分析，证明在标度不变性假设下（即使考虑无关注扰动），协方差矩阵本征值的衰减由最小的Nℓ个标度维度控制，并提出利用本征向量构建连续两点关联函数振幅的新方法，从而从晶格协方差矩阵完全确定连续两点函数的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界晶格模型的连续两点函数包含量子场论的普适信息，但晶格算符会耦合到无穷多个未知系数的标度算符，导致这些普适信息难以直接提取。作者旨在深入分析并完善一种通过协方差矩阵本征分解来分离这些信息的方法。

Method: 作者采用基于有限集合Nℓ个晶格算符交叉关联构造的算符协方差矩阵，并对其进行本征分解。具体包括：(1) 在标度不变性假设下，严格证明即使计入无关注扰动，协方差矩阵本征值的主导衰减由最小的Nℓ个标度维度控制；(2) 提出一种新方法，利用协方差矩阵的本征向量来构造连续两点关联函数的振幅，从而实现对连续两点函数结构的完整确定。方法在合成数据集（由标度假设生成）和二维Ising模型的数值精确数据上进行了验证。

Result: 作者给出了在标度不变性下协方差矩阵本征值主导衰减由最小Nℓ个标度维度控制的显式证明，且该结果在计入无关注扰动后仍然成立。基于此，提出用本征向量构造连续两点函数振幅的方法，能够从晶格协方差矩阵完全确定连续两点函数结构。该方法在合成数据集和二维Ising模型的数值精确数据上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过对算符协方差矩阵本征分解方法的详细分析，证明了本征值衰减受最小标度维度控制的严格结果，并利用本征向量成功构造连续两点关联函数振幅，实现了从晶格协方差矩阵对连续两点函数结构的完全确定，为提取临界晶格模型的普适量子场论信息提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The long distance behavior of two-point functions of critical lattice statistical mechanics models depend on detailed information of the underlying quantum field theory. Yet these universal properties can be difficult to extract because generic lattice operators couple to the infinite hierarchy of continuum scaling operators with unknown coefficients. Recently in Physical Review E 114, 024146 (2026), one approach to disentangle some of this information was proposed using the eigen-decomposition of the operator covariance matrix constructed from cross correlations of a finite set of $N_\ell$ lattice operators. In this work we analyze this method in detail. We provide an explicit proof that under the assumption of scale invariance the leading decay of the eigenvalues are controlled by the smallest $N_\ell$ scaling dimensions, even after taking irrelevant perturbations into account. Based on this understanding, we propose a new method by which the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix can be used to construct the amplitudes of the continuum two-point correlation functions. This allows for a complete determination of the structure of the continuum two-point functions from the lattice covariance matrix. We demonstrate our ideas both on synthetic data sets (drawn from the scaling hypothesis) and numerically exact data for the two dimensional Ising model.

</details>


### [126] [Run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external field: constructing the solution of the Fokker-Planck equation from the equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24961)
*Aoran Sun,Fangfu Ye,Jeff Z. Y. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对run-and-tumble粒子朗之万运动方程进行积分的方法，用于计算其位置与活性速度的联合分布（含与不含谐振外力场两种情况），并通过构造条件矩和相关函数，得到一维谐振势中run-and-tumble粒子的精确稳态分布，并证明其与Fokker-Planck方程精确解等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: run-and-tumble粒子是典型的活性物质模型，其位置的统计性质依赖内部活性速度状态，难以直接通过标准方法得到联合分布；尤其在有外力场时，位置与速度方向耦合，需要一种能严格处理这种非平衡稳态的系统方法。

Method: 引入一种对朗之万运动方程进行积分的方法：核心步骤是构造粒子分布的各阶矩及相关函数，并以"粒子在末时刻处于特定状态"为条件，从而分别处理tumble态和指定速度方向的run态，进而得到一维谐振势下run-and-tumble粒子的精确稳态分布。

Result: 得到一维谐振外力势中run-and-tumble粒子关于tumble态的精确稳态分布，以及在指定速度方向条件下的run态精确稳态分布，并证明这些结果与对应Fokker-Planck方程的精确解完全等价。

Conclusion: 该方法为run-and-tumble粒子（尤其是有外势时）的位置-活性速度联合分布提供了精确解析框架，验证了朗之万积分方法与Fokker-Planck描述在稳态问题上的等价性。

Abstract: A method for integrating the Langevin equations of motion for run-and-tumble particles is introduced to compute the joint distribution of their position and active velocity, with and without a harmonic external force field. The key step is the construction of the moments of the distribution and the correlation functions, conditional on the particles being in a specific state at the final time. For one-dimensional run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external potential, the exact steady-state distributions for the tumble state, as well as for the run state conditional on a specified velocity direction, are obtained. This enables us to demonstrate their equivalence to the exact solutions of the corresponding Fokker-Planck equations.

</details>


### [127] [Large Deviations in Switching Diffusion: Dynamical Condensation and Diffusivity BBP Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25072)
*Mathis Guéneau,Timo Schorlepp,Satya N. Majumdar,Gregory Schehr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散率在泊松切换时间随机变化的布朗粒子，在远超典型扩散尺度的大偏差区域 x(t)≈yt（y=O(1)）中，详细刻画其动力学相变与轨迹凝聚现象。结果显示位移在适中的 y 下由 O(t) 个增量分担，超过第一阈值 y_- 后出现一个 O(t) 量级的宏观增量（动力学凝聚）；对 ν>1 存在第二阈值 y_+（该增量承载全部位移），对 0<ν≤1 则两阈值重合。矩母函数相应地从多扩散率集体贡献转变为靠近 D_max 的状态选择，且该转变由与 Baik–Ben Arous–Péché (BBP) 转变相同的边缘机制支配，并通过稀有事件采样数值验证了动力学凝聚预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期工作（Guéneau et al., PRL 135, 067102, 2025）中，该系统的位移速率函数已通过更新方法求得并显示出多个奇异性，但对应的动力学相变和轨迹凝聚的具体机制尚未被系统阐明。本文旨在深入理解这些奇异性背后的物理图像，厘清扩散率选择与轨迹凝聚之间的关系。

Method: 考虑扩散率按泊松切换时刻独立从有界分布 W(D) 中抽取（上边缘处 W(D)~C(D_max-D)^ν）的布朗粒子。采用更新/大偏差方法，在 x(t)≈yt（y=O(1)）的大偏差区域分析位移的速率函数与矩母函数；结合边缘机制分析与 Baik–Ben Arous–Péché (BBP) 转变进行类比，识别阈值 y_- 与 y_+；并用稀有事件采样进行数值模拟验证动力学凝聚预测。

Result: 对于中等 y，位移由 O(t) 个切换间隔内积累的增量分担；超过第一阈值 y_- 后，单个间隔产生 O(t) 量级的增量并承载总位移的有限比例，标志着动力学凝聚。对 ν>1，存在第二阈值 y_+>y_-，此时宏观增量渐近承载全部施加位移；对 0<ν≤1，两阈值重合 y_-=y_+。相应地，矩母函数表现出从多扩散率集体贡献到靠近 D_max 状态选择的互补转变，且该转变受与 BBP 转变相同的边缘机制支配，表明扩散率选择与轨迹凝聚是同一现象的两个表现。数值稀有事件采样结果验证了动力学凝聚的预测。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了随机扩散率布朗粒子在大偏差区域的动力学相变与轨迹凝聚，确立了阈值 y_-、y_+ 及其对指数 ν 的依赖关系，并揭示扩散率选择与轨迹凝聚统一由 BBP 型边缘机制支配，二者是同一现象的不同表现。数值结果验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We consider a Brownian particle with position $x(t)$ whose diffusivity changes at Poissonian switching times, with each new value drawn independently from a bounded distribution $W(D)$. Assuming $W(D)\sim C(D_{\max}-D)^ν$ near its upper edge, we study the large-deviation regime $x(t)\simeq yt$ with $y = O(1)$ at large times, far beyond the typical diffusive scale. In a recent work Guéneau et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 067102 (2025), the rate function of the displacement was obtained through a renewal approach and it was shown to display several singularities. In this paper, we carry out a detailed study of the corresponding dynamical phase transitions and trajectory condensation. For moderate $y$, the imposed displacement is shared among $O(t)$ displacement increments accumulated during successive intervals between switches. Beyond a first threshold $y_-$, one interval produces an increment of order $t$ carrying a finite fraction of the total displacement, signaling dynamical condensation. For $ν>1$, a second threshold $y_+>y_-$ is reached when this macroscopic increment carries asymptotically the entire imposed displacement, whereas for $0<ν\leq1$ the two thresholds merge, $y_-=y_+$. In turn, the moment-generating function of $x(t)$ displays a complementary transition from collective contributions of many diffusivities to selection of states near $D_{\max}$. We show that this transition is governed by the same edge mechanism as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché (BBP) transition, indicating that diffusivity selection and trajectory condensation are two manifestations of the same phenomenon. We validate our predictions for the dynamical condensation against numerical results from rare event sampling.

</details>


### [128] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Orthogonally Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25103)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在时间反演与旋转不变系统中（正交对称类）Ericson区间内非对角散射矩阵元与截面的分布，证明其渐近展开主阶项呈高斯和指数行为，并推导了次阶项，与数值模拟比较，还讨论了三类Wigner-Dyson系综的异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前序工作已通过渐近展开方法分析了幺正与辛不变哈密顿量情形下的Ericson相变，但实验上最常用的正交对称类（时间反演与旋转不变）尚未得到同等理论处理，故需证明该情形下高斯与指数分布同样作为渐近主阶出现。

Method: 采用渐近展开技术，对正交情形下Ericson区间的非对角散射矩阵元及散射截面分布进行展开，推导主阶项与次阶项，并以数值模拟验证结果。

Result: 证明正交情形下这些分布的主阶渐近项呈高斯与指数行为，成功求得次阶项，数值模拟与理论推导吻合。

Conclusion: 高斯与指数行为并非幺正或辛系综特有，在正交对称类中同样作为渐近展开主阶出现；通过对比三类Wigner-Dyson系综，厘清了Ericson相变在不同对称类中的相似性与差异。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for systems that are time-reversal and rotationally invariant. In previous works, we developed techniques to analyze the Ericson transition by means of asymptotic expansions in the cases of unitarily and symplectically invariant Hamiltonians. Here, we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of those distributions also in the orthogonal case, which is often the relevant one for analyzing experiments. Moreover, we derive the subleading-order term and compare with numerical simulations. Finally, we discuss similarities and differences of the Ericson transition for all three Wigner-Dyson classes.

</details>


### [129] [Origins of Faceted Anomalous Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26356)
*Maximilien Bernard,José Luis Iguain,Alejandro Kolton,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了“分面异常标度”（faceted anomalous scaling）界面（α_s>1、α_s≠α、α_loc=1）的统计起源，指出其指数结构由斜率场的空间统计决定：斜率增量分解为典型涨落与稀有的大跳跃，二者竞争可导致斜率的双分形标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分面异常标度的统计起源一直不清楚，尤其是“分面（facets）是否必需”这一问题悬而未决。作者旨在阐明这类界面异常标度背后的斜率场统计机制。

Method: 将斜率增量分解为典型涨落与稀有的大跳跃两类贡献，并在广义非谐 Larkin 模型和柱状 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）方程中解析地建立该框架，精确推导出全局、谱与局域粗糙度指数。

Result: 异常标度要么源于典型斜率增量具有真正的系统尺寸依赖的异常标度，要么源于稀有跳跃主导二阶矩，两者可共存并导致斜率双分形标度。Larkin 模型同时实现两种机制，可产生有或无分面的异常界面；柱状 KPZ 属于跳跃主导情形，给出 α_s=3/2。

Conclusion: 分面异常标度的指数结构完全由斜率场的空间统计决定，且分面并非必需；该框架统一解释了 Larkin 模型与柱状 KPZ 中的异常标度行为。

Abstract: Fluctuating interfaces are typically characterized by their global, spectral, and local roughness exponents. Faceted anomalous scaling, characterized by $α_s>1$, $α_s\neqα$, and $α_{\mathrm{loc}}=1$, owes its name to the tendency of such interfaces to develop long, nearly linear segments called facets. Its statistical origin, and whether facets are required, have remained unclear. We show that this exponent structure is determined by the spatial statistics of the slope field. Slope increments split into typical fluctuations and rare, larger jumps. This anomalous scaling arises either when the typical slope increment displays genuine system-size-dependent anomalous scaling or when the rare jumps dominate the second moment. The two contributions can coexist, and their competition leads to bifractal scaling of the slopes. We establish this framework analytically in the generalized anharmonic Larkin model and the columnar Kardar--Parisi--Zhang equation, and we derive exactly the global, spectral, and local roughness exponents in both cases. The Larkin model realizes both mechanisms and produces anomalous interfaces with or without facets, whereas columnar KPZ belongs to the jump-dominated case and yields $α_s=3/2$.

</details>


### [130] [Annealing Dynamics of the Two-Dimensional Fully Frustrated Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25109)
*Kai-Hsin Wu,Anders W. Sandvik,Ying-Jer Kao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟退火研究了二维全阻挫伊辛模型的动力学标度性质，将Kibble-Zurek机制推广到临界温度为零、关联长度指数发散的情形，并估算了动力学指数z≈2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型具有高度简并的临界基态，其平衡性质已知，但其非平衡行为（尤其是关联长度在T→Tc=0时指数发散的动力学标度）尚未充分研究。作者希望将Kibble-Zurek机制推广到此类零温临界点上。

Method: 作者采用模拟退火方法，设计了一种便于推广Kibble-Zurek机制的退火协议。通过与推导出的标度形式进行拟合，系统地分析了初始温度依赖性，并分别从磁化率、剩余能量和Edwards-Anderson序参量三个物理量中提取动力学指数z。

Result: 数值结果与推导的标度形式高度吻合，且表现出常规Kibble-Zurek机制中不存在、依赖于初始温度的行为。三个物理量给出的动力学指数一致：磁化率z=1.99(2)，剩余能量z=2.072(8)，Edwards-Anderson序参量z=2.09(3)，均接近z=2，与瞬态淬火后弛豫及平衡时间关联的已有研究（包括平衡自关联函数给出的z=2.004(9)）相符。

Conclusion: 二维全阻挫伊辛模型在退火过程中遵循可推广到零温临界点的Kibble-Zurek标度形式，且动力学指数z≈2，与多种独立测量一致，验证了该推广的有效性并深化了对该模型非平衡普适行为的理解。

Abstract: We study the dynamical scaling properties of the two-dimensional fully frustrated Ising model by simulated annealing. The model has a highly degenerate, critical ground state with well known equilibrium properties. To study its out-of-equilibrium behavior, we develop an annealing protocol that is convenient for extending the Kibble-Zurek mechanism to the case of an exponentially divergent correlation length in the limit of the temperature $T \to T_c = 0$. We find excellent agreement with the scaling forms derived, including a dependence on the initial temperature that is not present in the conventional Kibble-Zurek mechanism. The dynamic exponent $z$ is consistent among all quantities studied; fits of its dependence on the initial temperature give $z = 1.99(2)$ (susceptibility), $z = 2.072(8)$ (excess energy), and $z = 2.09(3)$ (Edwards-Anderson order parameter). These values are close to $z=2$, which is consistent with previous studies of relaxation after instantaneous quenches and of time dependent correlations in equilibrium, including the estimate $z = 2.004(9)$ from equilibrium autocorrelation functions.

</details>


### [131] [Tensor network Monte Carlo study of the J1-J2 Ising model on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25270)
*Wanying Zheng,Tao Chen,Youjin Deng,Wanzhou Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种张量网络蒙特卡洛(TNMC)算法，用于研究受挫的方格子J1-J2伊辛模型，克服了传统蒙特卡洛的临界慢化与张量重整化群方法的截断误差问题，并通过有限尺寸标度提取了临界温度与热指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方格子J1-J2伊辛模型表现出丰富的磁相，但由于传统蒙特卡洛方法存在临界慢化、张量重整化群方法存在截断误差，其相变表征十分困难，因此需要一种高效、几乎无偏差的新数值方法。

Method: 作者开发了支持开边界与周期性边界条件的张量网络蒙特卡洛(TNMC)算法。对周期晶格采用扫描式块更新策略，即在固定周围自旋的同时更新自旋块。为提升效率，将采样器与直方图重加权技术结合，并对每种技术进行独立测试以验证优化效果。

Result: 在g=0.55和g=1.0处进行了广泛的有限尺寸标度分析，从Binder累积量数据中提取了表观临界温度和有效热指数。

Conclusion: 该方法为周期性边界下J1-J2型受挫磁体的大规模模拟提供了一条高效、几乎无偏差的路径，并且易于推广到其它受挫自旋晶格。

Abstract: The frustrated square-lattice J1-J2 Ising model exhibits rich magnetic phases, yet its phase transitions remain difficult to characterize due to critical slowing down in conventional Monte Carlo and truncation errors inherent to tensor renormalization group methods.We develop a tensor-network Monte Carlo (TNMC) algorithm for this frustrated model, which supports both open and periodic boundary conditions. For periodic lattices, we use a sweeping block-update strategy that updates spin blocks while fixing surrounding spins.To boost efficiency, we couple our sampler with histogram reweighting, and test each technique standalone to validate these independent optimizations.We perform extensive finite-size scaling analyses at g=0.55 and g=1.0, extracting apparent critical temperatures and effective thermal exponents from Binder-cumulant data.Our method offers an efficient, nearly bias-free path for large-scale simulations of J1-J2-type frustrated magnets under periodic boundaries and generalizes readily to other frustrated spin lattices.

</details>


### [132] [Generation of $N$-particle kappa-distributed velocities via superstatistical Gibbs sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25487)
*Leonardo Herrera_Fuenzalida,Sergio Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超统计结构与 Gibbs 采样的方法，用于在粒子模拟（PIC）中正确采样服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布的速度，以再现粒子动能之间的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kappa 分布广泛用于模拟空间和实验室等离子体中的重尾非平衡速度分布，但在 PIC 模拟中仅采样单粒子边缘分布是不够的，因为 N 粒子 kappa 分布会引入粒子动能之间的非平凡相关性，需要正确还原。

Method: 基于 kappa 分布的超统计结构，将速度与参数 β 的联合分布作为目标分布，实现一个 Gibbs 采样器，使其平稳分布为速度与 β 的联合分布。

Result: 采样得到的速度服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布，并通过构造方式自动再现了粒子动能之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 该 Gibbs 采样方法能够在 PIC 模拟中正确生成服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布的速度样本，并保留粒子间的动能相关性，为准确建模重尾非平衡等离子体提供了有效手段。

Abstract: The \emph{kappa} distribution is widely used to model heavy-tailed, non-equilibrium velocity distributions in space and laboratory plasmas. Its correct use in particle-in-cell (PIC) simulations requires more than sampling the single-particle marginal: the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution predicts non-trivial correlations between the kinetic energies of particles. Building on the superstatistical structure, we implement a Gibbs sampler whose stationary distribution is the joint distribution of the velocities and $β$, so that the sampled velocities follow the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution and reproduce these correlations by construction.

</details>


### [133] [Phase transitions of the antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25884)
*Kai-Wei Huang,Chien-Ting Chen,Shang-Wei Li,Fu-Jiun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用蒙特卡罗模拟和神经网络，重新研究三维立方格子上反铁磁 J1-J2-J3 Ising 模型（J1=J2=J3=1.0）的相变性质，明确判定其为二级相变，解决了此前研究中的争议；同时提出了一种高效且存储需求极低的神经网络训练与测试策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于三维反铁磁 J1-J2-J3 Ising 模型（J1=J2=J3=1.0）相变性质（一级或二级）的研究结论相互矛盾，需要对该模型的相变本质给出明确判定，并寻求更高效、更节省资源的神经网络方法用于高维系统临界行为研究。

Method: 采用蒙特卡罗模拟与神经网络相结合的方法，对更大晶格尺寸（远超此前研究）的数据进行分析；除 J1=J2=J3=1.0 外，还研究了 J2/J1=4/7、J3=J1=1.0 的情形。同时设计了一种专用的神经网络训练与测试策略，大幅提升计算效率并降低存储需求（仅为传统方法千分之一量级）。

Result: 在大晶格数据支持下，明确判定 J1=J2=J3=1.0 时的相变为二级相变，没有歧义，从而解决了既有争议；所提出的神经网络策略在保持高效的同时，存储需求仅为常规方法的每千分之一（per-mille）量级，并成功应用于临界现象研究。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了三维反铁磁 J1-J2-J3 Ising 模型在 J1=J2=J3=1.0 处的相变二级性质，终结了长期争论；同时展示了一种极其节约资源的神经网络应用路径，为高维系统临界行为的神经网络研究提供了新方法。

Abstract: Motivated by the conflicting findings in prior studies regarding the nature of the phase transition for the three-dimensional antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice with $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, in this study we conduct a detailed investigation of this model using both Monte Carlo simulations and neural networks (NN). Here $J_1$ and $J_2$ are the intralayer nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings, respectively, and $J_3$ is the nearest-neighbor interlayer interaction. By investigating the data obtained from much larger lattices than those used in previous studies, we find that the phase transition for $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$ is second order without any ambiguity. Hence we bring a resolution to this debate. Apart from $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, we study the critical phenomenon associated with $J_2/J_1 = 4/7$ and $J_3 = J_1 = 1.0$ as well. In addition, by employing a dedicated training and testing strategy, our NN computation not only is much more efficient than those utilizing the standard procedures commonly used in the literature, but also requires only a per-mille fraction of the storage capacity needed by a conventional NN calculation when a large linear system size $L$ is considered. The method employed here provides an extremely resource-efficient route for applying NN techniques to study the critical behaviors of higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [134] [Gini coefficient of scientists and universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26053)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文借鉴经济学中衡量财富不平等的洛伦兹曲线与帕累托曲线方法，分析科学家和大学的论文引用分布，并以基尼系数量化引用不平等程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引用分布高度不均的现象长期存在，但缺乏一种直观且可与财富不平等类比的量化分析工具。作者希望将经济学中成熟的洛伦兹/帕累托曲线方法引入科学计量学，以更清晰地刻画顶尖科学家和大学的引用集中程度。

Method: 利用 Google Scholar 公开的科学家与大学论文引用数据库，对所得引用分布应用洛伦兹曲线构造算法，并由洛伦兹曲线计算基尼系数 G；进一步将引用分布与 Rayleigh-Jeans 热化与凝聚（玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚类比）概念进行拟合与解释。

Result: 对 15 位诺贝尔奖得主（含爱因斯坦、费曼）的论文分析得平均 G=0.81：50% 的论文仅获得总引用的 2.7%，而前 10%（1%）的论文获得 71%（30%）的引用。对顶尖大学（如普林斯顿、剑桥）分析显示，约 65% 的总引用由仅 10% 的科学家贡献，而 50% 的科学家仅贡献 3% 的引用，引用分布与全球财富不平等呈现出相似特征。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹曲线与基尼系数能有效揭示科学家和大学层面引用的高度集中与不平等，且引用分布可用 Rayleigh-Jeans 热化与凝聚概念很好描述，显示出与全球财富不平等的深层相似性。

Abstract: Using the public database of Google Scholar article citations of scientists and universities we apply the algorithm for constructing Lorenz curves to the resulting citation distributions. Using Lorenz and Pareto curves, broadly used for economic analyses of wealth inequality across countries, we show that this approach gives a useful insight of citation distributions of world-leading scientists characterized by the Gini coefficient $G$ obtained from the Lorenz curves. For articles of 15 Nobel laureates (including A. Einstein and R. Feynman) we find the average $G=0.81$ with 50% of articles capturing only 2.7% of all citations and the top 10% (1%) of articles capturing 71% (30%) of all citations. Furthmore, we determine for world-leading universities the Gini coefficient via the citations of affiliated scientists. We show that about 65 percent of all citations belonging to a university (e.g. Princeton, Cambridge) are generated only by 10 percent of their university scientists while 50% of scientists produce only 3% of all university citations. The citation distributions are well described by the concept of Rayleigh-Jeans thermalization and condensation with similarities traced to the world wealth inequality.

</details>


### [135] [Generalised Fluctuating Dynamic Density-Functional Theory: Derivation and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26129)
*Toby Kay,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从微观哈密顿动力学出发，利用粗粒化技术与 Zwanzig 投影算符方法，推导出一个适用于任意可观测场的广义涨落动态密度泛函理论（FDDFT），并通过对胶体和简单黏性流体的应用验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密度泛函理论通过变分原理连接流体的微观与宏观描述，其动态推广 DDFT 在远离平衡的介观系统中因涨落显著而不足，因此需要从第一性原理出发推导含涨落的随机扩展形式（FDDFT），以提供更普遍、更严格的理论框架。

Method: 作者从微观哈密顿动力学出发，采用粗粒化技术（包括 Zwanzig 投影算符方法）进行第一性原理推导，得到适用于任意可观测场的广义 FDDFT。其中动力学由以熵泛函表达的热力学力驱动，包含可逆、耗散和随机三项，并分别通过 Liouville 算符、Green-Kubo 关系与涨落-耗散定理与微观动力学建立联系。进一步通过平均化粗粒化，得到宏观尺度上有效的广义 DDFT。

Result: 该广义 FDDFT 被应用于胶体与简单黏性流体两类原型系统：对胶体，重新推导出近期发现的胶体 FDDFT；对简单黏性流体，严格恢复了 Landau-Lifshitz 涨落 Navier-Stokes 方程的非等温推广形式，从而为该唯象模型提供了基本原理层面的推导。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个从第一性原理导出的广义涨落动态密度泛函理论，统一了可逆、耗散与随机动力学贡献，并阐明了 DDFT 与 FDDFT 在描述层次上的差异。通过对胶体和简单黏性流体的应用，验证了该框架的普适性与有效性，并为 Landau-Lifshitz 涨落 Navier-Stokes 方程提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Classical density-functional theory is a powerful theoretical framework for studying the equilibrium density distribution of complex many-body systems. The core of the framework is to bridge microscopic and macroscopic descriptions of fluids through a variational principle that minimises the system's free energy. For dynamical settings, dynamic DFT (DDFT) extends the equilibrium framework to capture the time evolution of an order parameter, typically density. However, DDFT is often insufficient for mesoscopic systems far from equilibrium where fluctuations become significant, necessitating a stochastic extension: fluctuating DDFT. Here we derive a generalised FDDFT from first principles starting from the microscopic Hamiltonian dynamics using coarse-graining techniques, including Zwanzig's projection operator method. The resulting FDDFT applies to arbitrary observable fields, where the dynamics are driven by a thermodynamic force expressed in terms of an entropy functional. The dynamics consist of reversible, dissipative, and stochastic contributions, each directly linked to the underlying microscopic dynamics via the Liouville operator, Green-Kubo relations, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. To highlight the difference in the level of description between DDFT and FDDFT, we demonstrate how further coarse-graining via averaging leads to a generalised DDFT, valid at the macroscale. To showcase the effectiveness and utility of the generalised FDDFT, we apply it to two prototypical fluid systems: colloidal and simple viscous fluids. In the former case, we utilise the new formalism to re-derive the recently found colloidal FDDFT. In the latter case, we rigorously recover the non-isothermal extension of the Landau-Lifshitz fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations, providing fundamental derivation of this well-known phenomenological model, enabling to clarify its underlying principles.

</details>


### [136] [Motility-Induced Phase Separation of Run-and-Tumble Particles under Local Stochastic Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26149)
*Pascal Grange*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维活性气体（run-and-tumble 粒子）在原点处施加局域随机重置时的非平衡稳态。结果表明当重置率超过临界值时，系统形成以原点为中心的致密液滴与周围气体共存的两相稳态，液滴大小对重置率非单调，且与重置速度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局域随机重置对活性物质相分离（MIPS）的影响，区别于全局重置——局域重置在原点维持质量注入通量，同时保留大尺度空间自组织，从而探讨非平衡通量驱动的相变与成核过程。

Method: 采用 run-and-tumble 粒子的一维活性气体模型，在原点引入局域随机重置；利用动力学演化方程求解稳态密度分布，并用 Lambert 函数给出精确解析形式；进一步分析过饱和气体相的动力学稳定性与液滴弛豫过程，并将框架推广到有限重置速度情形。

Result: 存在临界重置率 r_c，超过该值后系统转变为以原点为中心的致密液滴与外围气体共存的两相稳态；稳态密度剖面可用 Lambert 函数精确表达；液滴尺寸对重置率 r 呈非单调依赖且达到最大值；成核液滴以 1/r 的弛豫时间指数衰减至稳态尺寸；稳态液滴尺寸对重置速度不变。

Conclusion: 局域重置驱动活性气体在临界重置率之上发生向两相稳态的转变，形成稳定的原点液滴，其尺寸对重置率非单调、对重置速度不变，且液滴以时间尺度 1/r 指数弛豫至稳态，揭示了局域重置与活性相分离耦合的非平衡稳态行为。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium stationary states of a one-dimensional active gas composed of run-and-tumble particles (RTPs) undergoing motility-induced phase separation (MIPS) and subjected to local stochastic resetting at the origin. Unlike global resetting, local resetting maintains a mass-injection flux at the origin while preserving large-scale spatial self-organization. We demonstrate that above a critical resetting rate $r_c$, the active system undergoes a transition toward a stationary two-phase state featuring a dense liquid droplet centered at the origin coexisting with a surrounding gas phase. Using kinetic evolution equations, we derive the exact steady-state density profile expressed in terms of the Lambert function. The size of the droplet has a non-monotonic dependence on the resetting rate $r$, and attains its maximum. Furthermore, we analyze the dynamical stability of the supersaturated gas phase and show that a nucleated droplet relaxes exponentially toward its steady-state size with a relaxation timescale of $1/r$. We extend our framework to model a finite resetting velocity, proving that the stationary droplet size is invariant with respect to the resetting velocity.

</details>


### [137] [Poisson-shot-noise induced escape dynamics of a passive particle and a dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26316)
*Gemeit Goel,Rahul Marathe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热浴与活性浴（泊松分布"踢"噪声）共同作用下、处于双稳态势阱中的单个布朗粒子与二聚体（由弹簧连接的两粒子）的平均首次通过时间（MFPT）。在小"踢"方差近似下，解析给出弱噪声（Arrhenius 标度）与强噪声（幂律标度）两种标度机制；一般情形用数值求解。二聚体在小键强区采用顺序跳跃机制、大键强区采用绝热消去法处理。此外研究了引入中间势垒对逃逸的加速与优化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFPT 及其倒数（逃逸率）是刻画随机动力学系统的重要量。被动粒子在活性噪声作用下的 MFPT 动力学是一个有趣的研究方向，且受近期关于"加速逃逸机制"研究的启发，希望探究中间势垒对逃逸的增强与优化作用。

Method: 分析两类系统（单布朗粒子、弹簧连接的二聚体），均置于热浴与活性浴中并束缚于双稳态势阱；活性浴产生服从泊松分布的"踢"。在小"踢"方差近似下解析推导 MFPT，区分弱噪声（Arrhenius 标度）与强噪声（对有效噪声强度的幂律标度）两种标度机制；一般情况数值求解。二聚体在小键强区用顺序跳跃机制、大键强区用绝热消去法推导 MFPT，并考察引入中间势垒的情形。

Result: 解析给出小"踢"方差近似下单粒子与二聚体的 MFPT：弱噪声区逃逸率呈经典 Arrhenius 标度，强噪声区 MFPT 按有效噪声强度的幂律标度；一般情况由数值求解。二聚体在小键强区通过顺序跳跃、大键强区通过绝热消去分别得到 MFPT 结果。数值结果表明，通过选择合适的中间势垒轮廓，可以增强并进一步优化 MFPT（即加速逃逸）。

Conclusion: 在活性噪声（泊松"踢"）与热噪声并存的双稳态系统中，MFPT 在小"踢"方差近似下呈现弱噪声 Arrhenius 标度与强噪声幂律标度两种机制；二聚体的 MFPT 可用顺序跳跃（弱键）与绝热消去（强键）方法解析描述；引入合适的中间势垒可增强并优化逃逸速率。

Abstract: Mean First Passage Time (MFPT) is a crucial quantity that characterizes stochastic processes in various stochastic dynamical systems across different disciplines. The inverse of the MFPT, known as the escape rate, is helpful in the context of Brownian motion and chemical processes. The MFPT dynamics of a passive particle in the presence of active noises presents a fascinating avenue. In this work, we study the dynamics of two different systems: first, a single Brownian particle and second, a dimer (two Brownian particles connected by a spring). In both cases, the systems are kept in contact with a thermal bath and an active bath, and are trapped in a bistable potential. The active bath produces kicks that follow the Poisson distribution. The MFPT of the particle and the dimer is investigated in various scenarios. Analytical results are presented for the small kick variance approximation only. In this approximation, we formulate the MFPT in two scaling regimes: a weak-noise regime characterized by classical Arrhenius scaling for escape rate and a strong-noise regime in which the MFPT scales as a power law in the effective noise strength. For the general case, the problem is solved numerically. Next, under the same approximation, the MFPT of a dimer in the small-bond-strength regime is studied using a sequential-hopping mechanism, and in the large-bond-strength regime, the MFPT is formulated using the adiabatic elimination approach. Motivated by recent studies on speeding up the escape mechanism, the MFPT of the particle and the dimer in the presence of an intermediate barrier is also examined. We deduced from the numerical results that the MFPT can be enhanced and further optimized by selecting a suitable intermediate potential profile.

</details>


### [138] [An infinite sequence of higher order Ehrenfest transitions in a one-dimensional crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26371)
*Sakineh Mizani,Szabolcs Varga,Martin Oettel,Péter Gurin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可精确求解的一维模型，能展示任意阶 n≥2 的埃伦费斯特相变序列，这些相变无对称性破缺且不具常规临界行为，揭示了一类非临界相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶埃伦费斯特相变极其罕见且理解不足，需要展现在何种条件下可产生任意阶相变，并厘清埃伦费斯特分类的适用范围。

Method: 构造一个可精确求解的一维模型（likely 涉及可积系统或自由能的人工构造），通过解析计算自由能对温度的导数，展示任意 n≥2 阶埃伦费斯特相变序列，并分析关联长度及其密度导数。

Result: 该模型展示任意 n≥2 阶的埃伦费斯特相变序列；所有相变均无对称性破缺；二阶相变缺乏常规临界行为，但关联长度对密度的导数发散。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了一类不同的非临界相变，并有助于厘清埃伦费斯特分类法的适用范围。

Abstract: Higher-order Ehrenfest phase transitions are exceptionally rare and poorly understood. We present an exactly solvable one-dimensional model exhibiting a sequence of Ehrenfest transitions of arbitrary $n\geq2$ order. These transitions occur without symmetry breaking and even the second-order transition lacks conventional critical behavior, although the density derivative of the correlation length diverges. Our results reveal a distinct class of noncritical phase transitions and help clarify the scope of the Ehrenfest classification.

</details>


### [139] [Coexistence phase of the two-dimensional Vicsek model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26561)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究Vicsek模型共存相中带型图案的多样性，指出大多数图案在无限尺寸极限下消失，并发现共存相至少部分亚稳，渐近上应是由多种混沌相互作用行波带组成的无序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型是活性物质统计物理研究的核心模型，近期关于著名Toner-Tu群聚相部分亚稳性的发现带来重大意外。作者旨在进一步揭示其共存相中带型图案的多样性及其渐近行为，以修正此前对该相的理解。

Method: 通过对Vicsek模型共存相进行系统数值模拟，观察并分类出现的有序带型图案（包括此前未观察到的新图案），检验这些图案在系统尺寸趋于无穷时的稳定性；同时施加局域扰动，考察规则、全局有序的带构型能否被破坏。

Result: 发现共存相中存在多种带型图案，部分为首次观察到；但大多数图案在无限尺寸极限下消失。局域扰动可摧毁这些规则、全局有序的带构型，表明共存相至少部分亚稳。

Conclusion: 共存相在无穷大系统极限下应呈现为一种无序相，由多种混沌相互作用的行波带构成，这与先前预期的图像不同。

Abstract: The Vicsek model continues to be central in statistical physics studies of active matter, with major recent surprises such as the partial metastability of the famous Toner-Tu flocking phase. Here we report on the diversity of band patterns observed in its coexistence phase --some of them never observed before--, but we also argue that most disappear in the infinite-size limit, leaving nevertheless a different picture than anticipated previously. We finally show that these rather regular, globally ordered band configurations can be destroyed by a localized perturbation: the coexistence phase is also at least partially metastable, and thus must be, asymptotically, a disordered phase made of a variety of chaotically interacting traveling bands.

</details>


### [140] [Phase Diagram of the Two-Dimensional Vicsek Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26569)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模数值模拟，系统更新了二维Vicsek模型的相图，发现多种相分离机制并存，并将共存相细分为两类，同时标定了均匀Toner-Tu液相的亚稳区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对Vicsek模型的认识存在空白，尤其是2015年文献提出的简单微相分离图像可能不完整，需要更大时空尺度上的系统数值模拟来修正和完善其相图。

Method: 在二维空间中对Vicsek模型开展前所未有的长度尺度和时间尺度的全面数值模拟，通过相图重构分析相分离行为。

Result: 发现相图中存在多种相分离机制，而非仅此前提出的简单微相分离；共存相被进一步细分为两个子区域；并绘制出均匀Toner-Tu液相亚稳定性区域的范围。

Conclusion: Vicsek模型的二维相图比以往认知更为复杂，需要以多重相分离的图像加以理解，且共存相可再分为两类，同时明确了均匀液体亚稳区的位置。

Abstract: Motivated by gaps in our current knowledge of the Vicsek model, we present a major update of its phase diagram in two space dimensions, summarizing comprehensive numerical simulations at length- and timescales never explored before. Our main conclusions are that multiple phase separation scenarios are at play, not just the simple microphase separation put forward in [Phys. Rev. Lett. 114, 068101 (2015)], and that the coexistence phase is subdivided in two. We also map out the region of metastability of the homogeneous Toner-Tu liquid phase.

</details>


### [141] [Avalanches and Aging Driven by Parametric Amplification in Kerr Oscillator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26573)
*Alejandro S. Gómez,Javier del Pino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: KPO 网络在非线性耦合下会于瞬态动力学中出现类似玻璃系统弛豫雪崩的激活级联：参量增益逐个激活模式，休眠模式在等待期间持续衰减，导致激活间隔几何增长、网络呈老化（logarithmic time）行为；加性噪声为种子设下限，可缩短最长驻留并"解黏"网络，但激活序列在强数个数量级的噪声下仍能存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动-耗散系统（如 KPO 网络）的稳态已被广泛用于 Ising 机、玻色纠错码和传感等应用，但瞬态如何从给定初态演化到这些稳态仍未被充分探索；作者旨在揭示 KPO 网络瞬态动力学中未被研究的涌现行为。

Method: 研究具有非线性耦合的 KPO 网络瞬态动力学，分析参量增益、非线性频率移动如何依次激活休眠模式，研究激活间隔的几何增长与网络老化规律，并考察加性噪声对激活序列及驻留时间的影响。

Result: 增益先激活失谐可被克服的模式，其非线性频率移动将休眠模式逐个拉入增益窗口；休眠模式等待期间持续衰减，使每次激活从更小的种子出发，激活间隔几何增长，激活数仅在时间上对数累积（网络老化）。在 20 模式网络中，每个驻留约为前一个的两倍长。加性噪声为种子设下限并"解黏"网络，首先缩短最长驻留，而激活序列在强数个数量级的噪声下依然保持。

Conclusion: KPO 网络的瞬态动力学呈现老化与雪崩式激活级联，老化延迟趋近稳态，但噪声通过抬升种子下限解黏网络并加速到达，噪声鲁棒的激活序列表明该瞬态结构可用于理解或设计 KPO 网络的动力学行为。

Abstract: Gain, loss, and nonlinearity shape the phase-space landscape of driven-dissipative systems, such as Kerr parametric oscillator (KPO) networks realized in superconducting, nanomechanical, and photonic arrays. Networks of KPOs exploit the stationary states of this landscape for Ising machines, bosonic error-correcting codes, and sensing. Yet how transients reach these states from a given initial condition remains largely unexplored. Here we show that KPO networks with nonlinear couplings can develop avalanches in their transient dynamics: rapid cascades of amplification between modes reminiscent of the relaxation avalanches of glassy systems. The mechanism is that parametric gain first activates the modes whose detuning it can overcome, and their nonlinear frequency shifts then pull dormant modes into the gain window one at a time. Dormant modes keep decaying while they wait for their turn, so every activation starts from a smaller seed and the intervals between activations grow geometrically. The network thus ages: the longer it has evolved, the slower it changes, and activations accumulate only logarithmically in time. In a 20-mode network, each dwell lasts about twice as long as the previous one. While aging delays the approach to the stationary state, additive noise sets a floor on the seeds and unsticks the network, shortening the longest dwells first while the activation sequence survives to noise several orders of magnitude stronger.

</details>


### [142] [From particles to continuum theories in effective models of self-propelled rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26593)
*Elia Bronzo,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Alexandre Solon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为自推进杆状粒子的集体运动建立了简化的Vicsek型模型（以速度向列对齐和两种排斥作用体现杆的几何效应），并由此推导出流体动力学方程，其解在性质上忠实于粒子层面行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自推进杆碰撞模型是滑动细菌、分子马达驱动的生物丝等真实系统的抽象，主要在二维空间研究并展现出丰富的集体运动相图，但缺少忠实于该现象的连续介质理论，因为对显式杆模型进行粗粒化极为困难。作者希望构建足够简单、可作为系统粗粒化起点的模型。

Method: 提出Vicsek型模型，通过引入速度的向列对齐以及两类排斥作用来有效体现杆状几何，而非显式建模杆；在此基础上进行系统粗粒化，推导出连续介质流体动力学方程。

Result: 推导出的流体动力学方程在定性上能够复现其粒子层面模型所表现出的集体行为，说明该粗粒化方案可行且忠实于微观现象。

Conclusion: 通过将杆几何以有效方式（向列速度对齐+两类排斥）纳入Vicsek框架，可以得到既简单又可系统粗粒化的模型，为自推进杆体系的连续介质理论提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Models of colliding self-propelled rods are abstractions of many real systems such as gliding bacteria and biofilaments displaced by molecular motors. They have been studied mostly in two space dimensions and shown to display a wealth of different collective regimes with many parameters involved in the ensuing phase diagrams. Continuum-level theories faithful to this phenomenology are largely lacking since coarse-graining models of explicit rods is notoriously difficult. Here we study collections of particles in which the rods geometry is taken into account effectively, by implementing the nematic alignment of their velocities and two types of repulsion. These Vicsek-style models are simple enough to be starting points for systematic coarse-graining. We thus derive hydrodynamic equations that we show to have solutions qualitatively faithful to their particle-level origin.

</details>


### [143] [Statistical decision theory in thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26617)
*Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将统计决策理论引入随机热力学系统，利用 Le Cam 两点法推导 minimax 风险的下界，并在马尔可夫过程的分辨与分布估计问题中，证明这些下界由动力学活性（乃至熵产生）给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机热力学中已有多种不确定性关系，但缺乏一个统一的统计框架来涵盖估计、假设检验与分类等任务；作者希望通过引入统计决策理论，将感知、分类与不确定性关系统一起来。

Method: 采用统计决策理论中的 minimax 风险（即最优估计器在最坏参数下的最小可实现期望损失）作为度量，并运用 Le Cam 两点法推导 minimax 风险的下界，进而将所得关系应用于马尔可夫过程中的分类与分布估计问题。

Result: 推导出的下界适用于更广泛的热力学场景；在分类与分布估计问题中，minimax 风险的下界由动力学活性给出；对特定动力学进行考察后，所得下界可简化为涉及动力学活性或熵产生的不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 统计决策理论可用于统一随机热力学中的多种统计任务，minimax 风险与动力学活性、熵产生等热力学量之间存在定量联系，为热力学不确定性关系提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We introduce statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamic systems. Statistical decision theory integrates several statistical tasks, such as estimation, hypothesis testing, and classification. In this study, we consider statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamics, which unifies sensing, classification, and uncertainty relations. In particular, we focus on the minimax risk, the smallest achievable expected loss under the worst-case parameter choice, even with an optimal estimator. We derive lower bounds on the minimax risk using Le Cam's two-point method. The derived relationships can be used across a broader range of thermodynamic scenarios. To illustrate these relations, we study classification and distribution estimation problems in a Markov process. We demonstrate that the minimax risk provides lower bounds given by the dynamical activity in these problems. Furthermore, by examining particular dynamics, we establish that the resulting bounds simplify to uncertainty relations involving dynamical activity or entropy production.

</details>


### [144] [Nucleation-type criticality with infinite number of free-energy minima beyond mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26638)
*Jun Takahashi,Yoshihiko Nishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和Potts型各向异性的三维海森堡模型相图，发现winding相与铁磁序相之间的转变表现出反常临界性：物理量连续但临界指数却与一阶相变一致，并在临界点出现大量自由能局域极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在结合de Gennes的成核型相变理论与Dzyaloshinskii对手征磁体的计算，探究这种"连续却带有一阶特征"的反常临界相变能否在超越平均场的微观模型中实现，从而统一两类理论图景。

Method: 采用三维海森堡模型，并引入Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用与Potts型各向异性，通过数值方法（如蒙特卡洛模拟）计算相图，分析winding相到铁磁相的相变临界行为、临界指数以及临界点处自由能景观中局域极小值的数目。

Result: 发现winding相与铁磁有序相之间的相变为临界相变，具有反常特征：物理量保持连续，但临界指数与一阶相变一致；临界点处自由能局域极小值数量不断增长，支持"无穷多个不连续相变汇聚成临界性"的图景。

Conclusion: 数值结果在超越平均场的微观模型中实现了这种反常临界性，从而统一了de Gennes的成核型相变理论和Dzyaloshinskii的手征磁体计算，证明该反常临界性并非平均场特有的假象。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram of a three-dimensional Heisenberg model with Dzyaloshinski--Moriya interaction under the presence of Potts-like anisotropy. The phase transition between the winding phase and the ferromagnetically ordered phase exhibits a critical phase transition with anomalous features such as critical exponents consistent with that of a first-order transition while maintaining continuity of physical quantities. Furthermore, at the critical point, we see a growing number of free energy local minima, supporting the picture of infinitely many discontinuous transitions forming criticality. Our numerical result unify de Gennes' theory of nucleation-type transitions and Dzyaloshinskii's calculation of chiral magnets by demonstrating that such anomalous criticality can be realized in microscopic models beyond mean-field.

</details>


### [145] [Phase-sensitive quantum-state texture during thermalization of a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Heisenberg dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26736)
*Onofre Rojas,Zhirayr Adamyan,Fadwa Benabdallah,Vadim Ohanyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性自旋-1/2海森堡二聚体在纵向磁场与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya(DM)相互作用下，经玻色浴热弛豫过程中的量子态纹理。利用弱耦合Lindblad方程，发现DM耦合通过改变能级劈裂和旋转手性相位来控制单激发相干的模与相位；弛豫动力学将布居热化与能量基相干衰减/进动分离，二者共同产生依赖于DM的纹理过冲及冷却时的阻尼振荡和相干正交分量符号变化，最终提出一种相位敏感的弛豫诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究各向异性自旋-1/2海森堡二聚体在纵向磁场、DM相互作用及玻色浴耦合下的热弛豫过程，明确DM耦合如何影响量子态纹理（rugosity、保真度纹理、迹距离纹理）与相干、矢量手征性、纯度之间的关系，并揭示弛豫过程中相位敏感的动态特征。

Method: 从局部横向与纵向玻色浴耦合出发，构建全局弱耦合Lindblad主方程，识别其跃迁通道，讨论久期近似的适用性与局限；在局域自旋基中显式求得Gibbs态；对加热与冷却方案，数值比较rugosity、保真度纹理、迹距离纹理与相干、矢量自旋手征性、纯度等量；将弛豫动力学分解为布居热化与能量基相干衰减/进动，并考察降低耗散时纹理极大值在久期模型内的保持情况。

Result: DM耦合通过修正能级劈裂并旋转手性相位，同时控制单激发相干的模与相位；弛豫动力学将布居热化与特意制备的能量基相干的衰减和进动解耦，二者共同贡献产生依赖于DM的纹理过冲，冷却过程中出现阻尼振荡与相干正交分量的符号反转；这些瞬态行为不同于相干幅值与纯度的演化，且迹距离纹理对二者的加权方式异于基于重叠的量化指标；在降低耗散下纹理极大值仍保存在久期模型内，形成一种相位敏感的弛豫诊断。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了量子态纹理可作为探测自旋二聚体在DM相互作用下热弛豫的相位敏感诊断工具，揭示了纹理过冲、相干振荡与符号变化等瞬态特征，并厘清了不同纹理度量（rugosity、保真度纹理、迹距离纹理）在加权相干贡献上的差异，为开放量子系统弛豫动力学与手性相互作用的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-state texture during the thermal relaxation of an anisotropic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg dimer subject to a longitudinal magnetic field and a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction. Starting from local transverse and longitudinal couplings to a bosonic bath, we construct a global weak-coupling Lindblad equation, identify its transition channels, and discuss the applicability and limitations of the secular approximation. The Gibbs state is obtained explicitly in the local spin basis, revealing that the DM coupling controls both the modulus and phase of the single-excitation coherence by modifying the energy splitting and rotating the chiral phase. For heating and cooling protocols, we compare rugosity, fidelity-based texture, and trace-distance texture with coherence, vector spin chirality, and purity. The relaxation dynamics separates population thermalization from the decay and precession of deliberately prepared energy-basis coherence. Their combined contribution to local coherence produces DM-dependent texture overshoots and, during cooling, damped oscillations and sign changes of the coherence quadratures. These transients differ from the evolution of coherence magnitude and purity, while trace-distance texture weights them differently from the overlap-based quantifiers. Calculations at reduced dissipation preserve the texture maxima within the secular model. The results identify a phase-sensitive diagnostic of relaxation.

</details>


### [146] [A McLennan formula for equiaccessible steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26755)
*Jacob Calvert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从接近平衡态的 McLennan 公式出发，将其推广为围绕"等可达稳态"展开的远平衡类比公式：零阶项由局域逃逸率唯一决定稳态占据（"低抖动"原理的强形式），一阶修正则度量流量不平衡对各态可达性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McLennan 公式仅在近平衡时有效，用瞬态不可逆熵流修正 Boltzmann 分布；作者希望得到能适用于任意远离平衡情形的类比结果，从而刻画非平衡稳态的占据规律。

Method: 将平衡展开改为围绕一类"等可达稳态"（equiaccessible steady states）展开，推导出 McLennan 公式的远平衡类比，并按阶分析稳态占据的贡献来源。

Result: 零阶近似下，稳态占据仅由局域逃逸率决定，构成活性物质中"低抖动"（low rattling）原理的强形式；一阶修正则量化流量不平衡对各状态可达性的影响，标志着相对占据的非局域决定因素开始出现。

Conclusion: 通过围绕等可达稳态展开，可将 McLennan 公式推广到远离平衡的情形，揭示局域逃逸率主导占据的零阶图像，并以一阶项刻画非局域流量不平衡的修正作用。

Abstract: Near equilibrium, the Boltzmann distribution is corrected by the transient irreversible entropy flux, a result known as the McLennan formula. By expanding around equiaccessible steady states instead, we derive an analogue that can hold arbitrarily far from equilibrium. At zeroth order, steady-state occupation is determined solely by local escape rates, yielding a strong form of the active-matter principle of "low rattling." The first-order correction measures the impact of traffic imbalances on the accessibility of each state, marking the onset of nonlocal determinants of relative occupation.

</details>

### [147] [Quantifying the Kinetic and Thermodynamic Origins of the Glass-Forming Ability in Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24101)
*Weiwei Jin,Jinpeng Fan,Mark D. Shattuck,Corey S. O'Hern*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过将结晶速率与冷却速率联系起来，研究了模型纯金属及BCC/B2与FCC合金的玻璃形成能力，发现结晶速率与临界冷却速率在十一个数量级范围内强正相关，并揭示了热力学贡献对结晶速率起主导作用，而界面自由能与体相驱动力分别抑制不同体系的形核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃形成能力通常由临界冷却速率R_c表征，但直接测定R_c困难且计算代价高；作者旨在建立R_c与可独立计算的结晶速率K_X之间的定量关系，并将K_X分解为动力学与热力学贡献，从而从物理上阐明决定玻璃形成能力的关键因素。

Method: 采用经典形核理论，在T=0.8T_m处计算形核率I与生长率U，并定义K_X=(IU^3)^(1/4)，进而与R_c进行关联。将K_X分解为涉及原子团簇附着与扩散的动力学贡献和热力学贡献，以约化有效液-晶界面自由能(γ̃)与约化体相驱动力(Δμ̃)表征形核势垒ΔG_c~γ̃^3/Δμ̃^2，并通过结构与化学序分析（二十面体短程有序、类晶体局域环境、化学序失配）解释玻璃形成能力差异。

Result: 在十一个数量级范围内，K_X与R_c呈强正相关。在所考察的冷却速率范围内，热力学贡献对K_X的主导程度超过动力学贡献。形核势垒由γ̃与Δμ̃共同决定，但二者的相对重要性因材料而异：某些体系由大界面自由能抑制形核，另一些则由弱的体相驱动力抑制结晶。结构分析表明，更易形成玻璃的体系具有更强的结构挫折、更多二十面体状及更少类晶体的局域环境，以及过冷液体与晶相之间更大的化学序失配。

Conclusion: 结晶速率K_X是预测玻璃形成能力的有效指标，与临界冷却速率R_c强相关；热力学因素在决定结晶速率中占主导地位，而结构与化学序特征为玻璃形成能力背后的物理机制提供了统一解释。

Abstract: The glass-forming ability (GFA) of a metallic liquid can be quantified by the critical cooling rate $R_c$, below which it crystallizes upon cooling. $R_c$ is determined by the competition between $R$ and the crystallization rate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$. Here, we calculate $R_c$ and $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ in model pure metals and alloys with BCC/B2 and FCC equilibrium crystal structures. Using classical nucleation theory and independently calculated crystal growth rates, we evaluate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}=(IU^3)^{1/4}$ at $T=0.8T_m$, where $I$ and $U$ are the nucleation and growth rates and $T_m$ is the melting temperature. We find a strong positive correlation between $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ and $R_c$ over eleven orders of magnitude. We decompose $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ into a product of a kinetic contribution, involving atomic cluster attachment and diffusion, and a thermodynamic contribution. We show that the thermodynamic contribution to $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ dominates the kinetic contribution over the range of cooling rates that we consider. The nucleation barrier $ΔG_c \sim \tildeγ^3/Δ\tildeμ^2$, where $\tildeγ$ is the reduced effective liquid--crystal interfacial free energy and $Δ\tildeμ$ is the reduced bulk driving force, determines the magnitude of the thermodynamic contribution. The relative importance of $\tildeγ$ and $Δ\tildeμ$ depends on the material: a large interfacial free energy can suppress nucleation in some systems, whereas a weak bulk driving force can inhibit crystallization in others. Analyses of the structural and chemical order show that better glass formers have enhanced structural frustration, more icosahedral-like and less crystal-like local environments, and a larger chemical-order mismatch between the supercooled liquid and crystal phases.

</details>


### [148] [Signed FK percolation in Ising spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24629)
*Alberto Gandolfi,Charles Newman,Danile Stein*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文引入带符号的 Fortuin-Kasteleyn（FK）渗流，用于刻画 +/-J 随机相互作用 Ising 模型中的非零均匀磁化密度和双副本重叠；该方法通过保留开放路径上耦合乘积的奇偶性来细化 FK 连通性，并分别在单副本和双副本情形下将磁化密度与互重叠等价于伴生对象的密度可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 +/-J 随机相互作用 Ising 模型中，需要一种能够刻画非零均匀磁化密度与双副本重叠的工具。传统的 FK 渗流仅按连通性划分，无法区分由路径耦合乘积符号（奇偶性）带来的信息，因此作者引入带符号的 FK 渗流，以在满足无限体积 ES/FK 表示至多只有一个无穷 FK 簇的 Gibbs 分布类中表征这些序参数。

Method: 作者引入带符号 FK 渗流，方法是在普通 FK 连通性的基础上，保留沿开放路径耦合乘积的奇偶性。利用无限体积 Edwards-Sokal（ES）表示及其 DLR 方程，研究成对的伴生对象（companion objects）及其上的相容自旋赋值。在单副本情形，伴生对象由正奇偶性的 FK 连接定义；在双独立副本情形，伴生对象由奇偶性相干的 FK 连接定义。对满足无穷簇假设、且沿所选 van Hove 序列伴生密度几乎必然存在的极端态，建立磁化密度/重叠与伴生对象密度可区分性之间的等价关系。

Result: 在单副本情形，对满足无穷簇假设和伴生密度几乎必然存在的极端态，非零极限期望磁化密度等价于无穷 FK 簇的伴生对象的密度可区分性。在双独立副本情形，对满足同一假设的极端态乘积，伴生对象的密度可区分性等价于非零极限期望互重叠；当两个副本取自同一极端态时，该结果特化为 Edwards-Anderson 自重叠。

Conclusion: 带符号 FK 渗流为 +/-J 随机相互作用 Ising 模型提供了一种统一的几何刻画，将非零磁化密度和双副本（互/自）重叠归结为伴生对象的密度可区分性，从而在至多一个无穷 FK 簇的 Gibbs 分布类中建立了序参数与符号渗流几何之间的精确等价。

Abstract: We introduce signed Fortuin-Kasteleyn (FK) percolation for the +/-J random-interaction Ising model to characterize nonzero uniform magnetization density and two-replica overlap within the class of Gibbs distributions whose infinite-volume Edwards-Sokal (ES)/FK representation has at most one infinite FK cluster almost surely. The construction refines ordinary FK connectivity by retaining the parity of coupling products along open paths. Using the infinite-volume ES representation and its DLR equations, we study pairs of companion objects and the compatible spin assignments on them. In one replica, companions are defined by positive-parity FK connections. For extremal states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of companion densities along the chosen van Hove sequence, nonzero limiting expected magnetization density is equivalent to density distinguishability of the companions of the infinite FK cluster. For two independent replicas, companions are defined by FK connections coherent in parity. For products of extremal one-replica states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of the companion densities along the chosen sequence, density distinguishability of the companions is equivalent to nonzero limiting expected mutual overlap. For two independent replicas drawn from the same extremal state, this specializes to the Edwards-Anderson self-overlap.

</details>


### [149] [Synchronous and asynchronous Monte Carlo dynamics of mean-field spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: what they share and where they differ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24942)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种用于旋转不变耦合Ising自旋玻璃的MCMC动力学：并行更新全部自旋的同步蒙特卡洛方法（SyncMC）与逐次更新单自旋的异步Metropolis-Hastings动力学。通过Guionnet-Maïda公式，作者推导出两种动力学的动力学平均场理论，发现二者共享迟滞自相互作用和高斯噪声，而SyncMC额外具有瞬时自耦合和仅依赖响应函数的噪声。这些附加项只作用于最快时间尺度，因此两种动力学在慢时间尺度上的方程（包括涨落耗散比和Parisi方程）相同。数值模拟证实两种动力学趋近相同的涨落耗散关系，仅在所需更新次数上不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并统一同步（并行更新）与异步（单自旋更新）MCMC动力学在自旋玻璃中的行为，特别是它们在慢时间尺度上的等价性，以及二者在最快时间尺度上的差异如何影响收敛速度。

Method: 利用Guionnet-Maïda公式，以耦合谱的R变换为基础，推导SyncMC（通过高斯辅助场并行更新全部自旋）和异步Metropolis-Hastings动力学（逐次更新单自旋）的动力学平均场理论，将二者化为有效单自旋过程并比较其自相互作用与噪声项，进而分析不同时间尺度的方程。

Result: 两种有效单自旋过程共享迟滞自相互作用和高斯噪声；SyncMC额外具有瞬时自耦合和仅依赖响应函数的噪声，但这些附加项仅作用于最快时间尺度，因此慢时间尺度方程（包括涨落耗散比及Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型的Parisi方程）对二者相同。随机正交模型和Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型的模拟确认二者趋近相同的涨落耗散关系，差异仅在于所需的更新次数。

Conclusion: 同步MC与异步Metropolis-Hastings动力学在慢时间尺度上等价（涨落耗散关系和Parisi方程相同），SyncMC的附加项只影响最快时间尺度；数值上二者收敛到相同的涨落耗散关系，只差更新次数。这为理解并行与串行更新方案在自旋玻璃采样中的关系提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We compare two Markov chain Monte Carlo dynamics for Ising spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: the synchronous Monte Carlo method (SyncMC), which updates all spins in parallel with the help of Gaussian auxiliary fields, and asynchronous Metropolis-Hastings dynamics, which updates one spin at a time. Using the Guionnet-Maïda formula, we derive the dynamical mean-field theory of both dynamics in terms of the R-transform of the coupling spectrum. The two effective single-spin processes share a retarded self-interaction and a Gaussian noise; SyncMC has, in addition, an instantaneous self-coupling and a noise that depends only on the response function. These additional terms act only on the fastest time scale, so that the equations for slow time scales, including the fluctuation-dissipation ratio and, for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, the Parisi equation, are the same for the two dynamics. Simulations of the random orthogonal and Sherrington-Kirkpatrick models confirm that both dynamics approach the same fluctuation-dissipation relations, differing only in how many updates they need to do so.

</details>


### [150] [Random spin-3/2 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chains revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24994)
*Yen-Tung Lin,Pochung Chen,Yu-Cheng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络强无序重整化群（tSDRG）重新研究随机自旋-3/2反铁磁海森堡链中的自旋约化相变，该相变将弱无序下由有效自旋-1/2磁矩构成的随机单态相与强无序下由完整自旋-3/2磁矩构成的随机单态相区分开来，属于 Damle–Huse 分类中浮现的置换对称 P4 多临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视随机自旋-3/2反铁磁海森堡链中自旋约化相变的具体性质，并精确确定相变点位置及随机单态相的重入行为，以验证与完善 Damle–Huse 分类框架下的理论预言。

Method: 采用张量网络强无序重整化群（tSDRG）方法进行数值研究；首先在 Damle–Huse 框架内推导 P_n 普适类中无序平均体内与端到端关联函数的临界指数，再结合由能隙分布均值与方差构造的诊断量来识别 P4 相变点及随机单态相的重入行为。

Result: 成功确定了自旋-3/2链中 P4 相变点的位置，并揭示了随机单态相的重入行为；所得的临界指数与理论推导结果一致，表明相变对应于 P2 临界线上的 P4 多临界点。

Conclusion: tSDRG 结果与 Damle–Huse 分类的理论预言一致，证实自旋约化相变为 P2 临界线上浮现的置换对称 P4 多临界点，并成功刻画了随机单态相的重入现象。

Abstract: We revisit the spin-reduction transition in the random spin-$3/2$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain using the tensor-network strong-disorder renormalization group (tSDRG). The predicted transition separates two random-singlet (RS) phases: one formed by effective spin-$1/2$ moments at weaker disorder and the other by full spin-$3/2$ moments at stronger disorder. Within the Damle--Huse classification, this transition corresponds to an emergent permutation-symmetric $\mathcal{P}_4$ multicritical point on a $\mathcal{P}_2$ critical line associated with the RS phases. We first derive the critical exponents governing the disorder-averaged bulk and end-to-end correlation functions in the $\mathcal{P}_n$ universality classes within the Damle--Huse framework. Using these theoretical predictions, together with a diagnostic constructed from the mean and variance of the energy-gap distribution, we identify the $\mathcal{P}_4$ transition point and the reentrant behavior of the RS phase.

</details>


### [151] [Higher-order disorder correlations control reflected phonon counting statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25153)
*Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一维声学堆栈中超出功率谱的结构关联如何影响声子计数，发现整数厚度构造消去主要的体背散射项后，有限链反射幅度由端点新息决定，两声子Fock输入下的系综平均反射阶乘关联g^{(2)}=(μ_4+5)/8，并构造了两个具有相同均值和两点关联函数却得到相反偏离泊松值的系综，表明计数方差由四阶结构而非低阶谱决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超出功率谱的结构关联（特别是四阶统计量）如何影响一维无序声学堆栈中的声子计数统计，明确计数方差非经典的来源，并厘清系综平均脉冲统计与单个被动样本条件阶乘关联之间的区别。

Method: 采用整数厚度构造消除大范围的前导背散射项，使有限链反射幅度仅取决于端点新息；对具有单位方差与四阶矩μ_4的对称独立新息推导归一化反射功率二阶矩的极限(μ_4+5)/4；引入真空环境输入与两声子Fock输入计算系综平均反射阶乘关联；构造两个均值与完整两点关联函数相同、但g^{(2)}分别为3/4和9/8的显式系综；利用条件张量平均导出任意阻抗对比下的一般四阶阻力增长系数，并通过精确枚举验证固定N、小失谐极限，进一步测试带宽、厚度误差、传播损耗和热背景的影响。

Result: 对称独立新息（单位方差、四阶矩μ_4）下归一化反射功率二阶矩趋于(μ_4+5)/4；真空环境下两声子Fock输入给出系综平均反射阶乘关联g^{(2)}=(μ_4+5)/8；两个具有相同均值和完整两点关联函数的显式系综分别得到g^{(2)}=3/4与9/8，计数方差分居泊松值两侧；四阶阻力增长系数含有一个正的、不能通过平移最低阶谱得到的高阶散射贡献；精确枚举证实固定N小失谐极限，并显示固定失谐下增大N会产生由累积高阶体散射主导的交叉而非收敛到端点公式。

Conclusion: 结构关联超出功率谱的部分（四阶统计）实质上决定声子计数统计，仅凭均值和两点关联函数无法确定g^{(2)}及其相对泊松值的偏离方向；相关结论仅适用于无序平均脉冲统计，既不会由相干输入产生非经典性，也不改变单独分辨的被动样本的条件阶乘关联。

Abstract: We investigate how structural correlations beyond the power spectrum affect phonon counting in a one-dimensional acoustic stack. An integer-thickness construction cancels the extensive leading backscattering term and leaves a finite-chain reflection amplitude determined by endpoint innovations. For symmetric independent innovations with unit variance and fourth moment $μ_4$, the normalized reflected-power second moment tends to $(μ_4+5)/4$. With vacuum environmental inputs, a two-phonon Fock input therefore gives an ensemble-averaged reflected factorial correlation $g^{(2)}=(μ_4+5)/8$. Two explicitly constructed ensembles with identical mean and complete two-point correlation function yield $g^{(2)}=3/4$ and $9/8$, placing their counting variances on opposite sides of the Poisson value. Conditional tensor averaging also gives a general fourth-order resistance-growth coefficient for arbitrary impedance contrast, including a positive higher-scattering contribution not recovered by shifting the lowest-order spectrum. Exact enumeration confirms the fixed-$N$, small-detuning limit and shows that increasing $N$ at fixed detuning produces a crossover governed by accumulated higher-order bulk scattering rather than convergence to the endpoint formula. Further calculations test bandwidth, thickness error, propagation loss and thermal background. The result concerns disorder-averaged pulse statistics; it neither creates nonclassicality from coherent input nor changes the conditional factorial correlation of an individually resolved passive sample.

</details>


### [152] [Signatures of slow particle transport in the interacting Aubry-André chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25557)
*S. Sharifan,M. Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值研究相互作用 Aubry-André 链（准周期势）在量子淬火后的纠缠熵、粒子数熵和 Hartley 数熵，揭示强无序多体量子链中的慢离域源于能量无关的组合路径熵阻塞，而非稀有区域雪崩，且该机制在准周期与随机淬灭无序两种情形下表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无序多体量子链中的慢离域可由两种机制产生：基于能量统计的稀有共振与雪崩，或算符空间中与能量无关的组合路径熵阻塞。准周期势可消除被认为导致雪崩不稳定性的稀有区域，并改变共振统计，从而能区分这两种机制。

Method: 在相互作用 Aubry-André 链（无序强度为 D）中，对量子淬火后的演化进行数值模拟，计算纠缠熵 S(t)、粒子数熵 S_N(t) 和 Hartley 数熵 S_H(t)，分析其时间增长规律及随系统尺寸 L 的饱和行为。

Result: 发现 S(t)~μ ln t，S_N(t)~ν ln ln t，增长率满足 μ~ν~D^(-γ) 且 γ≈3，与之前淬灭无序情形下得到的行为相同；此外，饱和值 S_H(t→∞) 随系统尺寸 L 按 ln L 持续增长，且该增长在 D≳8（此前被认为 MBL 稳定的区域）依然存在。

Conclusion: 准周期势与随机淬灭无序情形的相似性表明，慢粒子输运具有不依赖稀有区域雪崩的共同起源，并且尽管共振统计被强烈改变，该慢输运机制依然持续存在。

Abstract: Slow delocalization in strongly disordered many-body quantum chains can originate from two distinct mechanisms: rare resonances and avalanches, rooted in the energy statistics, or an energy-independent combinatorial path-entropy obstruction in operator space. Quasiperiodic potentials eliminate rare regions with similar potentials that are thought to be responsible for avalanche instabilities and modify the statistics of resonances, thus allowing us to delineate the two mechanisms. To do so, we numerically study the entanglement entropy $S(t)$, number entropy $S_N(t)$, and Hartley number entropy $S_H(t)$ following a quantum quench in the interacting Aubry-André chain with disorder strength $D$. We find $S(t)\simμ\ln t$, $S_N(t)\simν\ln\ln t$ with growth rates $μ\simν\sim D^{-γ}$ and $γ\approx 3$, the same behavior that was previously found in the case of quenched disorder. Furthermore, the saturation values $S_H(t\to\infty)$ continue to grow with system size $L$ as $\ln L$, a growth that persists for $D\gtrsim 8$ where MBL was argued to be stable. The similarity of the quasi-periodic and quenched disorder cases points to a common origin of the slow particle transport that does not rely on rare-region avalanches and persists despite strongly modified resonance statistics.

</details>


### [153] [Exact cost of the Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26536)
*Sergio Caracciolo,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一维直线上的随机欧几里得最大旅行商问题（随机欧氏最大TSP），刻画了凸增、凹增、凸减三类费用函数下最大哈密顿圈的结构，并在单位区间均匀采样条件下给出了任意 N 点平均最大费用的显式表达式及幂律费用的渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典旅行商问题通常关注最小化路径，而最大旅行商问题（寻找总费用最大的哈密顿圈）在多种应用（如绕行最远的巡视路径、最坏情形分析等）中同样重要。此前对随机欧氏最大 TSP 的解结构缺乏统一刻画，尤其对非线性的边费用函数（凸/凹、增/减）缺乏系统性结果，因此需要对各类费用函数下的最优圈形态及其平均费用给出精确或渐近的刻画。

Method: 考虑 N 个城市位于一条直线上，边费用为欧氏长度的函数。针对凸增、凹增、凸减三种费用函数，分析并刻画最大化哈密顿圈的结构（即城市排序与访问顺序的确定模式）。然后假设城市点独立均匀地采样自单位区间 [0,1]，利用所得最优结构，对任意有限 N 推导平均最大总费用的显式表达式，并针对幂律边费用（cost = length 的幂次）给出当 N 增大时的渐近展开。

Result: 得到以下结果：(1) 在凸增、凹增、凸减费用函数下，最大哈密顿圈的最优结构分别被完全刻画（不同费用形状对应不同的最优访问顺序模式）；(2) 对单位区间均匀采样，给出任意 N 的平均最大费用的显式公式；(3) 对幂律边费用，给出显式的渐近展开。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了一维随机欧几里得最大旅行商问题的结构刻画与平均费用计算：最优圈形态由费用函数的凸凹性与单调性所决定，且在均匀采样模型下可以得到精确的有限 N 公式与幂律费用的渐近展开，为最大 TSP 的随机分析提供了完整框架。

Abstract: We study the random Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem: finding a Hamiltonian cycle of maximal total cost through any set of $N$ cities on a line, where each edge cost is a function of its Euclidean length. We characterize the maximizing cycles for convex increasing, concave increasing, and convex decreasing cost functions. For points sampled independently and uniformly from the unit interval, we derive explicit expressions for the average maximal cost for arbitrary $N$ and asymptotic expansions with power-law edge costs.

</details>
